### [1] [Never Look Back: Understanding Persistence in 3D Object Memory from Egocentric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10538)
*Shravan Chaudhari,William Paul,Suchi Saria,Rama Chellappa,Homanga Bharadhwaj*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Ledger：一种从第一视角视频构建的持久 3D 物体记忆，融合物体位置、历史与上下文描述，用于具身助手回答空间问题并定位物体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日常活动中，物体可能在之后才变得相关；人类能在未预期需要时记住物品位置或容器内容。本文研究具身助手如何通过观察人的第一视角日常活动，建立类似记忆。

Method: Ledger 关联录制中的多次观测，并在物体离开视野后仍保留记忆，包括人从未触碰的物体。它将每个物体的观测按静止位置聚类，仅在出现重复证据后记录移动，以降低定位噪声。同时保存简短描述，如物体内容物或支撑面。记录可后续直接用于空间问答，无需访问原始图像或视频。

Result: HD-EPIC 准确率从 29.7% 提升至 42.6%，UCS-Bench 从 33.8% 提升至 38.5%；在 Ego4D 上返回预测的物体定位中位误差为 0.99 m。分析表明时间持久性、上下文描述和检索具有互补作用。对 100 条多场景拼接流的实验暴露了检索与构建失败；逐场景构建相比单场景流可部分恢复跨场景变化造成的性能损失。

Conclusion: Ledger 作为持久 3D 物体记忆，有效提升空间问答与物体定位，并强调时间持久性、上下文描述和检索的互补价值；跨场景拼接仍是挑战，逐场景构建可部分缓解。

Abstract: As we move through the world and carry out everyday tasks, we encounter objects that may become relevant only later. We are capable of recalling where we left something or what was inside a container, even without knowing we would need it later. Here, we study how an embodied assistant can build a similar memory from egocentric videos, by observing a person's day-to-day activities. We present Ledger, a persistent 3D object memory that combines object locations, their histories, and contextual descriptions. It associates observations across the recording and retains objects after they leave the view, including those the person never touches. It clusters each object's observations by resting locations and records a move only after repeated evidence, reducing the effect of localization noise. Short descriptions preserve details such as an object's contents or supporting surface. It saves these records to later answer spatial questions without having to access the original images or video. Our memory raises HD-EPIC accuracy from 29.7% to 42.6%, UCS-Bench accuracy from 33.8% to 38.5% and localizes Ego4D objects with a 0.99 m median error on returned predictions. Our analyses identify complementary roles for temporal persistence, contextual descriptions, and retrieval. Our study on 100 stitched streams of multiple scenes each further exposes failures in both retrieval and construction. Per-scene construction partially recovers the performance lost across scene changes compared to that of single scene streams.

</details>


### [2] [ECHO: Embodied Camera Observations of Human Object Carrying](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10438)
*Xuefei Sun,Lorin Achey,Kali Hamilton,Alberto Speranzon,Gregory Grebe,Yonatan Bisk,Christoffer Heckman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出情境化物体放置（contextual object placement）基准任务，并发布ECHO大规模合成数据集，用于预测人类搬运物体时的合理目的地；实验表明单一模态不足，需联合推理场景结构、人类活动与情境知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身与辅助智能体不仅需识别物体，还需根据环境布局和居住者习惯推断物体应放置何处。现有RGB-D扫描数据集缺乏人类活动，人-物交互数据集缺乏可导航完整场景及物体自然目的地的真值，且无专用基准或数据集来定义和评估该问题。

Method: 定义情境化物体放置任务：在观测到的物体搬运片段中预测目的地。构建ECHO数据集：将室内场景密集RGB-D扫描与具身人类搬运日常物品至情境合适目的地的记录配对。数据含3,805个人工标注片段，覆盖115个HM3D场景的159个楼层、198类物体；每层有完整RGB-D扫描、房间标签和表面列表；每片段含同步RGB-D encounter clips、6-DoF相机/人/物体轨迹、起始与目的地表面、动作描述，以及不直接命名目的地但暗示居住者日常惯例的人类撰写情境句。评估采用输入掩码探针与端到端基线。

Result: 输入掩码探针和端到端基线结果表明，没有任何单一输入模态足以完成该任务。

Conclusion: 需联合推理场景结构、人类活动与情境知识；ECHO为情境化物体放置提供了首个同时包含重建场景、人类活动、自然语言和情境放置标注的公开数据集。

Abstract: Embodied and assistive agents must do more than recognize objects: they must reason about where an object belongs given the layout of an environment and the habits of the people who live in it. Progress on this problem has been limited, in part because no dedicated benchmark or dataset exists to define and evaluate it. Existing RGB-D scan datasets reconstruct static rooms without human activity, while human-object-interaction datasets capture motion without a navigable, fully reconstructed scene or a ground-truth notion of an object's natural destination. We introduce contextual object placement as a benchmark task: predicting an object's destination during an observed object-carrying episode. To support this task, we present Embodied Camera observations of Human Object carrying (ECHO), a large-scale synthetic dataset that pairs dense RGB-D scans of indoor scenes with recordings of an embodied human carrying everyday objects to context-appropriate destinations. ECHO is the first publicly available dataset to combine reconstructed scenes, human activity, natural language, and contextual-placement annotations. It comprises 3,805 human-annotated episodes across 159 floors of 115 HM3D scenes, involving 198 distinct objects. Each floor includes a complete RGB-D scan with human-annotated room labels and a surface list. Each episode provides synchronized RGB-D encounter clips; 6-DoF camera, human, and object trajectories; start and destination surfaces; an action caption; and a human-written context: a single sentence describing the inhabitant's routine that implies the destination without naming it. We evaluate contextual object placement using input-masked probes and an end-to-end baseline. Results show that no single input modality is sufficient, highlighting the need to jointly reason over scene structure, human activity, and contextual knowledge.

</details>


### [3] [Rubix: Global Correspondence-Free Point Set Alignment through Assignment Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10408)
*Subhransu S. Bhattacharjee,Dylan Campbell,Rahul Shome*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Rubix 将等权平面 Procrustes-Wasserstein 对齐转化为置换多边形，全局求解匹配与旋转，证明 n(n-1) 顶点紧界，并在实验中实现快速且更优的对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Procrustes-Wasserstein 对齐在无对应条件下联合估计匹配与旋转，但交替最小化易陷入次优解；因此需要平面问题的全局最优算法，并扩展到三维旋转和部分匹配。

Method: 对中心化点集，每个匹配 σ 定义复相关 z_σ = Σ_i conj(x_i)·y_{σ(i)}，其凸包为置换多边形。固定旋转下支撑顶点给出最优匹配，最远顶点给出全局对齐。精确算术下用分配查询在 O(n^5) 内恢复多边形；基于分配界的分支定界扩展到三维旋转及给定平移下的部分匹配。

Result: 证明 n≥2 时置换多边形顶点数的紧界为 n(n-1)，解决 Rote 的旋转-分配开放问题。在 MPEG-7 计时形状对上，Rubix 平均 12 ms 达到全部数值参考值，比同精度旋转网格快 50 倍；在真实 3D 扫描重力对齐匹配、形状检索和噪声晶体分类中，其距离优于交替最小化。

Conclusion: Rubix 为无对应 Procrustes-Wasserstein 问题提供了平面全局精确解法，并推广到三维旋转与部分匹配，兼具理论最优性和实际效率。

Abstract: Procrustes-Wasserstein alignment jointly estimates a matching and rotation without supplied correspondences, but alternating minimization can stop at suboptimal solutions. Rubix solves the equally weighted planar problem globally under squared Euclidean loss. Each matching $\sigma$ of two centered $n$-point sets defines a complex correlation $z_\sigma=\sum_i\bar x_i y_{\sigma(i)}$. Their convex hull is the permutation polygon: supporting vertices give optimal matchings at fixed rotations, and the farthest vertex gives the global alignment. We prove the sharp bound of $n(n-1)$ vertices for $n\ge2$, answering Rote's rotation-assignment open problem. In exact arithmetic, assignment queries recover the polygon in $\mathcal O(n^5)$ operations. Assignment-based bounds extend the approach to three-dimensional rotations and partial matching at a supplied translation through branch-and-bound. On timed MPEG-7 shape pairs, Rubix attains every numerical reference value in 12 ms on average, 50 times faster than a rotation grid at the same accuracy. Its distances improve gravity-aligned matching of real 3D scans, shape retrieval and noisy crystal classification over alternating minimization.

</details>


### [4] [Video Prediction Policy 2: Predict Better, Act Better](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10270)
*Yanjiang Guo,Haodong Yan,Zhide Zhong,Zhongru Zhang,Qingyuan Yang,Qingzhou Lu,Xiaoyu Chen,Yen-Jen Wang,Shuying Deng,Chenghan Yang,Puzhen Yuan,Chenxin Liu,Tun Ban,Xiang Zhu,Yichen Liu,Kun Feng,Haoang Li,Jianyu Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Video Prediction Policy 2（VPP2），一种世界动作模型（WAM），通过面向操作的大规模视频预训练、视频模型蒸馏为单步视觉规划器，以及基于混合 Transformer 的动作模块，实现视频预测与动作生成的强零样本泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 WAM 虽试图将视频预测先验迁移到动作学习，但在开放环境中常产生错误运动预测并导致错误动作。原因在于：基础视频模型未针对操作任务优化；简单将动作组件加入视频模型会显著损害其泛化能力。

Method: VPP2 首先构建大规模、多样化的操作视频数据集，为视频片段添加详细描述，并进行事件级视频预训练以提升开放操作任务泛化。其次，后训练并蒸馏视频模型为固定预测视野的单步视觉规划器。最后，通过混合 Transformer（MoT）架构引入动作模块，学习隐式逆动力学模型。

Result: 实验显示：VPP2-14B 在开放任务视频预测指令遵循成功率上比 Cosmos3-64B 高 11.0 个百分点；在真实世界零样本 ALOHA 操作任务成功率上超过最强基线 18.5 个百分点；在基准特定后训练后，于 LIBERO-Pro、LIBERO-OOD 和 RoboDojo 上取得所评估方法中的最高成功率。

Conclusion: VPP2 能在视频预测和动作生成两方面实现强零样本泛化，并作为通用机器人策略在开放任务与多个挑战性基准上优于现有方法，表明其设计有效缓解了 WAM 的运动预测错误与泛化退化问题。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) have emerged as an important class of generalist robot policies, aiming to transfer video prediction priors to action learning. However, we find that existing WAMs frequently produce incorrect motion predictions in open-ended environment, leading to erroneous actions. We attribute this limitation to two factors: (1) base video models are not optimized for manipulation, and (2) naively incorporating action components into video models can substantially degrade their generalization capabilities. We introduce Video Prediction Policy 2 (VPP2), a WAM that enables strong zero-shot generalization in both video prediction and action generation. First, we curate a large-scale, diverse dataset of manipulation videos to continue pretraining the base video foundation model. We annotate video clips with detailed captions and perform \textit{event-level} video pretraining to promote generalization across open-ended manipulation tasks. Second, we post-train and distill the video model into a single-step visual planner with fixed prediction horizon. Finally, we introduce action module via a mixture-of-transformers (MoT) architecture to learn implicit inverse dynamics model. Experiments demonstrate three key results: (1) VPP2-14B outperforms Cosmos3-64B by 11.0\% points in video prediction instruction-following success rate on open-ended tasks; (2) VPP2 surpasses the strongest baseline by 18.5\% points in success rate on real-world zero-shot ALOHA manipulation tasks; and (3) following benchmark-specific post-training, VPP2 achieves the highest success rates among evaluated methods on the challenging LIBERO-Pro, LIBERO-OOD, and RoboDojo benchmarks.

</details>


### [5] [UltraWorld: Learning Interactive Ultrasound World Models from Untracked Clinical Videos with Acoustic Sampling Map](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09785)
*Keke Yang,Erqi Wang,Sainan Guan,Hongliang Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UltraWorld 是一种自蒸馏方法，无需真实动作标注，将临床超声视频先验迁移到交互式世界模型中，预测探头运动的未来观测，并引入 AsMap 提升动作跟随。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主超声扫描需要世界模型预测探头运动结果，但同步视频-位姿对采集成本高且在常规临床记录中稀缺；同时可靠动作跟随需建模超声的截面采样几何。

Method: 从临床视频出发，将视频基础模型适配为以参考图像和解剖掩码为条件的超声生成器；沿 3D 解剖中的可编程轨迹采样解剖掩码，合成动作-视频对；再用合成对将生成器自蒸馏为世界模型，推理时无需解剖掩码或 3D 资产；并引入 Acoustic Sampling Map (AsMap)，将探头位姿与成像设置表示为像素级 3D 采样位置、波束方向和深度。

Result: 实验表明预测保真度和动作跟随均有提升；在九个模拟闭环局部规划回合中，UltraWorld 相比视觉伺服将到目标的平均最终距离和方向误差分别降低 29% 和 38%。

Conclusion: UltraWorld 提供了一种无需真实动作标注的超声世界模型自蒸馏方案，能有效提升预测保真度与动作跟随性能，支持自主超声扫描。

Abstract: World models can enable autonomous ultrasound scanning by predicting the outcomes of probe motions from local observations. Learning this action--observation relationship typically relies on synchronized video--pose pairs, which are costly to collect at scale and largely unavailable in routine clinical recordings. Reliable action following further requires modeling ultrasound's cross-sectional sampling geometry. We present UltraWorld, a self-distillation recipe that transfers priors from clinical ultrasound videos into interactive world models without real action annotations. Starting from clinical videos, we adapt a video foundation model into an ultrasound generator conditioned on reference images and anatomical masks. Anatomical masks sampled along programmable trajectories through 3D anatomy provide spatial guidance for synthesizing action--video pairs. We then use these synthetic pairs to self-distill the generator into a world model that predicts future observations from local observations and actions, without requiring anatomical masks or other 3D assets at inference time. To further improve action following, we introduce the Acoustic Sampling Map (AsMap), which represents probe poses and imaging settings as pixel-wise 3D sampling positions, beam directions, and depths. Experiments demonstrate improved prediction fidelity and action following. Across nine simulated closed-loop local planning episodes, UltraWorld reduces the mean final distance to the goal and orientation error by 29\% and 38\%, respectively, compared with visual servoing. Project Page: this https URL .

</details>


### [6] [WAPR: A Foundation Model for Wide-Angle Refinement in Unseen Object Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09535)
*Yulin Wang,Mengting Hu,Hongli Li,Jianghao Zhou,Chen Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出WAPR，一种零样本宽角度6D位姿细化模型，可细化旋转偏差达90°的候选位姿，并在仅有12个候选位姿时实现快速推理；同时构建SA6D大规模训练数据集和角度平衡损失，在七个BOP核心数据集上取得未见物体6D位姿定位与检测的先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实应用要求6D位姿估计准确、快速，并能扩展到未见物体；现有方法在宽角度旋转偏差、旋转对称物体训练和大规模数据支持方面仍面临挑战。

Method: WAPR采用零样本宽角度位姿细化，支持每个检测实例仅12个候选位姿，并利用旋转对称先验在损失计算前规范化对称等价位姿目标；构建SA6D数据集，通过KASAL辅助获得944个GSO扫描的旋转对称先验，并经几何与纹理增强扩展到约5万个增强物体实例和约200万张渲染RGB-D图像；引入角度平衡损失，降低无信息大误差样本影响，稳定不同角度范围的学习。

Result: WAPR支持每帧1秒内快速推理，吞吐量最高达每秒25个检测物体实例；在七个BOP核心数据集上，在快速和不受约束推理设置下，均取得未见物体6D位姿定位与检测的最先进性能。

Conclusion: WAPR通过宽角度零样本位姿细化、旋转对称先验、SA6D数据集和角度平衡损失，有效提升未见物体6D位姿估计的准确性、速度与可扩展性。

Abstract: Real-world applications require 6D pose estimation to be accurate, fast, and scalable to unseen objects. This paper introduces WAPR, a zero-shot wide-angle pose refinement model that refines candidate poses with rotational deviations up to 90 degrees. With as few as 12 candidate poses per detected object instance, WAPR supports fast inference within 1 s per frame and reaches a pose-estimation throughput of up to 25 detected object instances per second. To support wide-angle training for rotationally symmetric objects, WAPR uses rotational symmetry priors to canonicalize symmetry-equivalent pose targets before loss computation. We further construct SA6D, a large-scale 6D training dataset with such priors. SA6D obtains KASAL-assisted rotational symmetry priors for 944 GSO scans and expands them through geometry and texture augmentation into about 50K augmented object instances and about 2M rendered RGB-D images. In addition, an angle-balanced loss stabilizes learning across different angular ranges by reducing the influence of uninformative large-error cases. Experiments on seven BOP core datasets show that WAPR achieves state-of-the-art performance in unseen-object 6D pose localization and detection under both fast and unconstrained inference settings. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [7] [ActiveLang: Active Open-Vocabulary 3D Mapping with Semantic-Uncertainty-Guided Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09518)
*Liyan Chen,Hairong Yin,Huangying Zhan,Yi Xu,Raymond A. Yeh,Philippos Mordohai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ActiveLang 是一个主动开放词汇 3D 建图系统，利用语义不确定性引导探索，在紧凑双高斯表示上在线自适应语言特征，从而高效构建带语言标注的 3D 地图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人需在陌生环境和新任务中同时理解几何与语义，且常无法预知相关概念；因此需要支持开放词汇场景理解与人机交互的语言标注 3D 地图。

Method: ActiveLang 在紧凑双高斯表示上进行在线语言特征自适应，联合重建场景几何、外观与开放词汇语义，内存开销适中；其规划器高效选择信息量大的视角，减少观测次数并降低计算成本。

Result: 在 Replica 和 ScanNet++ 上，ActiveLang 在 2D 和 3D 开放词汇分割任务中相比在线与离线基线均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 主动探索场景能够更高效地构建语言标注 3D 地图。

Abstract: As robots increasingly assist humans with diverse tasks, they need both geometric and semantic understanding of their surroundings. Moreover, robots often operate in unfamiliar environments and take on new tasks without knowing the relevant concepts ahead of time. This motivates language-annotated 3D maps that support open-vocabulary scene understanding and human-robot interaction. We introduce ActiveLang, an autonomous system for active open-vocabulary 3D mapping with semantic-uncertainty-guided exploration. ActiveLang performs online language-feature adaptation on a compact dual-Gaussian representation to jointly reconstruct scene geometry, appearance, and open-vocabulary semantics with modest memory overhead. Its planner efficiently selects informative viewpoints, enabling effective mapping with fewer observations and lower computational cost. Experiments on Replica and ScanNet++ demonstrate substantial improvements in 2D and 3D open-vocabulary segmentation over both online and offline baselines, highlighting that actively exploring scenes builds language-annotated 3D maps more efficiently.

</details>


### [8] [TERRA: Learning Transportable Latent Actions through Temporal Effect Representation and Relational Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09509)
*Tianxingjian Ding,Mubarak Shah,Yu Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TERRA 通过紧凑时序效应表示（净特征变化加低阶窗口内动态）学习潜动作，并用 Effect-Anchored Transport（EAT）在不同初始状态间校验复用该潜变量，从而提升机器人策略学习与跨上下文泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜动作依赖视觉转移推断动作码，但其有效性取决于两点：动作码应从转移中保留什么，以及换到不同初始状态后语义是否一致。端点差分丢失运动过程，完整序列又引入无关变化；重构目标只能观察潜变量及其来源状态，无法保证复用一致性。

Method: TERRA 用紧凑时序效应描述转移：净特征变化与低阶窗口内动态分量，并据此学习连续潜变量；同一效应空间用于复用：EAT 在其他初始状态解码潜变量，并将结果效应锚定到源状态观测效应，使潜变量由跨上下文效果塑造，而非仅拟合来源转移。

Result: 使用冻结线性读出器时，TERRA 的动作预测精度优于 UniVLA 和 LAPA 风格基线，在视觉干扰下退化更慢，并且当接收上下文远离源状态时，迁移后的转移仍更忠实于供体动作；同预算对照表明增益主要来自 EAT。匹配预训练规模下，完整系统在 LIBERO 上平均成功率达 93.4%，UniVLA 为 91.8%。

Conclusion: TERRA 将时序效应表示与关系对齐统一起来，使潜动作既要保留关键运动动态，又能在不同初始状态中保持语义一致，从而提升机器人策略学习与跨上下文泛化性能。

Abstract: Latent actions supervise robot policies with action-like codes inferred from visual transitions, and their usefulness hinges on two questions: what a code keeps from a transition, and whether it still means the same thing when reused in a different initial state. The first is a tension in time: an endpoint difference discards how motion unfolds, while the full sequence admits nuisance variation. The second is left open by reconstruction, which only ever observes a latent together with the state it came from. We argue that both questions can be answered in the same place. TERRA (Temporal Effect Representation and Relational Alignment) describes a transition by a compact temporal effect, its net feature change together with a low-order within-window dynamics component, and learns a continuous latent from this effect. The same effect space then serves as the reference for reuse: Effect-Anchored Transport (EAT) decodes a latent in other initial states and anchors the resulting effect to the one observed at its source, so that the latent is shaped by what it does across contexts rather than only by the transition it came from. With frozen linear readers, TERRA predicts actions more accurately than UniVLA and a LAPA-style baseline, degrades more slowly under visual distractors, and keeps transported transitions faithful to the donor action as the recipient context moves farther away; a same-budget control shows that these gains come largely from EAT. At matched pretraining scale, the complete system reaches 93.4% average success on LIBERO, compared with 91.8% for UniVLA.

</details>


### [9] [RLHND: Video Foundation Models as Physically Grounded Hand Trackers for Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09455)
*Seungjun Moon,Subin Jeon,Sangwoo Kim,Hanbyul Joo,Jinwoo Shin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RLHND，一个基于视频基础模型的手部跟踪模型，从单目第一人称视频中联合估计手部姿态与真实触觉信息，并验证其在机器人学习中的效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用人类视频进行机器人策略训练日益流行，但现有手部跟踪器多从裁剪帧回归姿态，缺乏手部运动与物体交互先验，导致估计不准确且物理不一致；同时缺少接触、力等物理线索，限制了人类视频在机器人策略训练中的应用。

Method: RLHND将预训练的Cosmos 3视频扩散骨干通过干净潜变量条件化为确定性片段级特征提取器，继承手部运动与手-物交互先验。姿态估计预测解剖学合理的关节角度，并可选条件化形状参数以保持同一视频乃至同一演员视频间的手形一致。触觉估计采用独立触觉专家流，冻结姿态流训练，预测手部表面的密集接触与力。采用基于LBS的特征扩散实现顶点级特征提取，避免昂贵的逐顶点注意力。

Result: 在多个姿态估计基准数据集上达到SOTA，在接触与力估计上也达到SOTA。通过重定向结果和真实机器人实验证明了RLHND在机器人学习中的效用。代码将公开。

Conclusion: RLHND通过联合估计姿态与触觉，提升了手部跟踪的准确性与物理一致性，并为机器人学习提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Recently, approaches that leverage human video datasets for robot policy training have become increasingly prevalent. However, most existing hand trackers regress pose from cropped frames with limited priors on hand motion and object interaction, resulting in inaccurate and physically inconsistent estimates. Moreover, the lack of physical cues, e.g., contact and force, limits the use of human videos for robot policy training. To this end, we propose RLHND, a video foundation model-based hand tracking model that jointly estimates hand pose and realistic tactile information from monocular egocentric videos. RLHND turns the pre-trained Cosmos 3 video diffusion backbone into a deterministic clip-level feature extractor via clean-latent conditioning, carrying its learned priors on hand motion and hand-object interaction into tracking. For pose estimation, RLHND (i) predicts hand poses with anatomically plausible joint angles and (ii) enables optional conditioning on the shape parameter to maintain consistent hand shape within the same video and even across videos recorded by the same actor. For tactile estimation, a separate tactile expert stream, trained with the pose stream frozen, predicts dense contact and force over the hand surface. We further adopt LBS-based feature spreading to enable vertex-wise feature extraction without costly per-vertex attention. RLHND achieves state-of-the-art performance across various benchmark datasets for pose estimation, while also achieving state-of-the-art performance in contact and force estimation. Moreover, we demonstrate the utility of RLHND for robot learning through retargeting results and real-world robot experiments. The code will be publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [10] [Event-Aligned Visual Action Reasoning for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09427)
*Xiaomeng Yang,Yushu Wu,Yi Gao,Yuhao Lei,Xuan Zhang,Pu Zhao,Yanzhi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出事件对齐的视觉动作推理框架，按交互事件组织视觉-动作预测，并引入执行有效性头；在DOMINO上成功率提升10.26个百分点，且无需目标层级适应即可从Level 1迁移至Levels 2和3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型（WAMs）按预定义时间间隔构建视觉想象，未显式考虑任务关键交互与连接过渡的不同作用；作者认为有效的视觉预见应与任务相关交互及其推理需求直接对齐。

Method: 提出事件对齐的视觉动作推理框架：围绕交互事件组织视觉-动作预测；通过事件对齐的视觉-动作监督，使WAM在每次想象rollout中生成事件对齐的视觉上下文，重点关注关键状态变化；依据交互动态调整视觉推理粒度，任务关键事件细粒度推理，连接过渡粗粒度推进；引入执行有效性头，识别每个预测动作序列的有效部分，避免分块推理中的冗余动作。

Result: 在DOMINO上成功率较基线提升10.26个百分点，在RoboTwin 2.0上表现具有竞争力；从DOMINO Level 1迁移至Levels 2和3时无需目标层级适应。

Conclusion: 事件对齐的视觉预见能根据交互动态指导动作生成，提升任务成功率并实现跨层级泛化。

Abstract: World-Action Models (WAMs) utilize future visual prediction as an intermediate reasoning process to guide action generation. However, existing WAMs typically structure visual imagination according to predefined temporal intervals, without explicitly accounting for the different roles of task-critical interactions and connecting transitions. We argue that effective visual foresight should align directly with task-relevant interactions and their corresponding reasoning demands. To this end, we introduce an event-aligned visual action reasoning framework that organizes visual-action prediction around interaction events. Through event-aligned visual-action supervision, WAM learns to generate event-aligned visual context in each imagined rollout, placing greater emphasis on critical state changes that inform action generation. This shapes the visual reasoning granularity according to the underlying interaction dynamics, with detailed reasoning around task-critical events and coarser progression through connecting transitions. Furthermore, we introduce an execution validity head that identifies the valid portion of each predicted action sequence, avoiding redundant actions during chunked inference. Experiments demonstrate a 10.26 percentage point improvement in DOMINO success rate over baseline and competitive performance on RoboTwin 2.0. It also transfers from DOMINO Level 1 to Levels 2 and 3 without target-level adaptation.

</details>


### [11] [StableGrasp: Reconstructing Physically Stable Human Hand Grasps from Single Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09195)
*Han Jiang,Etienne Vouga,Qixing Huang,Georgios Pavlakos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StableGrasp提出基于可微分仿真的优化框架，将视觉手部姿态与控制目标分离，通过最小化抓取动能重建单张RGB图像中物理稳定且视觉一致的人类抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单张RGB图像重建物理稳定的人类抓取具有挑战性：物理建模本身困难，且需同时估计视觉约束的手部姿态与稳定抓取的控制目标；现有方法仅建模视觉手部几何而忽略物理，或物理建模不够合理，限制了抓取的物理有效性。

Method: 提出StableGrasp，一个可微分仿真优化框架，显式分离视觉手部姿态与决定抓取力的控制目标。在可微分仿真器中联合优化手部几何与控制，通过最小化抓取动能，并正则化手部几何以保持视觉一致性与几何合理性。

Result: 在严格物理仿真下，重建抓取稳定性显著提升，同时与输入图像保持视觉一致且几何合理。实验表明，相比其他手部控制策略，本方法产生远更稳定的抓取，并能将纯视觉抓取重建流程的输出转化为物理稳定的抓取。

Conclusion: 通过分离视觉姿态与控制目标并在可微分仿真中优化，StableGrasp有效提升了单图人类抓取重建的物理稳定性和视觉一致性。

Abstract: Reconstructing a physically stable human grasp from a single RGB image is challenging because physically modeling grasps is itself difficult, and the problem requires estimating not only a visually constrained hand pose but also a control target that stabilizes the grasp. Existing methods either model only visual hand geometry without considering physics, or rely on less plausible physical modeling, which limits the physical validity of the resulting grasps. In this paper, we present StableGrasp, a differentiable simulation-based optimization framework that explicitly separates the visual hand pose from the control target that determines the grasping forces. Our method jointly optimizes hand geometry and control by minimizing the kinetic energy of the grasp in a differentiable simulator, while regularizing the hand geometry to preserve visual consistency and geometric plausibility. The reconstructed grasps are substantially more stable under rigorous physical simulation, while remaining visually consistent with the input images and geometrically plausible. Experiments show that our approach produces far more stable grasps than alternative hand-control strategies, benefiting visual-only grasp reconstruction pipelines by turning their outputs into physically stable grasps.

</details>


### [12] [GUARD: Geometric Uncertainty-Aware Point Cloud Denoising and Segmentation for Robotic Hard Disk Drive Disassembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09068)
*Zuoxu Wang,Xiao Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出GUARD，一个几何不确定性感知框架，用于在单次前向传播中完成点云过滤与分割，以区分真实零件几何与扫描/重建伪影，提升机器人拆解中的部件识别可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠机器人拆解需要零件级表示，但硬盘点云中的结构化幽灵伪影会在局部类似有效组件，却与整体几何不一致，导致错误测量获得合理语义标签。仅依赖语义预测置信度无法判断底层几何是否可靠，因此需要建模几何可靠性。

Method: GUARD将多尺度几何Transformer与多带宽随机傅里叶特征高斯过程结合，估计逐点几何不确定性，并辅以预测熵，在抑制不可靠测量的同时保留有信息结构；点过滤和分割在单次前向传播中完成。

Result: 在2,745个真实HDD点云上，GUARD将PointNet++分割mIoU从0.7739提升至0.8318。在ShapeNetPart和ScanNet上验证了跨 corruption 类型、点云域和分割主干的鲁棒性。在人工标注ScanNet样本上，几何不确定性检测损坏点的F1为0.7931，而预测熵仅为0.2212。

Conclusion: 结果表明显式区分几何可靠性与语义置信度具有重要价值，并揭示了伪影抑制与保留有信息结构之间的权衡。GUARD为面向部件识别和后续机器人操作的缺陷3D测量解释提供了可靠性感知方法。

Abstract: Reliable robotic disassembly requires part-level representations that distinguish genuine component geometry from scanning and reconstruction artifacts. In point clouds of hard disk drives (HDDs), structured ghost artifacts can resemble valid components locally while remaining inconsistent with the overall geometry, allowing erroneous measurements to receive plausible semantic labels. This creates an engineering information problem: semantic prediction confidence alone does not establish whether the underlying geometry is reliable. We propose \textbf{GUARD}, a geometric uncertainty-aware framework that performs point filtering and segmentation within a single forward pass by modeling the reliability of learned geometric representations. GUARD combines a multi-scale geometric transformer with a multi-bandwidth random Fourier feature Gaussian Process to estimate per-point geometric uncertainty, complemented by predictive entropy to suppress unreliable measurements while preserving informative structures. Evaluation on 2,745 real HDD point clouds shows that GUARD improves PointNet++ segmentation mean intersection over union from 0.7739 to 0.8318. Additional experiments on ShapeNetPart and ScanNet examine robustness across corruption types, point-cloud domains, and segmentation backbones. On manually annotated ScanNet samples, geometric uncertainty achieves a corrupted-point detection F1 score of 0.7931, compared with 0.2212 for predictive entropy. The results demonstrate the value of distinguishing geometric reliability from semantic confidence and reveal a tradeoff between artifact suppression and preservation of informative structures. GUARD contributes a reliability-aware approach to interpreting imperfect 3D measurements for component identification and subsequent robotic handling. Project website: this https URL .

</details>

### [13] [A Scoping Review and Experimental Study on Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback for Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09891)
*Alexandra Coroiu,Andrea Vogt,Viktor Werbilo,Andreas Poppele,Johann Christensen,Sven Hallerbach*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该文通过PRISMA范围综述和一项VR被试间实验，首次聚焦人机协作中RLHF的双向闭环设计；发现用户主动反馈比系统主动反馈更能捕捉感知安全，反馈时机直接影响反馈质量，未来需在真实HRC中评估反馈收集方法对人与机器人指标的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HRC可推动工业4.0大规模定制，RLHF有望用于开发安全的AI机器人，但仍面临AI开发中的安全、人类反馈质量和双向人机适应等实际挑战；现有研究需系统映射这些挑战的解决方法，并实证检验反馈时机等关键空白。

Method: 遵循PRISMA指南进行范围综述，筛选199条记录并纳入20篇2020—2025年同行评审文献，覆盖多个HRC领域，聚焦RLHF的双向闭环设计；随后开展被试间VR实验，比较系统发起与用户发起反馈对机器人避障安全导航中近体行为（proxemic behaviour）的影响，并用贝叶斯模型分析反馈与安全指标的关系：心理安全（实验后问卷）和物理安全（逆碰撞时间）。

Result: 综述发现多种反馈模态可在不同反馈格式下收集数据，数据可整合到AI训练的不同阶段，形成多步开发流程；试点实验常用于基于人类和机器人指标评估HRC系统。实验显示，用户发起反馈比系统发起反馈更能捕捉感知安全，表明反馈时机直接影响反馈质量。

Conclusion: RLHF依赖恰当的反馈方法来保障HRC中的AI安全；未来RLHF研究应优先开展真实的HRC实验，评估反馈收集方法对相关人类和机器人指标的影响。

Abstract: Human-Robot Collaboration (HRC) can facilitate mass customisation in Industry 4.0, with Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) representing a promising approach for developing safe AI-based robots. Practical challenges remain regarding safety during AI development, human feedback quality, and bidirectional human-robot adaptation. We conducted a scoping review of RLHF in HRC systems, mapping methods that address these challenges. Following PRISMA guidelines, we screened 199 records and included 20 peer-reviewed publications (2020-2025) spanning multiple HRC domains. To our knowledge, this is the first review focused on the bidirectional, closed-loop design of RLHF. Our review found multiple feedback modalities enabling data collection in various feedback formats. Collected data can be integrated at different stages of AI training, resulting in a multi-step development process. Pilot experiments are commonly used to evaluate HRC systems based on both human and robot metrics. To empirically test a key gap identified in the review, we conducted a between-subjects VR experiment comparing system- and user-initiated feedback on robot proxemic behaviour for safe navigation. Using Bayesian models, we analysed the relation between the collected feedback and safety metrics: psychological safety (post-experiment questionnaire) and physical safety (inverse time-to-collision). Results show that user-initiated feedback captures perceived safety better than system-initiated feedback, indicating that feedback timing directly affects feedback quality. Our review and experiment findings show that RLHF relies on appropriate feedback methods to ensure AI safety in HRC, and future RLHF research should prioritise realistic HRC experiments evaluating the effects of feedback collection methods on relevant human and robot metrics.

</details>

### [14] [FlightMagNav: An Open Dataset and Probabilistic Map Learning and Validation Framework for Outdoor Magnetic Field-Based Positioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08982)
*Isaac Skog,Miguel Ramos Galrinho,Martin Gelin*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 公开了一个面向近地表空中平台磁场定位的无人机数据集，包含双光泵磁力仪和GNSS辅助惯性导航系统测量，并提供磁场地图学习与验证的概率框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁场定位无需外部基础设施，且难以被大规模干扰，具有鲁棒性；为促进近地表空中平台的磁场定位研究，需要开放数据集支撑磁场地图学习与验证。

Method: 使用无人机搭载两个光泵磁力仪（量子磁力仪）和GNSS辅助惯性导航系统采集数据；数据集覆盖磁场地图学习与验证测量；同时提出概率框架用于磁场地图学习与验证，并示例说明数据集用法，最后概述可研究方向。

Result: 发布了可用于磁场地图学习与验证的开放无人机测量数据集，并通过概率框架展示了该数据集在磁场定位研究中的使用方式；摘要未报告定量定位性能结果。

Conclusion: 该开放数据集与概率框架可推动近地表空中平台磁场定位研究，并为后续研究提供了可探索的方向。

Abstract: Magnetic field-based positioning is a resilient positioning technology that requires no external infrastructure and is hard to jam at scale. To facilitate research on magnetic field-based positioning for aerial platforms operating close to the Earth's surface, an open dataset is presented with measurements collected using an unmanned aerial vehicle carrying two optically pumped magnetometers, a type of quantum magnetometer, and a global navigation satellite system-aided inertial navigation system. The dataset includes measurements for both magnetic-field map learning and validation. Along with the dataset, a probabilistic framework for magnetic-field map learning and validation is presented and used to illustrate how the dataset may be used. Finally, we outline research directions that may be explored using the dataset.

</details>

### [15] [On the complexity of the single-move labeled token routing problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09084)
*Nicolas Bousquet,Remy El Sabeh,Amer E. Mouawad,Naomi Nishimura*

Main category: cs.CC

TL;DR: 本文研究中性原子量子计算中原子移动所抽象出的单步带标签令牌路由问题，并给出其在网格/平面图与树上的NP完全性及参数化复杂度结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在中性原子量子计算机中，原子需沿空位路径移动到目标位置，且目标位置可能只为特定原子种类保留。该物理任务被抽象为 Single-Move Labeled Token Routing：图中每个源和目标顶点带有标签集合，令牌占据源点；解需将源匹配到兼容目标、为匹配对规划路径，并给出移动顺序，使得移动某令牌时其路径上无其他令牌。

Method: 作者形式化该路由问题，并利用复杂性归约与参数化复杂度分析展开研究。重点关注网格图、最大度四平面图以及树；引入并分析解边重数（solution edge multiplicity）和候选边重数（candidate edge multiplicity），以及候选点重数；考察最大度、候选边重数等参数对问题可解性的影响。

Result: 已知全兼容时多项式可解，而每个源仅兼容一个目标时在网格图上NP完全。本文证明该受限情形在网格及最大度四平面图上仍NP完全，即使存在成对边不相交路由的解。树上最大度三时已知NP完全；本文进一步证明按解边重数上界参数化是W[1]-hard，即使给定移动顺序；在无界度树上，即使候选边重数至多为八，问题仍NP完全。本文还证明树上按最大度与候选边重数联合参数化是固定参数可 tractable（FPT），按候选点重数参数化也是FPT；除非P=NP，最大度或候选边重数均不可省略。

Conclusion: 该工作刻画了带标签令牌路由在拓扑结构与标签兼容性约束下的计算边界，表明最大度、候选边重数和候选点重数是树情形可解性的关键参数，为中性原子量子计算中原子移动与调度算法的理论分析提供了依据。

Abstract: In neutral-atom quantum computers, atoms are moved to target positions along paths of empty positions, and a target position may be reserved for one species of atom. Motivated by this task, we introduce Single-Move Labeled Token Routing: every source and every target vertex of a graph is assigned a set of labels, and tokens occupy the sources. A solution consists of a matching that assigns each source to a compatible target (one whose label set intersects its own), a route for each matched pair, and a movement order in which, when a token is moved, its route contains no other token. The problem is known to be polynomial-time solvable when every source is compatible with every target, and $\mathsf{NP}$-complete on grid graphs when each source is compatible with exactly one target. We prove that the latter case remains $\mathsf{NP}$-complete on grids and on planar graphs of maximum degree four even when some solution has pairwise edge-disjoint routes. On trees, the problem is known to be $\mathsf{NP}$-complete even for maximum degree three. We study trees through the solution edge multiplicity, the largest number of routes of a solution sharing an edge, and the candidate edge multiplicity, the largest number of compatible pairs whose paths share an edge. We prove that on trees of maximum degree three, the problem is $\mathsf{W}[1]$-hard parameterized by a bound on the solution edge multiplicity, even when a movement order is given, and that on trees of unbounded degree, it is $\mathsf{NP}$-complete even when the candidate edge multiplicity is at most eight. We show that on trees the problem is fixed-parameter tractable parameterized by the maximum degree together with the candidate edge multiplicity, and also by the candidate vertex multiplicity, the same count at vertices. Unless $\mathsf{P}=\mathsf{NP}$, neither the maximum degree nor the candidate edge multiplicity can be omitted.

</details>

### [16] [RoboJEPA: Scaling Robotic Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10515)
*Artem Zholus,Nicolas Beltran-Velez,Jianhao Yuan,Sarath Chandar,Tushar Nagarajan,Daniel Severo,Koustuv Sinha,Michal Drozdzal,Adriana Romero Soriano,Jeannette Bohg,Nicolas Ballas,Mahmoud Assran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RoboJEPA：基于JEPA的潜在世界模型，在覆盖12种机器人构型的大规模数据上训练；首次为多具身真实机器人世界模型建立缩放定律，并证明想象误差与规划性能随算力可预测提升，支持零样本长时程机器人规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在世界模型已能预测未来状态并在真实世界规划，但领域缺乏原则性方法估计其能力如何随模型规模、数据和算力扩展，这一问题阻碍了进展。

Method: 构建RoboJEPA，采用联合嵌入预测架构JEPA，并在覆盖12种机器人具身的大规模数据集上训练；分析潜在rollout的想象误差随算力的缩放规律；关联下游机器人规划性能；将模型零样本部署为机器人智能体，以单张目标图像为条件进行规划；发布模型检查点、训练与机器人部署代码。

Result: RoboJEPA的想象误差随算力呈二阶幂律，可外推预测模型质量；下游机器人规划性能随算力可预测提升，且与想象误差强相关，可作为真实机器人评估的可靠代理；模型规模达8B参数，是迄今最大的JEPA预测器模型；在真实硬件上零样本完成需长时程规划的任务。

Conclusion: 这是首个为基于真实机器人数据的多具身机器人世界模型建立缩放定律的工作；结果表明潜在世界模型可随算力可预测扩展，并能作为零样本机器人智能体进行长时程规划。

Abstract: Latent world models have shown a remarkable ability to predict future states and to plan in the real world. In practice, however, we lack a principled way to estimate how their capabilities scale with model size, data, and compute, an open problem that slows progress in the field. In this work we present RoboJEPA, a world model based on the Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) and trained on a large-scale dataset spanning 12 robotic embodiments. We show that RoboJEPA's imagination error, the error of its latent rollouts, follows a second-order power law in compute, allowing us to predict model quality well beyond the scale at which the law is fit. We further show that downstream robotic planning performance improves predictably with compute, and that imagination error is strongly correlated with it, making it a reliable proxy for real-robot evaluation. Finally, we demonstrate that latent world models can be deployed zero-shot as robotic agents, planning toward a single goal image to solve tasks requiring long-horizon planning on real hardware. We release all model checkpoints together with our training and robot deployment code. To our knowledge, this is the first work to establish scaling laws for multi-embodiment robotic world models trained on real robot data, and RoboJEPA, at 8B parameters, is the largest JEPA predictor model trained to date.

</details>


### [17] [MeshSIPP: Efficient Lattice Planning in Dynamic Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09652)
*Marat Agranovskiy,Konstantin Yakovlev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MeshSIPP 通过将运动原语按空间束合并验证并延迟精确出发时间搜索，在保持完备性和最优性的同时，将动态环境下时空轨迹规划加速至多 3 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态环境自主导航需计算满足非完整运动约束的时空轨迹；基于预计算可行运动原语构建状态格并与安全区间路径规划（SIPP）结合，虽有理论保证且能生成可行路径，但为平滑导航所需的丰富原语集导致分支因子过大，与时间依赖障碍区间结合时计算代价高昂。

Method: 提出 MeshSIPP：利用许多原语扫过相同区域、可一起验证的特性，将原语作为空间束传播，用轻量级包围区间检查进行筛选，并将昂贵的精确出发时间搜索推迟到原语到达终止状态；同时采用时间感知剪枝规则尽早丢弃冗余时空分支。

Result: 证明搜索具有完备性和最优性；在超过 6000 个基准实例和实时 ROS 2 仿真中，MeshSIPP 相比最先进的时空规划器取得最高 3 倍加速。

Conclusion: MeshSIPP 有效消除了状态格与 SIPP 结合规划中的计算瓶颈，在动态环境中兼顾效率、完备性与最优性，是面向非完整运动约束的高效时空规划器。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation in dynamic environments requires computing spatiotemporal trajectories that satisfy non-holonomic motion constraints. When the trajectories of the moving obstacles are predictable or known, a promising approach is to rely on the combination of state lattices constructed from precomputed feasible motion primitives and Safe Interval Path Planning -- a search-based algorithm with strong theoretical guarantees. While this approach yields feasible paths, the rich primitive sets needed for smooth navigation induce a large branching factor, which becomes costly when coupled with time-dependent obstacle intervals. To this end, we present MeshSIPP, an efficient planner that removes the computational bottleneck by exploiting the fact that many primitives sweep the same regions and can therefore be validated together. MeshSIPP propagates primitives as spatial bundles, screens them with lightweight bounding-interval checks, and defers the expensive exact departure-time search until a primitive reaches its terminal state. A time-aware pruning rule additionally discards redundant space-time branches early in the search. We prove that the resulting search is complete and optimal. Extensive experiments over more than 6,000 benchmark instances and real-time ROS~2 simulations show that MeshSIPP achieves up to a 3$\times$ speedup over state-of-the-art spatiotemporal planners.

</details>


### [18] [Learning Situation-Conditioned Thinking Policies for Long-Term LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09590)
*Hong Su*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出情境条件化思维记忆框架，将历史推理经验转化为轻量策略以预测当前情境应激活何种思考，并由LLM负责详细推理，从而在不无限增长显式记忆与上下文的前提下复用经验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期自主智能体需复用累积推理经验，但显式历史记忆与LLM上下文不能无限增长；现有记忆机制主要检索、摘要或压缩过去内容，无法直接学习何时激活特定思考，也难以从时间分散经验中发现新思维知识。

Method: 将历史推理经验转化为情境条件化的轻量策略，预测当前情境（可含时序或时空演化，而非仅当前状态）应思考什么，细节推理留给LLM；周期性地跨多个独立episode分析临时经验，识别重复的长程规律，将其固化为新思维知识，并由轻量策略进一步内化。

Result: 学习策略在时序规则泛化上达到1.000 F1；将DeepSeek推理F1从0.789提升至0.868；在30,000个历史情境下，每查询在线处理时间从0.3636 ms降至0.0382 ms；在足够重复的跨经验证据后，关系发现F1与未来思维准确率达到1.000。

Conclusion: 该框架表明，通过情境条件化策略与跨经验规律固化，可在提升推理性能的同时显著降低在线开销，并实现长期自主智能体中的可扩展经验复用。

Abstract: Long-running autonomous agents must reuse accumulated reasoning experience without allowing explicit historical memory and LLM context to grow indefinitely. However, existing memory mechanisms mainly retrieve, summarize, or compress past content and do not directly learn when particular kinds of thinking should be activated or discover new thinking knowledge from temporally dispersed experiences. This paper proposes a situation-conditioned thinking memory framework that transforms historical reasoning experience into a lightweight policy for predicting what should be thought about in the current situation, while leaving detailed reasoning to a large language model. Situations may represent temporal or spatiotemporal evolution rather than only current states. Temporary experiences are also periodically analyzed across multiple independent episodes to identify repeated long-range regularities, which are consolidated into new thinking knowledge and further internalized by the lightweight policy. Experiments show that the learned policy achieves 1.000 F1 on temporal-rule generalization, improves DeepSeek reasoning F1 from 0.789 to 0.868, reduces online processing time from 0.3636 ms to 0.0382 ms per query at 30,000 historical situations, and reaches 1.000 relation-discovery F1 and future-thinking accuracy after sufficient repeated cross-experience evidence.

</details>


### [19] [Shared-Roadmap Generation and Evaluator for Multi-Agent Path Planning Using Heterogeneous Graph Neural Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09034)
*Brandon Ho,Nikola Rogers,Seung-Kyum Choi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于异构GNN的可扩展框架，自动生成并评估共享多智能体路图，通过专家轨迹占用密度图剪枝冗余节点与边，在密集路图上至少减少40%运行时间和图规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续环境中的多智能体路径规划通常依赖路图以平衡安全性与搜索效率，但传统栅格路图或标准采样方法常面临图密度与找到可行高质量解可能性之间的权衡。

Method: 将路点、智能体位置和任务位置建模为异构图中的不同节点，使模型能推理全局连通性与智能体间交互；利用专家求解轨迹聚合的占用密度图训练GNN，识别关键兴趣点并剪除冗余节点和边，生成紧凑、协调感知、对任务排列不变且可复用于多智能体取送任务的路图。

Result: 实验表明该框架能减少规划开销，并有潜力找到更优解；在密集路图上，运行时间和图规模均至少降低40%。

Conclusion: 该框架可自动生成紧凑、协调感知且可复用的共享多智能体路图，提升连续环境多智能体路径规划的效率与解质量潜力。

Abstract: Multi-agent path planning (MAPP) in continuous environments often relies on roadmaps to balance safety and search efficiency. However, traditional roadmap generation methods, such as lattice grids or standard sampling-based approaches, frequently face a trade-off between graph density and the likelihood of finding feasible, high-quality solutions. In this paper, we propose a scalable heterogeneous Graph Neural Network (GNN) framework for the automated generation and evaluation of shared multi-agent roadmaps. Our model covers the representation of waypoints, agent locations, and task locations as distinct nodes in a heterogeneous graph, allowing it to reason over global connectivity and inter-agent interactions. By training on occupation density maps aggregated and collected from expert solver trajectories, the GNN learns to identify critical points of interest and prune redundant nodes and edges. This process produces a compact, coordination-aware roadmap that is invariant to task permutations and is reusable for multi-agent pick and delivery tasks. Experimental results demonstrate that our framework can reduce planning effort and can potentially find better solutions, reaching at least 40% reduction in runtime and in graph size for dense roadmaps.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [20] [A Review Of Robotic World Models For Dynamic Environments Based On Factor And Scene Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08800)
*Marco Giberna,Miguel Fernandez-Cortizas,Jose Luis Sanchez Lopez,Holger Voos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该综述系统梳理了机器人图基世界模型（因子图、场景图及二者混合模型）如何表示、构建/更新并利用动态环境，并总结了相关趋势与开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人图基世界模型多以静态环境为简化假设，但真实环境具有动态性，静态世界表示难以应对；因此需要全面审视动态性在图基世界模型中的处理方式。

Method: 围绕三个方面展开：适合的动态表示、表示构建与更新流程、面向下游任务的利用；分别评述因子图、场景图及混合模型，分析可建模的动态类型并归类常见架构模式。

Result: 综述归纳了因子图提供不确定性感知的几何估计、场景图提供结构化语义抽象，以及混合模型结合二者的优势；总结了动态建模方法与常见架构模式，并识别出不确定性传播、动态实体运动与尺度可观测性、可扩展终身维护、数据集与评估协议等趋势和挑战。

Conclusion: 动态图基世界模型需重点解决从学习感知到表示层的不确定性传播、最小感知下动态实体运动与尺度的可观测性、可扩展的终身维护，以及缺乏将世界模型质量与动态下游任务性能挂钩的数据集和评估协议等问题。

Abstract: Models based on graphs have emerged in robotics as a powerful foundation for internal world representations, where factor and scene graphs are among the most prominent model types found in the related literature and in successful robotic solutions. Initially, many of these models were assuming static environments as a simplification. Herein, factor graphs mainly provide uncertainty-aware geometric estimations while scene graphs enable a structured semantic abstraction. However, real-world robotic environments are often dynamic, posing severe challenges for purely static world representations. Therefore, this review presents a comprehensive view on how dynamic aspects of real-world environments can be addressed in such graph-based world models. We organize our assessments around three main aspects: (I) suitable representations, (II) pipelines to construct and update the representations, and (III) their exploitation for downstream tasks. We review approaches that are either based on factor or scene graphs, but put special emphasis on novel approaches that combine both types to form hybrid models. We mainly analyze how different types of dynamics can be modeled herein, and categorize common architectural patterns. Finally, emerging trends and open challenges are identified, including uncertainty propagation from learned perception through the representation layers, the observability of dynamic-entity motion and scale under minimal sensing, scalable lifelong maintenance, and the lack of datasets and evaluation protocols that ground world-model quality in downstream task performance under dynamics.

</details>


### [21] [Not All Uncertainty Matters: Simulation-in-the-Loop Fast-Slow Reasoning for Decision-Critical Autonomous Driving System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09520)
*Jiayi Chen,Shuai Wang,Guangxu Zhu,Derrick Wing Kwan Ng,Chengzhong Xu,Kaibin Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SIGMA 是一个面向任务的快慢协同仿真在环框架，通过评估云端推理对规划成本的预期收益来决定是否调用云端模型，从而在自动驾驶中减少不必要的云端交互并提升规划与导航表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型虽能为自动驾驶提供开放世界感知与推理，但计算成本高、推理延迟大，难以持续在云端使用。快慢协同中，车载模块负责实时感知与控制，云端仅在需要时提供高层推理。核心挑战在于判断云端推理何时应影响时间敏感的驾驶决策；现有方法多依赖感知不确定性、启发式触发或资源驱动策略，未评估解决不确定性是否会改善规划。

Method: 提出 SIGMA，一种仿真在环的任务导向快慢协同框架。SIGMA 将规划器嵌入不确定性评估中，在语义与几何不确定性下生成合理场景实现，并评估其对可行轨迹与规划成本的影响，进而估计解决不确定性带来的规划成本预期降低量。同时引入期望规划收益（EPG）作为决策级指标，用于云端调用、云端引导集成以及在截止时间和资源约束下的请求优先级排序。

Result: 在 CARLA 中的实验表明，SIGMA 在静态和动态障碍场景下减少了不必要的云端交互，并改善了规划、效率与导航成功率。与固定周期协同相比，SIGMA 将不必要云端交互减少 50%，导航成功率提升超过 6%，动态场景中完成时间最多缩短 26.2%。

Conclusion: SIGMA 通过任务导向的期望规划收益评估，使云端推理仅在能显著改善规划时介入，从而在降低云端依赖的同时提升自动驾驶的规划质量、运行效率与导航成功率，为快慢协同自动驾驶提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (VLMs) provide powerful open-world perception and reasoning for autonomous driving, but their high computational cost and inference latency make continuous cloud-side use impractical. This motivates fast--slow collaboration, where efficient onboard modules handle real-time perception and control while cloud models provide high-level reasoning only when needed. The key challenge is deciding when cloud reasoning should influence time-critical driving decisions. Existing methods often rely on perception uncertainty, heuristic triggers, or resource-driven policies, without assessing whether resolving an uncertainty will improve planning. We propose \textbf{SIGMA}, a simulation-in-the-loop framework for task-oriented fast--slow collaboration. SIGMA embeds the planner into uncertainty assessment and evaluates how plausible scene realizations under semantic and geometric uncertainty affect feasible trajectories and planning cost. Based on these outcomes, it estimates the expected reduction in planning cost from resolving uncertainty. We further introduce expected planning gain (EPG), a decision-level metric for cloud invocation, cloud-guidance integration, and request prioritization under deadline and resource constraints. Experiments in CARLA show that SIGMA reduces unnecessary cloud interactions while improving planning, efficiency, and navigation success in static and dynamic obstacle scenarios. Compared with fixed-period collaboration, SIGMA reduces unnecessary cloud interactions by 50\%, improves navigation success by more than 6\%, and cuts finish time by up to 26.2\% in dynamic scenarios.

</details>


### [22] [Sparse Feature Policy Unlearning Mitigates State Hallucination in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09496)
*Jiho Lee,Jeongeun Park,Heayoun Choi,Taekyung Kim,Eunwoo Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型中的状态幻觉失败模式，并提出SOUL方法，通过稀疏自编码器识别幻觉相关稀疏特征，选择性遗忘导致状态幻觉的策略知识，从而在仿真和真实环境中减少幻觉失败并提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在机器人操作中泛化能力强，但实际部署常出现反复不可靠行为。作者关注状态幻觉：VLA在机器人-物体状态未真正达成时仍继续按已达成状态行动。现有方法缺乏对这种失败机制的理解和针对性修正。

Method: 首先分析状态幻觉现象，发现其伴随对任务相关视觉区域注意力减弱；利用稀疏自编码器进行机制解释，识别幻觉发生时的稀疏特征。基于此提出SOUL（Sparse feature pOlicy UnLearning）：将幻觉失败中识别的稀疏特征作为遗忘目标，将成功行为中识别的稀疏特征作为保留目标，对策略中与状态幻觉相关的知识进行选择性遗忘/反学习。

Result: 在多种VLA架构以及仿真和真实环境中的实验表明，SOUL显著减少幻觉导致的失败，提高任务成功率，同时没有大幅损害原有操作能力。

Conclusion: 可解释的特征分析为选择性修改机器人策略中的不良知识提供了实用基础，说明针对状态幻觉等失败模式进行机制层面的特征干预是可行且有效的。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown strong generalization in robotic manipulation by leveraging rich representations from pretrained vision-language models. However, their deployment in real-world environments remains limited by recurring unreliable behaviors. In this work, we study state hallucination, a recurring failure pattern in which a VLA continues acting as if an unrealized robot-object state had been achieved. Our analyses find that state hallucination coincides with weakened attention to task-relevant visual regions, and a mechanistic interpretation via sparse autoencoders reveals that hallucination-associated sparse features are activated when these failures occur. Based on this analysis, we propose SOUL (Sparse feature pOlicy UnLearning), which selectively unlearns policy knowledge associated with state hallucination behaviors, where sparse features identified from hallucination failures and successful behaviors serve as explicit forgetting and retention targets, respectively. Experiments across VLA architectures in simulated and real-world environments show that our method substantially reduces hallucinated failures and improves task success without substantially compromising the existing manipulation capabilities. These results suggest that interpretable feature analysis provides a practical basis for selectively modifying undesirable knowledge in robot policies.

</details>


### [23] [SiGNgapore - An Interactive Dataset for Sign-based Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09488)
*Nicky Zimmerman,Joel Loo,Zishuo Wang,David Hsu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文发布了一个用于无地图导航、以导航标志为中心的独特数据集，覆盖新加坡多个公共空间，包含多模态传感器数据和导航任务，旨在推动机器人利用人类导航标志实现自主导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人在人类环境中发展的关键挑战之一，是在没有预建地图的情况下进行导航；利用人类为自身设计的导航标志（如指示牌）对实现自主性至关重要。

Method: 使用手持设备在新加坡多个公共空间采集数据，场景围绕导航标志及其复杂环境，包含RGB、稀疏深度、里程计和IMU测量；同时提供56个长时程导航任务和450多个以标志为中心的导航场景。数据以人类可读格式提供，并附带转换为ROS 2的实用脚本，还提供测试场地的 venue maps 和与GPS对齐的场景图。

Result: 构建并公开了一个面向无地图导航的标志中心数据集，包含多模态传感数据、导航任务、场景信息、ROS 2转换工具、场地地图和GPS对齐场景图。

Conclusion: 该数据集可用于研究机器人如何利用人类导航标志在无预建地图环境中进行长时程导航，并讨论了其潜在应用场景。

Abstract: The future of autonomous robots in human-oriented environments depends on their ability to navigate in the absence of prebuilt maps; exploiting navigational aids, such as navigational signs, designed by humans for humans is critical for enabling autonomy. This manuscript introduces a unique dataset collected using a handheld device, at various public spaces across Singapore, representing environments that humans frequent daily, and focusing on sign-centric decision making for mapless navigation. The dataset includes RGB, sparse depth, odometry and IMU measurements of scenarios centered around navigational signs and the complex environments where they are placed. Additionally, we provide 56 long-horizon navigation missions and over 450 sign-centric scenarios. All data is provided in a human- readable format, as well as utility scripts for conversion to ROS 2. Lastly, we provide venue maps and GPS aligned scene graphs of the test environments. We discuss the potential use cases for this dataset.

</details>


### [24] [Precise SE(3) End-Effector Tracking in Whole-Body Humanoid Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09479)
*Joohwan Seo,Xiaofeng Guo,Jinkun Cao,Roberto Horowitz,Rocky Duan,Guanya Shi,Koushil Sreenath*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ResGAC——结合几何导纳控制（GAC）与残差强化学习的全身人形控制器，在真实Unitree G1上实现人形机器人行走等全身运动中的精确末端位姿跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人全身运动时，浮动基座振荡、重力、动态耦合与行走扰动导致末端执行器难以精确跟踪目标位姿。

Method: ResGAC以GAC在SE(3)任务空间提供结构化反馈并生成标称手臂关节位置目标；残差RL在共享关节位置动作空间中补偿未建模动态并协调行走与平衡。其左不变几何形式使同一GAC律可跨操作参考系复用，并采用地面附着航向帧保留平面行走、去除骨盆滚转/俯仰/升降，从而减少参考系引起的末端运动。

Result: 在真实Unitree G1上验证：四个站立末端跟踪基准中一致优于SONIC等基线，平移与旋转误差更低；地面附着航向帧显著降低骨盆运动向期望末端位姿的传播；站立插孔任务成功率90%（SONIC为50%），并在下肢运动时实现准确的世界系SE(3)末端位姿跟踪。

Conclusion: 几何导纳控制与残差强化学习相结合的全身控制框架可有效提升人形机器人在动态全身运动中的末端精确跟踪能力。

Abstract: Precise end-effector tracking during humanoid whole-body motion is challenging due to floating-base oscillations, gravity, dynamic coupling, and locomotion-induced disturbances. We propose ResGAC, a whole-body humanoid controller for precise end-effector pose tracking that combines geometric admittance control (GAC) with residual reinforcement learning. GAC provides structured $\SE$ task-space feedback and generates nominal arm joint-position targets, while residual RL compensates for unmodeled dynamics and coordinates locomotion and balance in the shared joint-position action space. The left-invariant geometric formulation allows the same GAC law to be used across manipulation reference frames. This enables the use of a ground-attached heading frame that preserves planar locomotion while removing pelvis roll, pitch, and heave from the manipulation reference, thereby reducing reference-induced end-effector motion during locomotion. ResGAC is validated on a real Unitree G1 humanoid. Across four standing end-effector tracking benchmarks, ResGAC consistently outperforms representative baselines, including SONIC, achieving lower translational and rotational errors. Real-world experiments further demonstrate reduced propagation of pelvis motion to the desired end-effector pose using the proposed ground-attached heading frame. ResGAC achieves $90\%$ success in a standing peg-in-hole task compared with $50\%$ for SONIC, and accurate world-frame $\SE$ end-effector pose tracking during lower-body motion. Experimental videos are included in the supplementary material and are also available on the project website: this https URL .

</details>


### [25] [TMT: Runtime Backdoor Detection for Vision-Language-Action Policies on Unseen Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09462)
*Zirun Zhou,Jingfeng Zhang,HaoChuan Xu,Xizhe Zhang,Elliott Wen,Jing Sun,Hong Jia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TMT，一种面向视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略的运行时后门检测器，结合Token流形与潜在状态转移建模，并探索基于自蒸馏的策略净化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA后门策略在无触发器时保持正常任务性能，触发时产生恶意动作，检测困难：恶意行为由单个看似合理的动作组成，而陌生任务又会带来观测与行为的合理变化，使异常难以区分。

Method: TMT在良性 rollout 上训练，包含两个分支：Token流形分支评估输入 token 结构，潜在动态分支评估相邻层潜在表示的预测误差；当Token流形分支发现可疑 rollout 后，再由潜在偏差确认，并指导后续监控中的状态转移选择。此外，采用自蒸馏进行策略净化：以冻结的后门策略在良性输入上的动作监督学生策略，在配对良性/触发观测上训练，无需独立干净参考策略。

Result: 在事后对比中，与十种基线方法相比，TMT在三种VLA后门攻击下对未见任务的检测性能达到当前最优。

Conclusion: TMT能够有效进行VLA后门运行时检测，并在未见任务上优于传统后门检测、异常检测和失败检测等改造基线；自蒸馏策略净化也为缓解后门提供了可行方向。

Abstract: Backdoored vision-language-action (VLA) policies can preserve benign task performance while producing malicious actions when a trigger appears. Detecting such activation is difficult because malicious behavior can comprise individually plausible actions, while unfamiliar tasks introduce legitimate changes in observations and behavior. We introduce TMT, a runtime backdoor detector based on Token Manifold and latent Transition modeling. Trained on benign rollouts, its two branches assess input-token structure and prediction errors in adjacent-layer latent dynamics. A suspicious rollout identified by the token manifold branch, once confirmed through latent deviations, guides transition selection for subsequent monitoring. We further explore policy purification through self-distillation: a frozen copy of the backdoored policy provides benign-input actions to supervise a student on paired benign and triggered observations, without requiring a separate clean reference policy. For evaluation, we adapt traditional backdoor detectors and repurpose anomaly and failure detection methods as VLA backdoor detectors. In a post-hoc comparison with ten baselines, TMT achieves state-of-the-art backdoor detection performance on unseen tasks across three VLA backdoor attacks. Our project page is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [26] [RobotAPO: Adversarial Physics Preference Optimization for Robotic Manipulation Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09454)
*Kerui Li,Zhe Jing,Chenyi Huang,Xiaofeng Wang,Zheng Zhu,Haoming Cui,Huaibo Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 AgiBot-PhysPref 物理偏好数据集与 RobotAPO 对抗式物理偏好优化框架，在流匹配去噪空间中纠正机器人操作视频的局部物理违规，从而提升下游真实机器人执行成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作视频常被用作具身智能体的视觉规划，但仅优化视觉合理性会忽略真实交互中脆弱的物理流形。交互边界的轻微物理违规，如穿模或物体提前运动，会使推断的时序与位姿失效。标准监督微调缺乏直接惩罚这些局部失败的压力。

Method: 构建 AgiBot-PhysPref：严格筛选的 10,000 样本偏好数据集，隔离条件匹配的物理违规，将生成器自身失败分布转化为物理一致性信号。提出 RobotAPO：在连续流匹配去噪空间中进行对抗式物理偏好优化。为防策略仅记忆静态筛选失败，引入轻量对抗反事实提议器，学习条件相关的、偏向物理失败方向的去噪方向，促使模型探索并更好遵守物理交互边界。推理时保持纯 prompt-and-reference 接口，无需外部结构条件。

Result: 综合评估表明，显式纠正局部物理违规可提升生成视频驱动的下游机器人执行。在留出 AgiBot 条件下，RobotAPO 在物理一致性上比最强受控内部基线提升 6.8% hard score 与 10.0% soft score。在真实机器人回放中，物理一致性增益转化为任务成功率相对提升 37.4%。

Conclusion: 通过将生成器物理失败分布转化为偏好优化信号，并在去噪空间中用对抗反事实提议器引导，RobotAPO 能有效纠正局部物理违规，提升生成视频的物理一致性，并显著改善真实机器人任务成功率。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation videos are increasingly used as visual plans for embodied agents, but optimizing purely for visual plausibility often fails to capture the fragile physical manifold of real-world interactions. Even minor physics-violating errors at the interaction boundary, such as interpenetration or premature object motion, can completely invalidate the inferred timing and pose needed for downstream execution. Because standard supervised fine-tuning lacks the direct pressure to penalize these localized failures, we introduce AgiBot-PhysPref. This rigorously curated 10,000-sample preference dataset isolates condition-matched physics violations, turning the generator's own failure distribution into a foundational signal for physical consistency. Building upon this, we propose RobotAPO, an adversarial physics preference optimization framework operating in the continuous flow-matching denoising space. To prevent the policy from merely memorizing static curated failures, RobotAPO employs a lightweight adversarial counterfactual proposer that learns a condition-dependent, physical-failure-biased direction in denoising space. This encourages the model to explore and better respect the physical interaction boundary, all while maintaining a pure prompt-and-reference inference interface without requiring external structural conditioning. Comprehensive evaluations demonstrate that explicitly correcting these localized physics violations improves downstream robot execution from generated videos. On held-out AgiBot conditions, RobotAPO outperforms the strongest controlled internal baseline in physical consistency by 6.8% hard score and 10.0% soft score. Crucially, in real-robot replay, it translates these physical-consistency gains into a 37.4% relative improvement in task success over the strongest controlled internal baseline.

</details>


### [27] [TempoBridge: Language-Guided Tempo Control for Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09451)
*Yeonseo Lee,Hyosup Shin,Guebin Hwang,Sungho Jo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TempoBridge 是一种轻量框架，利用冻结的 VLA 表征，根据指令中的节奏线索在任务各阶段调节机器人动作，实现语言条件化的执行速度控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型能理解任务目标，但对如何执行任务（如快速或慢速）控制有限；且不希望依赖额外的带节奏条件机器人演示或针对节奏微调基础策略。

Method: TempoBridge 从上下文 VLM 表征中提取节奏线索，通过因果相位路由器将其与任务进度对齐，并在执行过程中调制标称运动命令；无需额外节奏条件演示或节奏专用基础策略微调。

Result: 在 LIBERO 任务上，TempoBridge 将标准节奏指令下的 Tempo Success Rate 从 52.6% 提升至 89.7%，同时保持高任务成功率；无节奏线索时性能接近基线，并能零训练泛化到未见节奏表达；真实机器人实验也展示了语言条件化节奏调制。

Conclusion: TempoBridge 以轻量方式有效实现了由语言指令控制的执行节奏调节，在保持任务成功率的同时提升节奏遵循能力，并具备泛化到新节奏表达和真实操作场景的潜力。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are effective at understanding what task to perform, but provide limited control over how it should be executed, such as moving quickly or slowly. We introduce TempoBridge, a lightweight framework that uses frozen VLA representations to modulate actions according to tempo cues in the instruction at each task phase, without additional tempo-conditioned robot demonstrations or tempo-specific base-policy fine-tuning. TempoBridge extracts tempo cues from contextual VLM representations, aligns them with task progress through a causal phase router, and modulates nominal motion commands during execution. Across LIBERO tasks, TempoBridge improves Tempo Success Rate from 52.6% to 89.7% under canonical tempo instructions while retaining high task success. It also preserves near-baseline performance when no tempo cue is present and generalizes to unseen tempo expressions without additional training. Experiments on a physical robot further demonstrate language-conditioned tempo modulation in real-world manipulation.

</details>


### [28] [IVG-UAV: An Intelligent Voice-Guided UAV System for Autonomous Ripe Fruit Harvesting with Vision-Based Classification and Adaptive Path Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09398)
*Dinh Trung Duong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出并仿真验证了一套面向热带大规模种植园水果采摘的语音控制无人机系统，集成Whisper+LLM语音识别、视觉成熟度分类和自适应路径规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热带地区农业采摘高度依赖人工，大规模农场面临显著劳动力成本和物流复杂性。

Method: 构建统一框架：使用Whisper与LLM进行语音识别/理解，基于计算机视觉进行果实成熟度分类，并采用自适应路径规划；在Gazebo仿真环境中对系统建模与验证。

Result: 在Gazebo受控农业场景中完成建模与仿真验证，用于评估系统性能；摘要未提供定量指标或田间试验结果。

Conclusion: 该方案展示了语音控制无人机自动化采摘的可行性，为热带大规模种植园降低人工依赖和物流复杂度提供了仿真验证基础，后续需真实场景验证。

Abstract: In tropical regions, their agricultural sectors remain highly dependent on manual labor for fruit harvesting. On large-scale farms, this dependency often results in significant labor cost and logistic complexities. This project presents the development and simulation of a voice-controlled Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) system designed to automate harvesting tasks in extensive plantations. The proposed system integrates speech recognition using Whisper [1] and LLM, computer vision-based ripeness classification, and adaptive path planning within a unified framework. The entire system is modeled and validated in a Gazebo simulation environment, allowing performance evaluation under controlled agricultural scenarios

</details>


### [29] [Immiscible Diffusion Policy: Preserving Multimodal Robot Actions through Label-Free Noise Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09369)
*Xiao Zhang,Yuxin Chen,Zhixuan Liang,Guojian Zhan,Chenran Li,Chenfeng Xu,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Yiheng Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文发现扩散策略常将多模态动作分布坍缩为单一模态；提出 Immiscible Diffusion Policy，通过训练时动作-噪声分配保持噪声到动作路径的相对可分性，在不改架构与推理的前提下提升多模态保持能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散策略原被期望恢复多模态动作分布，但即使数据集模态平衡且批内严格对称，也常坍缩为单一模态。作者分析认为独立动作-噪声配对会加剧扩散路径的混合与交叉，导致去噪响应被平均化并抑制模态特定行为；该问题在动作空间密集且低维的机器人规划中尤为严重。

Method: 提出 Immiscible Diffusion Policy，一种无标签、仅训练时附加的扩散策略增强方法。其核心是利用动作-噪声分配，使噪声到动作的路径保持相对独立与可分，从而减少路径混合与交叉，且不修改策略架构或推理过程。

Result: 在五个模拟和两个真实世界人形机器人操作任务上，覆盖状态、RGB 和点云观测，方法显著提升动作模态保持能力并维持较强任务性能。在三个双模态任务中，将非主导模态比例提升 6.0x-14.6x；在两个四模态任务中，恢复了原始策略 rollout 中完全缺失的示范模态。

Conclusion: Immiscible Diffusion Policy 是一种简单且鲁棒的方法，可在通用机器人学习任务中有效保持动作多模态性，缓解扩散策略的模态坍塌问题。

Abstract: When diffusion policies were first introduced, they were expected to recover multi-modal action distributions. However, we find this expectation does not always hold, as diffusion policies often collapse to a single modality even when we guarantee the balance of dataset modalities and exact within-batch symmetry. Our analysis indicates that independent action-noise pairing contributes to this failure by increasing mixing and crossing among diffusion paths, which can produce averaged denoising responses and suppress modality-specific behavior. This issue is especially severe in robot planning, where action spaces are dense and low-dimensional, significantly increasing such mixing and crossing. To alleviate this problem, we propose Immiscible Diffusion Policy, a label-free training-time add-on to diffusion policy that uses action-noise assignment to preserve relatively distinct noise-to-action routes without modifying the policy architecture or inference procedure. Across five simulated and two real-world humanoid manipulation tasks spanning state, RGB, and point-cloud observations, our method significantly improves the policy's preservation of action modalities while maintaining strong task performance. It increases the proportion of the non-dominant modality by 6.0x-14.6x across three two-modality tasks and recovers demonstrated modalities that are entirely absent from vanilla policy rollouts on both four-modality tasks. These results demonstrate that Immiscible Diffusion Policy provides a simple yet robust approach to preserving action multi-modality in general robot learning tasks.

</details>


### [30] [COOL: Curiosity-Driven Object Ownership Learning for Personalized Robotic Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09358)
*Samira Huber,Ruben Hammele,Sören Pirk*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出COOL机器人框架，通过日常观察自主学习物体归属并维护长期空间记忆，利用好奇心驱动数据采集更新记忆，实现归属条件导航与任务执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人需在日常生活提供个性化服务，必须理解自然语言命令并推理物体实例、人、位置和所有权；但所有权通常无显式标注，需从长期人-物交互行为证据中推断。

Method: COOL框架自主从日常观察学习物体所有权，并维护长期空间记忆；采用基于智能体的好奇心驱动数据收集策略，引导机器人前往最有希望获取信息的位置并刷新过时观察。

Result: 离线实验、消融研究和真实世界评估表明，COOL能从真实交互中推断所有权关系，并利用该知识进行归属条件导航和任务执行。

Conclusion: COOL验证了基于长期行为证据自主推断物体所有权并维持环境记忆的可行性，可支持所有权感知的个性化机器人服务。

Abstract: Robots are increasingly expected to provide personalized services in everyday environments. To do so, they must ground natural-language commands such as "Where is my backpack?" or "Find my bottle" and execute them by reasoning about object instances, people, locations, and ownership. This is challenging because ownership is rarely labeled explicitly and must be inferred from long-term, behavioral evidence of human-object interactions. To address this, we present COOL, a novel robotic framework for autonomously learning object ownership from everyday observations and maintaining a long-term spatial memory of its environment. To keep its memory current, COOL uses an agent-based curiosity-driven data collection strategy that guides the robot toward the most promising locations to gain information and refresh stale observations. Offline experiments, ablation studies, and real-world evaluations show that COOL can infer ownership relations from real-world interactions and use this knowledge for ownership-conditioned navigation and task execution.

</details>


### [31] [Learning Unknown Constraints without Unsafe Data via Optimality and Counterfactual Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09350)
*Zhouyu Zhang,Chih-Yuan Chiu,Glen Chou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Counterfactual KKT（CF-KKT）约束学习框架，利用学习到的可微动力学与局部最优示范，在无需已知动力学和额外高风险探索的情况下恢复未知约束，兼顾 CIOC 的数据效率/安全性与 ICRL 的灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从示范学习约束时，现有方法分为两类：CIOC 利用 KKT 等最优性条件，但通常假设已知动力学且约束表示结构化；ICRL 可处理复杂未知约束与未知转移动力学，却常需大量在线探索，易发生不安全约束违反。因此需要一种既数据高效、安全，又能处理未知动力学与复杂约束的方法。

Method: 提出 CF-KKT：首先用局部学习的可微动力学模型，在示范上施加 KKT 启发的最优性条件；其次利用学习动力学在示范附近生成可提升奖励的反事实行为，揭示在无未知约束时更优的行为，从而构造合成不可行数据。当约束参数化已知时，同一学习动力学框架支持基于 CIOC 的直接参数恢复，并分析其对动力学误设的敏感性。

Result: 在高维机器人控制任务上，该方法学习到神经约束表示，相较最先进的离线 ICRL 基线，在安全性和数据效率上均有提升。

Conclusion: CF-KKT 结合了 CIOC 的数据效率与安全优势以及 ICRL 的灵活性，无需已知动力学或额外风险探索即可恢复未知约束，并在高维控制任务中优于离线 ICRL 基线。

Abstract: Learning from demonstrations (LfD) provides a framework for inferring unknown constraints from locally optimal, constraint-satisfying expert behavior. Existing approaches largely fall into two paradigms, constrained inverse optimal control (CIOC) and inverse constrained reinforcement learning (ICRL). CIOC exploits optimality conditions such as the Karush--Kuhn--Tucker (KKT) conditions but typically assumes known dynamics and structured constraint representations. Meanwhile, ICRL accommodates complex unknown constraints and unknown transition dynamics but often requires extensive online exploration, during which unsafe constraint violations may occur. In this work, we introduce Counterfactual KKT (CF-KKT), a constraint learning framework that leverages learned dynamics and locally optimal demonstrations to recover unknown constraints without requiring known dynamics or additional risky exploration, thereby combining the data efficiency and safety advantages of CIOC with the flexibility of ICRL. First, we use a locally learned differentiable dynamics model to impose KKT-inspired optimality conditions directly on the demonstrations. Second, we use the learned dynamics to generate reward-improving counterfactual behaviors near the demonstrations, revealing behaviors that would be preferable in the absence of the unknown constraint and thus providing synthetic infeasible data. When the constraint parameterization is known, the same learned-dynamics framework enables direct CIOC-based parameter recovery, and we characterize its sensitivity to dynamics misspecification. Across high-dimensional robotic control tasks, our approach learns neural constraint representations with improved safety and data efficiency relative to state-of-the-art offline ICRL baselines.

</details>


### [32] [MCFR: A Mask-Guided Coarse-to-Fine Regression Framework for Robust Multi-Variant Board-to-Board Connector Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09327)
*Guanghui Shen,Song Wang,Dan Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MCFR——掩码引导的由粗到细回归框架，用于多型号板对板（BTB）连接器自动插接；通过目标感知掩码先验与显式光度细化抑制背景干扰，提升跨型号泛化与部署鲁棒性，真实手机装配平均插接成功率达99.25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3C制造中BTB连接器自动插接需同时满足高视觉精度与强部署鲁棒性。多型号连接器在形态与外观上差异显著，导致稳定的跨型号泛化困难；同时固定视角检测设置下训练与部署不匹配会引入背景伪相关，降低真实场景性能。

Method: 提出MCFR（Mask-Guided Coarse-to-Fine Regression）框架，面向多型号BTB连接器装配。方法引入目标感知掩码先验（object-aware mask prior）并进行显式光度细化（photometric refinement），以抑制背景干扰，提高对齐精度与实际部署鲁棒性。

Result: 在自建多型号数据集、BTB批量插接试验台及真实智能手机装配任务上，MCFR持续优于代表性基线，并在真实场景中取得平均99.25%的插接成功率。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，MCFR对多型号BTB连接器自动装配具有有效性与实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Automated insertion of board-to-board (BTB) connectors in 3C manufacturing requires both high visual accuracy and strong deployment robustness. This problem remains challenging because multi-variant connectors exhibit significant morphological and appearance variations, making stable cross-variant generalization difficult, while the mismatch between training and deployment under fixed-view inspection settings induces background spurious correlation and degrades real-world performance. To address these issues, this paper proposes MCFR, a Mask-Guided Coarse-to-Fine Regression framework for multi-variant BTB connector assembly. By introducing an object-aware mask prior and explicit photometric refinement, the proposed method suppresses background interference and improves alignment accuracy and robustness in practical deployment. Experiments on a self-constructed multi-variant dataset, a BTB batch insertion testbed, and a real smartphone assembly task show that MCFR consistently outperforms representative baselines and achieves an average real-world insertion success rate of 99.25%. These results demonstrate the effectiveness and practical potential of MCFR for automated assembly of multi-variant BTB connectors.

</details>

*Kentaro Uno,Warley F. R. Ribeiro,Yusuke Koizumi,Keigo Haji,Koki Kurihara,William Jones,Kazuya Yoshida*

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向腿式攀爬机器人的开源MATLAB仿真与分析平台，可构建任意浮动基多关节机器人并模拟其在任意环境中的行走与攀爬。
