  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为腿式攀爬机器人研究提供专用仿真与分析工具，支持机器人设计与复杂攀爬环境验证。

Method: 采用浮动基 articulated multi-body 建模任意腿式机器人，使用刚体动力学引擎进行仿真；环境主要变量包括倾角、重力和地面刚度，并支持导入点云作为地形图。

Result: 介绍了仿真器结构、计算流程，并给出四足机器人壁面抓附与陡坡攀爬的代表性仿真示例。

Conclusion: 该开源平台能够支持腿式攀爬机器人在任意环境中的设计、仿真与分析，并通过典型攀爬场景示例验证其适用性。

Abstract: This paper presents an open-sourced MATLAB simulation and analysis platform dedicated to legged climbing robots. This simulator enables the design of any limbed robotic system as an articulated multi-body with a floating base and simulates it walking and climbing in an arbitrary environment. The main variable environmental parameters are inclination, gravity, and ground stiffness, and any point cloud can be installed as the terrain map. Furthermore, the simulator employs a rigid body dynamics engine. This paper first describes the simulator structure, and the computational flow and next presents the representative simulation examples where quadrupedal robots assumed gripping on the wall or climbing on the steep slope.

</details>


### [34] [Predicted Futures Are Not Enough: Learning Executable Goals for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09309)
*Tzu-Yu Chuang,Ching-Hsiang Chang,Yi-Hsiu Lee,Yi-Ting Chen,Min Sun,YuanFu Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出实体级目标读出（Entity-Level Goal Readout），将3D轨迹世界模型预测转化为SE(3)紧凑目标，并由共享Pose-Native Executor闭环执行；在五类操作任务上平均成功率79.69%，Franka零样本部署也取得较好结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式世界模型能预测操作场景向任务目标演化，但这些未来预测并不直接给出控制所需的紧凑任务变量；仅以未来预测为监督时，终端目标精度并非显式学习目标，即使具备几何恢复能力。

Method: 将物体中心位姿预测与基于观测深度的平移相结合，把可执行终端目标显式输出为SE(3)紧凑目标；共享Pose-Native Executor利用该固定目标和在线物体位姿反馈进行闭环控制，执行时无需重跑世界模型。

Result: 在五项操作任务上平均成功率为79.69%；目标诊断直接测量终端目标精度，受控平移扰动刻画目标误差下的执行退化。Franka机械臂零样本部署在标称StackCube上成功率73.33%，有干扰物时66.67%，PickPlate上75.00%（目标在策略训练中未见过）。

Conclusion: 结果表明，应将预测到执行的接口视为世界模型规划中显式学习的组件，而非控制流程中的附带后处理。

Abstract: Generative world models provide rich predictions of how manipulation scenes may evolve toward task objectives, yet those futures do not directly expose the compact task variables required by control. When training supervises future prediction alone, terminal goal accuracy is not an explicit learning objective, even when geometric recovery is available. We present Entity-Level Goal Readout, a learned prediction-to-execution interface that makes the executable terminal goal an explicit output of a 3D trace world model. It combines object-centric pose prediction with translation grounded in observed depth to produce a compact goal in SE(3). A shared Pose-Native Executor consumes this fixed goal with online object-pose feedback for closed-loop control without rerunning the world model. Across five manipulation tasks, the pipeline achieves a mean success rate of 79.69%. Goal diagnostics directly measure terminal goal accuracy, while controlled translation perturbations characterize how execution degrades under goal error. Zero-shot deployment on a Franka arm achieves 73.33% success on nominal StackCube, 66.67% with distractors, and 75.00% on PickPlate with a target unseen during policy training. These results support treating the prediction-to-execution interface as an explicit learned component of world-model planning rather than incidental post-processing in the control pipeline itself. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [35] [Adaptive Risk-Certified Event-Triggered Replanning for Dynamic Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09302)
*Richie R. Suganda,Bin Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CERT-Replan 通过在线校准障碍预测残差，将校准后的屏障风险作为提前预警信号，触发有限时域MPC重规划，从而在非平稳预测不确定性下提升动态环境导航安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有控制屏障函数安全滤波器只能拒绝当前即时不安全控制，难以为“当前有限时域规划模式是否正随预测不确定性演化而变得不安全”提供指导，可能导致反复修正同一标称计划。

Method: 提出 Conformal Event-Triggered Risk-Certified Replanning（CERT-Replan）。该方法在线校准按预测时域索引的障碍预测残差，并用所得不确定性半径评估动态障碍安全裕度；一步安全滤波器保护下一应用控制；时域级风险监控器评估当前MPC rollout上预测屏障违反损失的上尾CVaR。当风险超过分配预算时，拒绝当前规划模式，转而选择更低风险替代方案，如不同速度剖面、走廊或同伦类，而非重复修正同一标称计划。

Result: 在非平稳基准中，CERT-Replan 相比仅安全滤波器基线碰撞减少83.3%，相比简单重规划触发减少77.8%，平均安全滤波干预减少32.4%；结合 Trajectron++ 实现96%无碰撞运行；硬件实验与机载运行时分析表明计算可行。

Conclusion: CERT-Replan 将共形校准、事件触发重规划与风险认证安全监控结合，在非平稳预测不确定性下显著提升碰撞规避能力并减少安全滤波干预，同时具备实际部署的计算可行性。

Abstract: Safe navigation in dynamic environments requires robots to plan under obstacle predictions whose errors are uncertain, non-stationary, and can induce rare but safety-critical failures. Existing control-barrier-function safety filters can reject immediately unsafe controls, but they provide little guidance on when the current finite-horizon planning mode itself is becoming unsafe as prediction uncertainty evolves. We propose Conformal Event-Triggered Risk-Certified Replanning (\emph{CERT-Replan}), a framework that uses calibrated barrier risk as an early-warning signal for replanning. CERT-Replan calibrates horizon-indexed obstacle-prediction residuals online and uses the resulting uncertainty radii to evaluate dynamic-obstacle safety margins. A one-step safety filter protects the next applied control, while a horizon-level risk monitor evaluates the upper-tail CVaR of predicted barrier-violation losses along the current MPC rollout. When this risk exceeds an allocated budget, CERT-Replan rejects the current planning mode and selects a lower-risk alternative, such as a different speed profile, corridor, or homotopy class, rather than repeatedly correcting the same nominal plan. In a non-stationary benchmark, CERT-Replan achieves an \(83.3\%\) collision reduction relative to the safety-filter-only baseline \textcolor{black}{and a \(77.8\%\) reduction relative to simple replanning triggers}, while reducing average safety-filter intervention by \(32.4\%\). \textcolor{black}{With Trajectron++, CERT-Replan achieves \(96\%\) collision-free operation. Hardware experiments and onboard runtime profiling demonstrate computational feasibility.}

</details>


### [36] [RoboPrompt: Intuitive Robot Policy Steering with Sparse Human Input](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10534)
*Yanwen Zou,Chenyang Shi,Guoxuan Xu,Wenye Yu,Wendi Chen,Ye Pan,Cewu Lu,Chuan Wen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboPrompt 是一种通用、轻量的机器人策略引导系统，通过稀疏直观输入（轨迹、目标点、粗略方向指令）在不修改基础策略架构或微调的情况下实现策略导向，并可结合 DAgger 实现在线策略改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习得到的端到端机器人策略受限于数据多样性，难以零样本可靠部署；共享自主方法依赖专用硬件与操作员训练，难以规模化；将人类引导作为策略额外输入的方法通常需改动架构并专门训练可导向性，通用性受限。

Method: RoboPrompt 将人类意图翻译与底层策略解耦：可复用模块把人类引导转换为动作草稿，再通过基础策略的扩散或流匹配动力学在噪声空间中细化动作生成，从而在不修改基础策略架构、不针对可导向性微调的前提下平衡人类意图与策略先验。

Result: 在 Diffusion Policy、π0.5 和 FastWAM 上均实现有效引导；利用引导 rollout 经 DAgger 在线改进，2-3 轮迭代后，π0.5 在三个任务上平均成功率提升 15.5%，三种策略在 Insert Bread 任务上平均提升 21.3%；平均人工干预次数分别下降 44.0%（2.86→1.60）和 81.9%（2.60→0.47）。

Conclusion: RoboPrompt 提供了一种无需架构修改或专门训练的通用轻量策略引导方案，能有效融合人类意图与策略先验，并通过在线迭代显著提升成功率、降低人工干预。

Abstract: End-to-end robot policies trained through imitation learning remain constrained by limited data diversity, making reliable zero-shot deployment in real-world settings challenging. Shared-autonomy methods enable human correction through teleoperation, but specialized hardware and operator training hinder deployment at scale. Other approaches incorporate human guidance as additional policy inputs, often requiring architectural changes and dedicated training for steerability, which limits their applicability across policies. We present RoboPrompt, a general-purpose, lightweight robot policy steering system that enables users to guide policy behavior through intuitive, sparse inputs, including drawn traces, target points, and coarse directional instructions. RoboPrompt decouples human-intention translation from the underlying policy: a reusable module converts human guidance into action drafts, which are refined through the diffusion or flow-matching dynamics of the base policy. By controlling action generation in noise space, RoboPrompt balances human intent with the policy prior without modifying the base policy architecture or fine-tuning it for steerability. Experiments demonstrate effective steering across Diffusion Policy, $\pi_{0.5}$, and FastWAM. We further use steered rollouts for online policy improvement through DAgger. After 2-3 rounds of iteration, average success rates increase by 15.5\% for $\pi_{0.5}$ across three tasks and by 21.3\% across three policies(Diffusion Policy, $\pi_{0.5}$, FastWAM) on the Insert Bread task, while average human intervention counts decrease by 44.0\% (2.86 to 1.60) and 81.9\% (2.60 to 0.47), respectively.

</details>


### [37] [Long-WAM: Scaling the Context of World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10528)
*Wei Huang,Bohan Zhang,Chenzhi Liu,Isabella Liu,Shuai Yang,Weian Mao,Luozhou Wang,Yicheng Xiao,Weifeng Lin,Qixin Hu,Bryan Chu,Sifei Liu,Linxi Fan,Xiaojuan Qi,Song Han,Yukang Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Long-WAM 是一个面向实时机器人控制的模型-系统框架，通过自回归视频预训练和系统优化扩展因果世界-动作模型的上下文，使长视觉历史真正提升操作性能并可实时部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时控制需要足够视觉历史推断运动与任务进度，但逐帧处理会延迟动作；核心问题是长历史能否在实时约束下被有效利用，以及其收益是否取决于视频基础模型的预训练方式。

Method: 先在机器人视频与第一人称视频上进行无动作标签的因果预测/自回归预训练，再在世界-动作适配中保留历史到未来的结构；结合流式观测编码、异步执行和硬件专属加速实现部署。

Result: 在 RoboCasa GR-1 上，上下文从0.0增至19.2秒使成功率从63.3%升至78.7%，而双向预训练初始化无净增益；机器人域AR预训练进一步提升GR-1和LIBERO-Long峰值成功率。方法在LIBERO-Long、RoboTwin 2.0、DOMINO上优于对比方法；RTX 5090上每动作块含未来视频潜变量预测耗时107.4 ms，可在RTX 5090、DGX Spark、Jetson AGX Thor部署；Unitree G1和YAM上支持动态长时程操作，动态叠杯95%成功，Pi0.5与Fast-WAM在20次试验中均未成功。

Conclusion: 历史可获取不等于被有效利用，自回归预训练是长上下文收益的关键；Long-WAM作为记忆信息执行器可在实时约束下实现长历史世界-动作建模，并与高层规划互补完成复合任务。

Abstract: Real-time robot control demands enough visual history to infer motion and task progress, but processing that history can delay action. We present Long-WAM, a model-system framework for scaling the context of causal world-action models under real-time control constraints. Our central finding is that access to history is not the same as using it: longer histories pay off far more when the video foundation is pretrained autoregressively (AR). We first learn causal prediction from robot and egocentric videos without action labels, then preserve this history-to-future structure during world-action adaptation. On RoboCasa GR-1, increasing context from 0.0 to 19.2 seconds raises success from 63.3% to 78.7%, whereas a bidirectionally pretrained initialization shows no net gain; robot-domain AR pretraining further raises peak success on GR-1 and LIBERO-Long. Long-WAM also achieves the best results among compared methods on LIBERO-Long, RoboTwin 2.0, and DOMINO. Streaming observation encoding, asynchronous execution, and hardware-specific acceleration enable deployment on RTX 5090, DGX Spark, and Jetson AGX Thor without dropping future prediction; on RTX 5090, each action chunk, including future-video latent prediction, takes 107.4 ms. Real-time deployment on Unitree G1 and YAM supports dynamic and long-horizon manipulation, including 95% success on dynamic cup stacking, where Pi0.5 and Fast-WAM succeed in none of 20 trials. As a memory-informed executor, Long-WAM also complements higher-level planning in composite tasks.

</details>


### [38] [Rephrase Before You Act: Characterizing and Mitigating Language Sensitivity in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10526)
*Mikey Watts,Yuchen Cui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）对指令措辞高度敏感，且未继承底层视觉-语言模型的语言鲁棒性。作者用统计检验和oracle短语搜索刻画该敏感性，并通过大语言模型从少量训练任务中蒸馏改写规则，在部署时对指令做一次重写，从而在不重训练策略的情况下提升冻结VLA在分布外任务上的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA虽建立在VLM之上，却对指令措辞异常敏感，一个词的改动即可造成数十点性能波动；需要系统表征并缓解这一问题，且希望不修改策略、不重训练、不做逐步验证。

Method: 以统计检验的单编辑波动和oracle短语搜索量化敏感性；对少量训练任务的多种措辞打分，用大语言模型将证据蒸馏为10至20条改写规则；部署时按这些规则对每条输入指令重写一次。方法无需重训练或逐步验证，可零样本用于未见任务与指令。

Result: π0.5在LIBERO炉灶任务上，"switch on the stove"成功率为100%，而"switch on the hot plate"仅2%；用复述增强微调的π0检查点仍有最高61点的波动。oracle短语搜索表明，仅靠措辞几乎可弥合分布内与分布外任务间21点的差距。改写规则使冻结π0在12个留出任务上相对提升16%至27%，增益集中在分布外任务；该流程在π0.5与LIBERO上复现，将微调内成功率从93.6%提升至97.8%。

Conclusion: VLA的指令措辞敏感性是系统性的，可被显式规则捕获；部署时一次指令重写即可有效缓解，且零样本适用于未见任务，无需重训练或逐步验证。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models (VLAs) are strikingly sensitive to instruction phrasing and do not inherit the language robustness of the vision-language models they are built on. A one-word edit can move success by tens of points: $\pi_{0.5}$ turns on a LIBERO stove 100% of the time for "switch on the stove" and 2% for "switch on the hot plate", and a $\pi_0$ checkpoint finetuned with rephrase augmentation still shows swings of up to 61 points. We characterize this sensitivity with statistically tested single-edit swings and an oracle phrase search, which shows that phrasing alone nearly closes the 21-point gap between in-distribution and out-of-distribution tasks. We then reduce it without modifying the policy. Because the sensitivity is systematic, it can be expressed as explicit rules: we score many phrasings of a few training tasks, have a large language model distill the evidence into ten to twenty rephrasing rules, and at deployment rewrite each incoming instruction once under these rules. The rules improve the frozen $\pi_0$ by 16 to 27% relative on twelve held-out tasks across adversarial, VLM-generated, and human-generated phrasings, with gains concentrated on out-of-distribution tasks. The pipeline replicates on $\pi_{0.5}$ and LIBERO, lifting in-finetune success from 93.6% to 97.8%. The method requires no retraining and no per-step verification, and applies zero-shot to unseen tasks and instructions. Project website: this https URL

</details>


### [39] [Factorized Tactile Representation and Control for Sim-to-Real Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10510)
*Siqi Shang,Bianca Aumann,Tye Brady,Joshua Migdal,Taskin Padir*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种因子化触觉表征与控制框架，将接触响应分解为接触几何、力分布和时间变化，并通过触觉门控策略实现无需重训练的跨掩码控制，从而提升触觉sim-to-real迁移与接触丰富操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉sim-to-real学习需弥合仿真接触与特定传感器响应之间的差异，同时保留控制所需信息；现有方法难以分别评估不同触觉表征的效用与迁移可靠性。

Method: 将法向力与接触斑映射为可从传感器读数恢复的有效接触响应，并分解为接触几何、力分布和时间接触变化；对每种表征采用特定编码与随机化；设计Tactile Gated Policy分别保留这些表征进行控制，并可适配所有掩码配置而无需重训练。

Result: 实现小于1 mm的接触定位、在未见几何上1.69 N的力跟踪误差，以及在真实世界对抗性插孔任务中相较未因子化响应提升35%；不同触觉表征对不同交互任务各有优势。

Conclusion: 因子化触觉表征能独立评估各类触觉信息的效用与迁移可靠性，并在仿真与真实世界的接触丰富任务中有效提升控制与迁移性能。

Abstract: Tactile sim-to-real learning must bridge simulated contact and device-specific sensor responses while preserving information needed for control. We propose a factorized tactile representation and control framework that maps normal force and contact patch to an effective contact response recoverable from sensor readings. The response is separated into contact geometry, force distribution, and temporal contact change, with representation-specific encoding and randomization. A Tactile Gated Policy preserves these representations separately through control and operates over all mask configurations without retraining. We evaluate the approach through response reconstruction, spatial alignment, force regulation, and contact-rich adversarial peg insertion in simulation and the real world, enabling the utility and transfer reliability of different tactile representations to be assessed independently. The approach achieves <1 mm contact localization, 1.69 N force-tracking error on unseen geometries, and a 35% improvement in real-world adversarial peg insertion over the unfactorized response, with different tactile representations benefiting different interactions.

</details>


### [40] [HuMBLE: Human Motion-Driven Behavior Learning for Embodied Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10489)
*Mike Zhang,Dongho Kang,Kevin Bergamin,Nicola Burger,Robin Deits,Jonathan Foster,Bilal Hammoud,Katie Hughes,Francesco Iacobelli,Twan Koolen,M. Eva Mungai,Zach Nobles,Shane Rozen-Levy,Jean Pierre Sleiman,Fangzhou Yu,Yunbo Zhang,Alfred Rizzi,Jessica Hodgins,Scott Kuindersma,Yeuhi Abe,Sylvain Bertrand,Farbod Farshidian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种学习框架，从人类运动数据中合成实时可转向、鲁棒且仿生的类人机器人运动策略，在命令跟踪/鲁棒性与人类步态风格之间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有类人运动控制中，面向命令跟踪与鲁棒性的控制器易产生机械步态；而依赖人类运动数据的控制器又难以泛化到数据分布外的命令。因此需要兼顾实时可转向、鲁棒性和仿生性。

Method: 使用自建覆盖多速度与多方向的运动数据集，先通过教师-学生蒸馏学习自然运动先验：用强化学习训练全身参考条件策略，再蒸馏为仅依赖本体感受和平面躯干速度转向命令的轻量先验策略；随后用多任务强化学习微调，结合跟踪任意命令的目标条件任务与跟踪人类数据作为显式风格正则项的参考引导任务，以扩展命令覆盖和鲁棒性。

Result: 在Boston Dynamics Atlas R1、Atlas D1和Unitree G1三款类人机器人上验证。实验显示在真实场景中具有鲁棒性能，包括室内外用户直接控制运动，以及作为分层控制栈中的运动层集成。与不使用人类数据的Tabula Rasa强化学习策略对比及消融实验表明，该框架得到轻量、可部署的策略，能仅由转向命令重建协调的全身行为，保留人类步态特征，同时保持鲁棒和完全可转向。

Conclusion: 该框架有效平衡了命令跟踪、鲁棒性与仿生性，可从人类数据中合成实时可转向、鲁棒且保留人类步态特征的类人运动策略，并具有实际部署与分层控制集成能力。

Abstract: Despite recent advances in humanoid locomotion, controllers optimized for command tracking and robustness tend to produce mechanical gaits, whereas controllers tied to human motion data often fail to generalize to commands outside the data distribution. This work introduces a learning framework that balances these competing objectives to synthesize real-time steerable, robust, and biomimetic locomotion policies from human data. Using an in-house curated locomotion dataset covering diverse speeds and directions, we first learn a natural locomotion prior policy through a teacher-student distillation process. Specifically, we train a full-body reference-conditioned policy with Reinforcement Learning (RL), then distill it into a lightweight prior policy conditioned solely on proprioception and a planar torso-velocity steering command. Next, we fine-tune the prior policy with multi-task RL to expand command coverage and robustness beyond the data distribution, pairing a goal-conditioned task that tracks arbitrary commands with a reference-guided task that tracks the human data as an explicit style regularizer. We validate our framework on three humanoid robots: the Boston Dynamics Atlas R1, Atlas D1, and Unitree G1. Experimental results demonstrate robust performance across real-world scenarios, including direct user-controlled locomotion in indoor and outdoor environments, and integration as the locomotion layer within hierarchical control stacks. Benchmarks against Tabula Rasa RL policies trained without human data and ablation studies confirm that our framework yields a lightweight, deployable policy that reconstructs coordinated whole-body behavior from a steering command, retaining the human gait characteristics while remaining robust and fully steerable.

</details>


### [41] [Agentic RSR: Real-to-Sim-to-Real through Scene Reconstruction and Execution-Grounded Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10479)
*Yihan Li,Yating Feng,Shengjiu Sun,Jianing Chen,Hao Ren,Bowen Yang,Weisheng Xu,Qiwei Wu,Hui Cheng,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Agentic RSR 框架，将场景重建、策略开发与真机执行通过同一操作任务串联，实现从真实到仿真再到真实的迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人工作空间的仿真必须保留任务相关交互，且仿真中开发的策略必须基于真实机器人可获得的观测；但场景重建与策略开发通常被分开处理。

Method: 给定工作空间视频、任务描述和已知机器人模型，智能体恢复度量尺度，利用视觉反馈迭代优化场景，并在 MuJoCo 中检查任务相关交互；编码智能体开发可执行策略，从特权物体位姿逐步过渡到视觉观测和随机化仿真，支持单次调用内交错多观测与动作，并根据执行反馈继续、重试或修订；共享任务级接口将策略和积累经验迁移至真机，结合新观测与安全检查执行。

Result: 在涉及两种机器人的 18 个重建场景中，四视角深度 MAE 均值为 0.1057 m，Lab ΔE76 均值为 11.04，灰度 SSIM 均值为 0.6990；真机实验中，总体任务成功率达到仿真任务成功率的 80%，表明仿真性能在硬件上得到较好保留。

Conclusion: Agentic RSR 通过同一操作任务连接场景重建、策略开发与真机执行，验证了真实到仿真再到真实的可行性与性能保留，代码和重建场景数据将公开。

Abstract: A simulation of a real robot workspace must preserve task-relevant interactions, while policies developed in it must operate on observations available to the real robot. Yet scene reconstruction and policy development are often treated separately. We present Agentic Real-to-Sim-to-Real (Agentic RSR), a framework that links scene reconstruction, policy development, and real-robot execution through the same manipulation task. Given a workspace video, a task description, and a known robot model, an agent recovers metric scale, iteratively refines the scene using visual feedback, and checks task-relevant interactions in MuJoCo. A coding agent then develops an executable policy, progressing from privileged object poses to visual observations and randomized simulation. The policy can interleave multiple observations and actions within one invocation, while the agent uses execution feedback to continue, retry, or revise its approach. A shared task-level interface carries the policy and accumulated experience to the real robot, where fresh observations and safety checks guide execution. Across 18 reconstructed scenes involving two robots, the mean four-view Depth MAE against reference depth estimates is 0.1057 m, the mean Lab $\Delta E_{76}$ is 11.04, and the mean grayscale SSIM is 0.6990. In real-robot experiments, the aggregate task success rate reaches 80% of the simulation task success rate, indicating substantial retention of simulated performance on hardware. Code and reconstructed scene data will be made publicly available.

</details>

*Yang Liu,Colin Jones,Aude Billard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种以释放状态为核心的基于模型的机械臂回旋镖投掷框架，并在6自由度机械臂上首次实现回旋镖返回飞行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能够产生气动升力的物体可显著扩展机器人投掷能力，不再局限于弹道飞行。回旋镖返回飞行对释放速度、姿态和旋转高度敏感，而机械臂难以复现人类投掷的快速动作，因此需要面向机器人约束的系统设计方法。

Method: 建立基于释放状态的模型框架；分阶段辨识回旋镖飞行动力学，预测不同设计变化下的飞行特性；在不确定接触条件下，筛选候选参数对释放旋转的控制强度和一致性；据此为关节速度受限的6自由度机械臂设计投掷运动与回旋镖。

Result: 据作者所知，这是首个由机械臂实现返回回旋镖飞行的系统。实验中回旋镖以51 rad/s（8.1 rev/s）释放，到达距机器人基座2.03 m处，并返回至距基座0.31 m处触地。成功释放与测得的人类投掷存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 机器人无需模仿人类投掷动作也能实现回旋镖返回飞行；基于释放状态的建模与参数筛选方法可有效设计机器人投掷运动和回旋镖。

Abstract: Throwing objects that generate aerodynamic lift can greatly extend robot throwing beyond ballistic flight. A returning boomerang is a challenging example because its flight depends strongly on the release velocity, attitude, and spin, while robotic manipulators cannot readily reproduce the rapid motions used in human throwing. We present a model-based framework for robotic boomerang throwing centered on the release state. We identify the boomerang flight dynamics in stages to predict how flight changes across design variations. To systematically design the robot throwing motion, we screen candidate parameters according to how strongly and consistently they control release spin under uncertain contact conditions. These models are then used to design the throwing motion and boomerang for a 6-DoF manipulator with limited joint speeds. To our knowledge, this is the first robotic manipulator to generate a returning boomerang flight. In the demonstrated returning trial, the boomerang is released at 51 rad/s (8.1 rev/s), reaches 2.03 m from the robot base, and returns to touch down 0.31 m from the base. The successful release differs significantly from the measured human throws, showing that a robot need not imitate human throwing motion to achieve a returning flight. The project page is available at this https URL

### [43] [CMP-IRRT*: A Perception-Assisted Height-Adaptive Planner for Quadruped Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10470)
*Mingfan Zhao,Wendong Mao,Zhongfeng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CMP-IRRT*：一种由Channel Mamba PointNet引导的Informed RRT*高度自适应规划框架，利用顶视RGB感知生成地面相对高度图，使四足机器人能穿越低矮障碍并绕行高障碍，提升2D规划效率与路径质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统2D规划常将障碍物视为二值占用区域，并依赖采样搜索，在有限计算预算下效率较低；同时四足机器人具备跨越低矮障碍的能力，但现有规划流程未充分利用高度信息，难以支持可穿越性感知的规划。

Method: 方法为CMP-IRRT*，即Channel Mamba PointNet引导的Informed RRT*规划器。给定标定后的顶视RGB观测，感知模块估计障碍区域并将深度预测转换为相对地面的高度图；规划器执行高度条件碰撞检测，将高障碍视为阻塞、低障碍视为可穿越，并利用CMP引导将采样偏向有希望区域，同时保留标准自由空间采样与informed采样作为回退。

Result: 在2D规划基准上，CMP-IRRT*相比经典和神经引导基线减少了探索节点数和迭代次数；消融实验支持Mamba引导的贡献。在构建的可穿越性感知场景中，当低障碍可穿越时路径长度最多减少16.3%；Unitree Go2演示进一步展示了可执行的绕行与穿越行为。代码已公开。

Conclusion: 该工作通过感知辅助与高度自适应规划，有效提升了四足机器人在含低矮障碍环境中的规划效率和可穿越能力，并验证了Mamba引导与高度条件碰撞检测的有效性。

Abstract: Quadruped robots can traverse low obstacles, but many 2D planning pipelines still model obstacles as binary occupied regions and rely on sampling-based search that can be inefficient under a limited budget. We propose a perception-assisted height-adaptive planning framework based on CMP-IRRT*, a Channel Mamba PointNet-guided Informed RRT* planner. Given a calibrated top-view RGB observation, the perception module estimates obstacle regions and converts depth predictions into a ground-relative height map. The planner then performs height-conditioned collision checking, treating high obstacles as blocked while allowing low obstacles to be traversed, and uses the CMP guide to bias sampling toward promising regions while retaining standard free-space and informed sampling fallbacks. Experiments on 2D planning benchmarks show that CMP-IRRT* reduces explored nodes and iterations compared with classical and neural-guided baselines, and a controlled ablation supports the contribution of the Mamba-based guide. In constructed traversability-aware scenarios, the proposed planner reduces path length by up to 16.3% when low obstacles are traversable, and a Unitree Go2 demonstration further shows executable bypassing and traversal behaviors. Our code is publicly available at this https URL .

</details>

*Sebin Jung,Maitham F. AL-Sunni,Juan Alvarez-Padilla,Zachary Manchester,Changliu Liu,John M. Dolan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LLA-MPPI：通过GPU批量接触仿真器库选择与近期运动最匹配的模型，并用全身MPPI规划控制，实现无需离线训练的实时腿部机器人全身自适应控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时全身控制器通常依赖固定标称模型，部署动力学变化后性能下降；现有自适应方法要么需要接触动力学难以提供的模型结构，要么需针对每种预期条件离线训练。

Method: 将全身自适应转化为在具有不同物理或结构参数的GPU批量接触仿真器库上进行选择；利用窗口化预测误差选出最能解释近期运动的仿真器；再由全身MPPI规划器通过所选模型优化控制。无需离线训练，所选假设具有物理可解释性。

Result: 在四个仿真任务中达到97.5%成功率，最强基线为74%，使用真实模型的oracle为98.5%。Unitree Go2硬件验证包括：运行中增加负载后行走、一条腿禁用后行走、边推动箱子至目标边实时增加其质量。

Conclusion: LLA-MPPI实现了无需离线训练、可解释且接近oracle性能的实时全身自适应控制，在仿真与硬件上对动力学和结构突变均表现出鲁棒性。

Abstract: Real-time whole-body controllers for legged robots typically plan through a fixed nominal model and degrade when the deployed dynamics change. Adaptive methods typically require a model structure that contact dynamics do not provide, or they need offline training for each anticipated condition. We present Look-back and Look-ahead Adaptive Model Predictive Path Integral control (LLA-MPPI). The method converts whole-body adaptation into selection over a bank of GPU-batched contact simulators with different physical or structural parameters. Windowed prediction errors select the simulator that best explains recent motion. A whole-body MPPI planner optimizes controls through the selected model. The framework requires no offline training, and its selected hypotheses are physically interpretable. Across four simulated tasks, it achieves 97.5% success while the strongest baseline reaches 74% and an oracle with the true model reaches 98.5%. Hardware validation on a Unitree Go2 shows the robot walking under a payload added mid-run, walking after one leg is disabled, and pushing a box to its goal while increasing its mass on the fly. Code, videos, and project details are available at: this https URL

</details>


### [45] [FoldBack: Self-Correcting Masked Generative Policy for Long-Horizon Garment Folding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10462)
*Lipeng Zhuang,Shiyu Fan,Yingdong Ru,Zhuo He,Florent P. Audonnet,Paul Henderson,Gerardo Aragon Camarasa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FoldBack 是一种面向长时程衣物折叠的自校正掩码生成策略，通过推理时决策实现失败抓取/滑落的检测、回滚与重试，无需恢复演示或基策略重训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长轨迹策略在抓取遗漏或滑落后仍可能继续执行，即使衣物未达到目标构型，导致失败累积。

Method: FoldBack 围绕三个推理时决策构建恢复机制：何时细化与验证、如何回滚、何处及如何重试。它将细化与抓取验证对齐到抓放事件；在保留成功抓取的同时使机器人回到可重试的预抓取构型；并选择性地重新生成失败片段及部分未来动作，同时避开此前失败的抓取位置。该工作是首个统一这些决策的可编辑全轨迹策略，能在继续执行前检测、撤销并修复失败交互，且无需恢复演示或基策略重训练。

Result: 在来自六个类别的 33 件真实衣物上，FoldBack 达到 75.2% 最终折叠成功率和 0.837 最终掩码 IoU；最强先前基线分别为 45.7% 和 0.689。

Conclusion: FoldBack 通过可编辑的全轨迹自校正机制显著提升长时程衣物折叠的成功率与掩码精度，并消除了对恢复演示和基策略重训练的需求。

Abstract: We present FoldBack, a self-correcting masked generative policy for long-horizon garment folding. Existing long-trajectory policies may continue after a missed or slipped grasp even when the garment has not reached the intended configuration. We structure FoldBack's recovery mechanisms around three inference-time decisions: when to refine and verify, how to roll back, and where and how to retry. FoldBack aligns refinement and grasp verification with pick-and-place events, returns the robot to a retryable pre-grasp configuration while preserving successful grasps, and selectively regenerates the failed segment and selected future actions while avoiding previous failed grasp locations. To our knowledge, FoldBack is the first editable full-trajectory policy to unify these decisions, enabling failed interactions to be detected, undone, and repaired before execution continues, without recovery demonstrations or base-policy retraining. Across 33 real garments from six categories, FoldBack achieves 75.2% final folding success and 0.837 final-mask IoU, versus 45.7% and 0.689 for the strongest prior baseline.

</details>


### [46] [RFPO: Rectified Flow Policy Optimization for Embodied Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10453)
*Ting Huang,Lisiyu Pan,Haoyu Wang,Zeyu Zhang,Siyuan Qian,Yanjun Li,Yandong Guo,Boxin Shi,Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RFPO，用奖励感知在线Reflow与冻结高斯PPO监督，使流策略可靠地单步欧拉执行，在多种四足与人形机器人上保持接近全步性能并大幅降低推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流策略表达力强但ODE迭代推理开销大；直接减少积分步数会因少步离散化差距导致控制性能严重下降，因为全步训练的策略未显式约束在粗积分下可靠。

Method: 将问题定义为few-step discretization gap；RFPO在在线学习中通过reward-aware online Reflow修正学生诱导的传输路径；冻结的Gaussian PPO控制器在全步和中间积分预算下提供动作空间补充监督；部署策略为单个流学生模型，推理时仅用一次Euler步。

Result: 在Unitree Go2、Boston Dynamics Spot、Unitree H1和Unitree G1上，单步执行保持全步控制性能，单步回报在零初始化和随机初始化下均在其对应64步值的2.4%以内；Go2上单步保留64步奖励的98.5%，机载平均推理延迟从4.39 ms降至0.08 ms，加速54.9倍；真机实验验证稳定单步运动。

Conclusion: RFPO有效弥合少步离散化差距，实现高表达力流策略的可靠单步部署，显著降低推理成本并保持控制性能，具备真实机器人应用潜力。

Abstract: Flow-based policies provide an expressive framework for continuous robot control, but their iterative ODE integration incurs substantial inference cost. Naively reducing the integration budget can severely degrade control, since policies optimized under full-step execution are not explicitly constrained to remain reliable under coarse numerical integration. We refer to this mismatch as the few-step discretization gap. To address this problem, we introduce RFPO, a flow-policy optimization framework for reliable few-step execution. Reward-aware online Reflow rectifies student-induced transport paths during on-policy learning, making the resulting policy more robust to coarse integration. A frozen Gaussian PPO controller supplies complementary action-space supervision at full and intermediate integration budgets, while the deployed policy remains a single flow student executed with one Euler step. Across Unitree Go2, Boston Dynamics Spot, Unitree H1, and Unitree G1, RFPO consistently preserves full-step control performance under one-step execution, with one-step returns remaining within 2.4% of their corresponding 64-step values across both zero and random initialization. On Unitree Go2, one-step execution retains 98.5% of the 64-step reward while reducing onboard mean inference latency from 4.39 ms to 0.08 ms, yielding a 54.9x speedup. Real-robot experiments further validate stable one-step locomotion. Code: this https URL . Website: this https URL .

</details>


### [47] [AirGroundVLN: A Large-Scale Benchmark for Goal-Oriented Air-Ground Collaborative Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10421)
*Zhenxuan Zeng,Qingle Wu,Wei Suo,Maojia Wu,Bairong Zhang,Hangzheng Yu,Peng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出目标导向空地协同视觉语言导航（VLN）基准 AirGroundVLN 与可训练参考框架 AG-CoNAV，以解决空地视角差异和空间可观测性不对称问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标导向 VLN 需要智能体根据自然语言定位并到达目标，而空地协同对广域搜索和精细定位很有价值；但现有大规模、多样化基准不足。核心挑战包括：空中与地面视角差异大，且有用观测随导航推进而失效，难以跨平台、跨时间保持空间一致上下文；空地空间可观测性不对称，地面细节丰富但范围有限，空中范围广但局部粗糙，导致单平台规划不可靠。

Method: 构建 AirGroundVLN 基准，包含 19 个 Unreal Engine 环境中的 10,281 个导航回合和 955 个目标实例，并提供 seen/unseen 划分与空中可见性协议。提出 AG-CoNAV 框架，包含时空锚定协同记忆（SACM）和空中引导区域到局部规划（AGRLP）：SACM 维护并检索跨空地观测的空间一致历史上下文，AGRLP 结合区域级空中引导与地面精细导航。

Result: 大量实验证明 AG-CoNAV 的有效性，并确立 AirGroundVLN 作为未来研究可用的综合基准。

Conclusion: 该工作为空地协同目标导向 VLN 提供了大规模基准和参考框架，有助于推动跨平台空间一致性与协同规划的未来探索。

Abstract: Goal-oriented Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) requires agents to locate and reach targets described in natural language without prescribed routes. Air--ground collaboration is valuable for tasks requiring both wide-area search and fine-grained localization. However, systematic study of goal-oriented air--ground collaborative VLN remains limited by the lack of large-scale, diverse benchmarks and two core challenges: 1) substantial differences between aerial and ground views, together with useful observations becoming unavailable as navigation proceeds, make it difficult to maintain spatially consistent context across platforms and over time; and 2) asymmetric spatial observability makes ground perception locally detailed but spatially limited and aerial perception broad but locally coarse, limiting the reliability of single-platform planning. To address these limitations, we introduce AirGroundVLN, a benchmark containing 10,281 navigation episodes and 955 target instances across 19 Unreal Engine environments, with seen/unseen splits and an aerial-visibility protocol for systematic evaluation. Alongside the benchmark, we propose AG-CoNAV, a trainable reference framework comprising two key components: Spatiotemporally Anchored Collaborative Memory (SACM) and Aerial-Guided Regional-to-Local Planning (AGRLP). SACM maintains and retrieves spatially consistent historical context across aerial and ground observations. Meanwhile, AGRLP combines regional aerial guidance with fine-grained ground navigation. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of AG-CoNAV and establish AirGroundVLN as a comprehensive benchmark for future exploration.

</details>


### [48] [RobotWorld: Benchmarking Multimodal Agents for Robot Use Across Diverse Tasks and Embodiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10409)
*Zhiqin Yang,Chenxin Li,Xiaomeng Hu,Yibin Liu,Weidong Huang,Jiankai Sun,Haitao Li,Zijian Wu,Yuzhi Huang,Fanding Huang,Hanwen Sun,Jiashun Liu,Jingqi Tong,Mingxin Huang,Shaoli Hu,Shijue Huang,Tianyi Bai,Xinyuan Wang,Yunlong Lin,Zhengyang Tang,Zhexin Zhang,Zhuo Chen,Xierui Song,Juntao Dai,Boyuan Chen,Jiaming Ji,Fangneng Zhan,Mengkang Hu,Wei Xue,Yonggang Zhang,Han Hu,Tsung-Yi Ho,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 RobotWorld，一个用于评估通用智能体在物理世界中执行机器人任务的仿真测试平台，包含 84 个跨操作、移动操作、运动、驾驶和空中控制的任务，并通过任务结果与执行轨迹分析揭示智能体的能力迁移与关键缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用智能体已能写代码、用工具并完成复杂数字任务，但这些能力在多大程度上能迁移到物理世界尚不明确。论文希望系统评估这种迁移，并定位阻碍可靠完成物理任务的能力差距。

Method: 构建 RobotWorld 仿真测试床，将指令和观测通过机器人接口转化为物理任务执行；包含 84 个任务，覆盖 manipulation、mobile manipulation、locomotion、driving 和 aerial control，并设置显式交互预算和可执行成功检查。通过分析任务结果与执行轨迹，识别可迁移能力和失败模式。

Result: 智能体能够构建复杂的感知与控制工作流，包括图像分割、相机标定、空间估计和基于动力学的计算，但这些能力不能稳定组合为成功行为。常见失败包括：到达指令位姿却丢失任务相关物体状态、无法纠正无效动作、恢复过晚，以及将未完成任务误判为完成。不同模型表现不同：Astra 在空间和受限接触目标上更常成功，Opus 5.5 在连续平衡和定时交互目标上更常成功。

Conclusion: RobotWorld 既是严格的物理世界智能体评测平台，也提供了对剩余能力差距的实证分析，为训练和设计更可靠的物理世界智能体确立了具体目标。

Abstract: General-purpose agents increasingly write code, use tools, and complete complex digital tasks, raising the question of how far these capabilities carry into the physical world. To investigate this, we introduce RobotWorld, a challenging simulation testbed for robot use: turning instructions and observations into physical task execution through robot interfaces. Its 84 tasks span manipulation, mobile manipulation, locomotion, driving, and aerial control, with explicit interaction budgets and executable success checks. By analysing task outcomes alongside execution traces, we identify both the capabilities that transfer and the gaps that prevent reliable completion. Furthermore, we find that current agents can construct sophisticated perception and control workflows, including image segmentation, camera calibration, spatial estimation, and dynamics-based computation. These capabilities, however, do not consistently compose into successful behaviour: agents lose task-relevant object states despite reaching commanded poses, fail to correct ineffective actions, recover too late, or mistake unfinished tasks for completion. This uneven transfer also differs across models: Astra succeeds more often on spatial and constrained-contact goals, whereas Opus 5.5 succeeds more often on continuous-balance and timed-interaction goals. By linking these outcomes to execution behaviour, RobotWorld provides both a rigorous proving ground and an empirical account of the remaining capability gaps, thereby establishing concrete targets for training and designing more reliable physical-world agents.

</details>


### [49] [RoboQuest: Generalist Physical Agents that Search, Inspect and Test](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10388)
*Liu Renhang,Navonil Majumder,Tej Deep Pala,Soujanya Poria*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboQuest 是一个面向目标导向具身探索的基准，要求智能体通过物理交互主动获取任务相关信息、据此调整动作并自主决定何时完成任务；现有前沿多模态智能体表现很差，最佳仅 23% 成功率，微调策略几乎不成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态基础模型虽可作为通用操作智能体，但在陌生环境中，观测常缺少任务关键信息，例如目标物体位置、未观测属性或陌生工具效果，因此智能体必须主动探索而非仅执行已知技能。

Method: 提出 RoboQuest，包含 10 个移动操作任务，覆盖三类不确定性：搜索、基于操作的检查、交互式测试。通过统一视觉运动接口评估 5 个前沿多模态智能体，并评估在发布的全回合演示上微调的 π0.5 策略。

Result: 最佳智能体仅 23% 回合成功，微调 π0.5 几乎从不成功；在提供隐藏信息时，孤立执行技能测试显示智能体大多能完成所需动作，失败分析仅少数归因于执行。智能体常过早停止探索，在未观察到完成任务所需证据前就做决策；很少预防或修复探索造成的干扰；多数模型仍难以通过试错学习。

Conclusion: 当前多模态智能体在目标导向具身探索上能力有限，RoboQuest 揭示其短板在于主动信息获取、证据驱动决策、探索干扰管理及试错学习，为后续研究提供评测与数据基础。

Abstract: Recent advances in multimodal foundation models have made them capable generalist physical agents for a range of manipulation tasks. However, successful operation in an unfamiliar environment may require an agent to seek task-relevant information through interaction when it is absent from the observations: it may need to determine where a relevant object is, inspect an unobserved property, or discover the effect of an unfamiliar tool. We thus introduce RoboQuest, a benchmark for goal-directed embodied exploration, where agents must actively acquire task-relevant information through physical interaction, use the resulting evidence to adapt subsequent actions, and autonomously decide when to commit to task completion. RoboQuest comprises ten mobile manipulation tasks centered on three forms of uncertainty: search, manipulation-based inspection, and interactive testing. We evaluate five frontier multimodal agents through a common visuomotor interface, as well as a $\pi_{0.5}$ policy fine-tuned on the full-episode demonstrations we release. The best agent succeeds in only 23\% of the episodes, and the fine-tuned policy almost never succeeds. Isolated tests of the execution skills the tasks are built from, with the hidden information supplied, show that the agents can carry out most of the required actions, and our failure analysis attributes only a minority of the failures to execution. Our failure analysis further finds that the agents often stop exploring too early as they make decisions before observing the required evidence for task completion. We also find that agents rarely prevent or repair the disturbances caused by their exploration. Moreover, learning by trial and error remains difficult for most models.

</details>


### [50] [NeRFifyMesh: Optimizing Neural Radiance Fields from Textured Meshes for Robotics Scene Building](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10387)
*Nillan Nimal,Mahboubeh Asadi,Sajad Saeedi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种将现有3D网格模型转换为NeRF表示的流程：通过采样网格几何与纹理生成点基辐射场真值，无需多视角渲染训练，渲染质量与基线相当，并可用于构建统一NeRF场景与碰撞仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人领域，场景表示对理解与交互环境至关重要；NeRF及其变体作为新型表示带来新方向。语义建图与仿真等任务常需用多个物体NeRF构建场景，且已有大量3D网格资产，因此亟需将网格资产快速转换为NeRF模型以支持算法开发与测试。

Method: 设计新的转换流水线：从网格几何和纹理中采样，人工生成ground truth点基辐射场，从而直接获得NeRF表示。该方法避免基于相机采样或渲染原始网格的多视角图像来训练NeRF。

Result: 广泛基准测试表明，该方法渲染质量与基线方法相当；应用方面，能够构建统一NeRF场景，并利用提取几何进行碰撞仿真。

Conclusion: 该工作为网格到NeRF的转换提供了有效工具，便于快速生成NeRF资产、组合场景并支持机器人仿真与碰撞检测等下游应用。

Abstract: In robotics, scene representation plays a pivotal role in understanding and interacting with the environment. The advent of Neural Radiance Fields (NeRF) and its variants, as a novel representation, has opened a new frontier of research. In applications such as semantic mapping and simulation, roboticists aim to build scenes using multiple NeRF models, each representing an object. While extensive datasets of 3D mesh models already exist, there is an urgent need to develop tools to convert these assets to NeRF models for rapid algorithm development and testing. This paper presents a new pipeline for converting existing mesh models to NeRF representations by artificially generating a ground truth point-based radiance field through sampling mesh geometry and texture. This approach alleviates the need for camera-based sampling or rendering multi-view images of the original mesh to train the NeRF model. Extensive benchmarking demonstrates that our method yields comparable rendering quality to the baselines. Additionally, the application of this representation is shown by constructing unified NeRF scenes and performing collision simulations with extracted geometry.

</details>


### [51] [OpenViTac: Learning and Benchmarking Visuo-Tactile Policies in a Unified Sim-and-Real Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10384)
*Yifan Wu,Qin Li,Nan Min,Guojin Zhong,Haoyu Zhao,Zhiyuan Li,Houze Xu,Shengqi Xu,Xingyao Lin,Zijie Diao,Zhaoxiang Liu,Shiguo Lian,Shunlin Lu,Shihao Zhao,Ziyi Ye,Zuxuan Wu,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 OpenViTac 视觉-触觉操作基准，用于在仿真与真实世界中统一评估机器人策略，并基于此构建触觉增强框架 OpenVTLA 及研究仿真-现实协同训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉反馈能为具身智能体提供视觉之外的物理信息，提升真实世界交互可靠性；但视觉-触觉-语言-动作（VTLA）策略快速发展之际，仍缺乏跨仿真与真实世界、面向触觉使能机器人操作统一基准。

Method: OpenViTac 将接触丰富操作组织为四个触觉相关能力维度，并提供配对的仿真-真实世界设置，以一致评估 VLA、WAM 与 VTLA 策略；在此基础上研究不同触觉表征与集成策略对预训练 VLA 模型性能的影响，并提出结合最佳表征与集成策略的触觉增强框架 OpenVTLA；进一步利用配对基准研究仿真-现实协同训练并分析跨域策略学习影响因素。

Result: 构建了统一的视觉-触觉操作评估基准，系统比较了触觉表征与集成方式对 VLA 性能的影响，给出了最优组合形成的 OpenVTLA 框架，并通过配对设置获得仿真-现实协同训练及跨域学习影响因素的分析结果。

Conclusion: OpenViTac 为评估和推进视觉-触觉机器人操作提供了统一平台。

Abstract: Tactile feedback provides embodied agents with physical information beyond visual observations, enabling more reliable interaction with the real world. However, despite the rapid progress of vision-tactile-language-action (VTLA) policies, there remains a lack of unified benchmarks for evaluating tactile-enabled robot manipulation across simulation and the real world. To address this gap, we introduce OpenViTac, a visuo-tactile manipulation benchmark for evaluating robot policies across simulation and the real world. OpenViTac organizes contact-rich manipulation into four tactile-relevant capability dimensions and provides paired simulation-real-world settings for consistent evaluation of VLA, WAM, and VTLA policies. Building upon this benchmark, we investigate how different tactile representations and integration strategies affect the performance of pretrained VLA models. Correspondingly, we introduce OpenVTLA, a tactile augmentation framework that combines the best-performing representation and integration strategy. Furthermore, we leverage the paired benchmark setting to study sim-real co-training and analyze factors affecting cross-domain policy learning. Together, OpenViTac provides a unified platform for evaluating and advancing visuo-tactile robot manipulation.

</details>


### [52] [Semantic-Aware Predictive Mapping for Exploration and Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10382)
*Kenneth J.K. Ong,William W.J. Teo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作研究语义门线索是否能改善室内门等几何模糊区域的预测占用栅格建图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅几何的占用表示难以区分几何相似但语义不同的结构；门常表现为占据栅格，类似墙体，却可能通向已观测区域外的房间或走廊，因此对机器人探索与导航造成歧义。

Method: 作者修改CogniPlan数据集子集，在部分占用图中注入由门引起的歧义并保持真值布局不变；比较仅几何控制模型与额外接收门通道的语义提示模型，并在全图及10像素门区域掩码上评估L1误差、F1和IoU。

Result: 全图性能两模型大体相近，但门区域局部结果显著改善：L1从0.004342降至0.000025，F1从0.031311升至1.000000，IoU从0.015905升至1.000000。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在几何观测本身存在歧义的区域，语义线索可有效提升预测占用补全性能。

Abstract: Predictive mapping can support robotic exploration and navigation by estimating unseen geometric layouts from partial occupancy observations. However, occupancy-only representations may fail to distinguish semantically different structures with similar geometry. This is particularly relevant for indoor doors, which may appear as occupied cells like walls but indicate possible connected rooms or corridors beyond the observed region. This work investigates whether semantic door cues improve predictive geometric occupancy mapping around such ambiguous regions. We modify a subset of the CogniPlan dataset by inserting door-induced ambiguities into partial occupancy maps while keeping the ground-truth layouts unchanged. We compare a geometry-only control model with a semantic-cued model trained on the same modified dataset, where the semantic-cued model receives an additional door channel. Evaluation uses L1 error, F1 score, and Intersection over Union (IoU) over both the full map and a 10-pixel door-region mask. Full-map performance remains broadly similar between models, but localized door-region results show a clear qualitative improvement: L1 decreases from 0.004342 to 0.000025, while F1 and IoU improve from 0.031311 and 0.015905 to 1.000000 and 1.000000, respectively. These results suggest that semantic cues can improve predictive occupancy completion in regions where geometric observations alone are ambiguous.

</details>


### [53] [MultiFly: A Real-World Multimodal Aerial Dataset with Annotation-Efficient Label Transfer and Cross-Modal Semantic Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10359)
*Markus Gross,Andreas Greiner,Taehyoung Kim,Sivasubiramaniam Subbiah,Tomaž Cotič,Sai Bharadwaj Matha,Conrad Christoph,Oussema Dhaouadi,Simon Zieher,Surya Vijaya Kumar,Gordon Elger,Henri Meeß,Olaf Wysocki,Paul Spannaus,Daniel Cremers*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 MultiFly：一个面向低空无人机语义感知的真实世界多模态数据集，涵盖 RGB、热成像、LiDAR 与雷达，并提供同步样本、逐帧语义标注、标定及 GNSS-RTK/IMU 数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态航拍感知需要一致且逐帧的语义标注，但为每种模态单独标注成本高且易不一致；因此需要一个可扩展、跨模态标注一致的真实低空基准。

Method: 采集四个郊区场景的 17,272 个同步样本，定义 15 个语义类别，并提供标定与 GNSS-RTK/IMU 测量。仅人工标注 115 张 RGB 图像，通过共享几何表示将标签传播至四种模态，生成额外 17,157 张 RGB、17,272 张热成像、8.4 亿 LiDAR 点和 340 万雷达点的语义标签，并建立四种模态的语义分割基准。

Result: 传播标注与留出人工标注平均一致率为 89.93%，在全部六组模态对间平均语义一致性为 90.94%；基准实验揭示稠密 LiDAR 与稀疏雷达数据呈现不同架构行为。

Conclusion: MultiFly 为多模态空中感知提供了可扩展基础，是据作者所知首个公开的真实世界低空航拍基准，统一了 RGB、热成像、LiDAR 与雷达的逐帧语义标注。

Abstract: We introduce MultiFly, a real-world, low-altitude UAV dataset for semantic perception across RGB, thermal, LiDAR, and radar modalities. MultiFly provides 17,272 synchronized samples from four suburban scenes with frame-wise annotations for 15 semantic classes, together with calibration and GNSS-RTK/IMU measurements. To avoid costly and inconsistent modality-specific annotation, we propagate labels from only 115 manually annotated RGB images through shared geometric representations to all four modalities. This approach generates semantic labels for 17,157 additional RGB images, 17,272 thermal images, 840M LiDAR points, and 3.4M radar points. Transferred annotations achieve 89.93% average agreement with held-out manual annotations, and 90.94% average semantic consistency across all six modality pairs. We further establish semantic segmentation benchmarks for all four modalities, revealing distinct architectural behavior for dense LiDAR and sparse radar data. Taken together, MultiFly provides a scalable foundation for multimodal aerial perception and, to the best of our knowledge, the first public real-world low-altitude aerial benchmark that combines consistent frame-wise semantic annotations for RGB, thermal, LiDAR, and radar. Data at this https URL .

</details>


### [54] [Hall Effect-Based Tactile Force Detection Sensor for Robot-Assisted Minimally Invasive Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10346)
*Charles DeLorey,Michael Brockdorff,Adam Metcalf,Benjamin Calmé,Pietro Valdastri,Dominic Jones*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种可贴附于机器人手术器械夹持面的紧凑型三轴触觉传感器，基于霍尔效应与磁体/弹性体结构，实现法向和剪切力感知，并验证了其在组织牵拉与滑移检测中的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人微创手术虽已革新外科实践，但术者与手术工具机械分离，导致触觉反馈缺失，因此需要为机器人手术工具集成小型化力/触觉传感能力。

Method: 设计紧凑型力传感器，包含三个毫米级传感单元（taxel），利用嵌入可变形弹性体接触层中的磁体和霍尔效应传感器；使用6自由度并联机器人进行标定，并在体模与离体猪组织上验证。

Result: 每个taxel均可检测法向与剪切力，平均RMSE为2.133 kPa；在体模和离体猪组织实验中实现了牵拉过程中的触觉感知以及滑移检测。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种小型化霍尔效应触觉传感器，可为力敏感机器人手术应用提供触觉反馈，支持微创手术中的力感知需求。

Abstract: Robotic minimally invasive surgery has revolutionized surgical practice. However, as the surgeons are mechanically separated from the surgical tools, loss of tactile feedback occurs. We present a compact force sensor with three, millimeter scale sensing elements (taxels) that can be affixed to the grasping face of a robotic surgical tool. The sensor features a miniaturized design using Hall effect sensors and magnets embedded in a deformable elastomer contact layer. Sensor calibration is achieved with a 6 degree of freedom (DoF) parallel robot. Each taxel can detect applied normal and shear forces with average RMSE of 2.133 kPa. The calibrated sensor was validated on phantom and ex vivo porcine tissue, demonstrating tactile sensing during retraction as well as slip. This work introduces a small-scale Hall effect based tactile sensor, enabling sensing for force-sensitive robotic surgical applications.

</details>


### [55] [Energy-Efficient Gait Adaptation via Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning for Quadrupedal Locomotion Across Diverse Terrains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10297)
*Ammar Issa,Anubhav Singh,Anton Tsaritsin,Sergey Kolyubin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分层强化学习框架，将高频关节级运动执行与低频步态适应解耦，并显式最小化运输成本，实现四足机器人节能、鲁棒且可零样本仿真到现实迁移的运动控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腿足机器人运动中能效至关重要，但端到端强化学习策略将步态生成、运动执行与能量优化紧密耦合，导致对奖励设计高度敏感，难以在不同速度和地形条件下同时保持低能耗与鲁棒性。

Method: 提出分层强化学习框架：高频策略负责稳定鲁棒的关节级运动执行，低频策略进行步态适应并显式最小化运输成本CoT；采用三阶段基于Isaac的训练流程，实现零样本sim-to-real迁移；学习到的层级策略表现出速度相关自动步态适应，低速采用pacing，高速采用trotting。

Result: 在仿真中与代表性单策略和分层运动基线对比，所提方法在广泛指令速度范围内降低CoT，并在平坦、崎岖不平和倾斜地形上保持鲁棒运动；进一步在Unitree AlienGo四足机器人上零样本部署，验证实际可行性。

Conclusion: 将高频运动执行与低频步态及能耗优化解耦，可提升跟踪精度、鲁棒性和能效，实现自动速度相关步态转换，并成功零样本部署于真实四足机器人，表明该分层强化学习框架对节能腿足运动控制有效。

Abstract: While energy efficiency is a critical objective for legged-robot locomotion control, achieving low energy consumption while maintaining robust performance across different velocity ranges and terrain conditions remains a key challenge. This is particularly true for end-to-end RL policies, where gait generation, motion execution, and energy optimization are tightly coupled, leading to high sensitivity to reward design. In this work, we propose a hierarchical reinforcement learning (HRL) framework that separates a high-frequency policy for stable and robust joint-level motion execution from low-frequency gait adaptation that explicitly minimizes the cost of transport (CoT). The three-stage Isaac-based training procedure enables zero-shot sim-to-real transfer with improved tracking accuracy, robustness, and energy efficiency. The learned hierarchy exhibits automatic speed-dependent gait adaptation, transitioning from pacing at low speeds to trotting at higher speeds. We validate the proposed approach in simulation against representative single-policy and hierarchical locomotion baselines, demonstrating reduced CoT over a broad range of commanded velocities, while maintaining robust locomotion across flat, uneven rough, and inclined terrains. We further demonstrate its practical feasibility through zero-shot deployment on a physical Unitree AlienGo quadruped.

</details>


### [56] [Temporal Visuo-Tactile Learning for Dexterous Grasp Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10283)
*Ken Nakahara,Aleksei Buvailik,Prokhor Kotov,Roberto Calandra*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统研究高分辨率动态触觉传感对多指灵巧手抓取稳定性预测与模型引导抓取的作用；基于1万次抓取试验的多模态数据集训练时序多模态模型，发现触觉（尤其高分辨率动态触觉）提升稳定性预测，并在实物在线门控中使执行举升成功率提升10.5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类可借助指尖触觉近乎完美地抓取物体，但机器人抓取研究多依赖视觉与平行夹爪；作者希望探究高分辨率、动态触觉传感如何贡献于灵巧手的抓取稳定性预测与模型引导抓取。

Method: 使用配备4个Digit 360触觉传感器的多指机械手，对200个物体采集10000次抓取试验，同步记录外部视觉、本体感觉与触觉流；训练端到端时序多模态模型，从抓取前观测预测举升后稳定性，比较不同传感模态与编码骨干，并进行受控输入消融；最后将学习到的预测器作为在线稳定性门控部署到真实机器人，执行视觉-触觉模型引导的重抓取。

Result: 实验与消融表明，引入触觉、尤其是高分辨率动态触觉，可改善抓取稳定性预测；在线部署中，视觉-触觉模型引导重抓取使执行举升的成功率相比非触觉门控提高10.5个百分点。

Conclusion: 丰富的指尖传感与能捕捉触觉动态的表达性时序模型，可在无需显式接触或力建模的情况下支持多指手学习抓取，为从触觉经验走向稳定灵巧操作提供可扩展的数据驱动路径；数据集已公开。

Abstract: Humans can grasp everyday objects with almost perfect success rates using fingertip tactile feedback, yet much of the robotic grasping literature emphasizes vision-based grasp selection with parallel grippers. In this work, we systematically investigate how high-resolution, dynamic tactile sensing contributes to grasp stability prediction and model-guided grasping in dexterous robotic hands. To this end, we collected a dataset of 10,000 grasp trials across 200 objects using a multi-fingered robotic hand equipped with four Digit 360 tactile sensors, recording external vision, proprioception, and tactile streams throughout each grasp. With this dataset, we trained end-to-end temporal multimodal models to predict post-lift stability from pre-lift grasp observations and compared sensing modalities and encoding backbones. Experimental results and controlled input ablations show that incorporating touch, and particularly high-resolution, dynamic touch, improves grasp stability prediction. Finally, we deployed the learned predictor as an online stability gate on the real robot, where visuo-tactile model-guided regrasping improved the success rate among executed lifts by 10.5 percentage points over a non-tactile gate. These results show how rich fingertip sensing and expressive temporal models that capture the dynamics of touch can support learned grasping with multi-fingered hands without explicit contact or force modeling, providing a scalable data-driven path from tactile experience toward stable dexterous manipulation. The dataset is publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [57] [Making Task Abstractions Executable: Control-Aware Layout Repair for a Fixed Controller](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10281)
*Chiyoung Kim,Seungyeon Back,Sumin Shim,Doyoung Heo,Rita Singh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作把任务抽象中的跟踪、间隙与驱动需求编译为可审计的仿射布局约束，仅修复声明的连续坐标，并用最违背行更新与二次投影实现带认证的布局修复；在三个任务抽象上验证了布局可认证与 rollout 全完成，同时揭示仅靠布局编辑无法满足的固定事件测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务抽象虽能指定预期事件，但其空间布局可能使固定智能体与控制器无法完成这些事件；因此需要在不改变事件顺序、时序、拓扑和控制器的前提下修复布局。

Method: 从结构化任务记录出发，将全任务跟踪、间隙和驱动要求编译为可审计的仿射布局约束；仅修复声明的连续坐标；采用最违背行更新，给出条件有限认证与净位移界；用同一编译器的二次投影将表示与优化器解耦。

Result: 在三个研究者构建的任务抽象上，两种后端均认证全部三个布局，并各自完成300次新鲜配对rollout；风险目标扫描还暴露出所选证书无法仅靠布局编辑满足的固定事件测试。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了可审计、保持语义的布局修复与有限认证，但证书能力存在边界：某些固定事件测试需要超出布局编辑的手段。

Abstract: A task abstraction can specify the intended events while its spatial layout prevents a fixed agent and controller from completing them. Starting from a supplied structured task record, we compile whole-task tracking, clearance, and actuation requirements into auditable affine layout constraints. We repair only declared continuous coordinates, preserving event order, timing, topology, and the controller. A most-violated-row update admits conditional finite-certification and net-displacement bounds; a same-compiler quadratic projection separates the representation from the optimizer. On three researcher-authored task abstractions, both backends certify all three layouts and complete all 300 fresh paired rollouts per backend. A risk-target sweep also exposes fixed event tests that the chosen certificate cannot satisfy through layout edits alone.

</details>


### [58] [Benchmarking Behavioral Steerability in Behavior Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10198)
*Minghe Gao,Zhanxi Yan,Jiahui Liu,Wendong Bu,Xiaoting Chen,Qizhou Wang,Yi Su,Siliang Tang,Jun Xiao,Yueting Zhuang,Tat-Seng Chua,Juncheng Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出“行为可操控性”概念，并发布首个行为基础模型评估基准RoboSteer，将可操控性划分为条件、约束与组合三个层级，并对9个现有BFM进行首次大规模实证研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为基础模型（BFMs）正从行为生成走向通用行为系统，核心问题是其能否根据用户意图被可靠操控。现有工作缺乏对BFM行为可操控性的系统定义与评估。

Method: 定义behavioral steerability为BFM忠实生成满足用户指定意图行为的能力；提出RoboSteer基准，构建条件操控、约束操控、组合操控三级层次，并建立由大规模多模态运动语料支持的统一评估框架；基于该基准对9个现有BFM开展大规模实证研究。

Result: 利用RoboSteer完成了对9个现有BFM行为可操控性的首次大规模实证研究；摘要未报告具体量化指标或模型排名。

Conclusion: 行为可操控性不仅是运动控制能力，更关乎具身系统如何将人类意图转化为有目的的行动。作者希望RoboSteer推动“意图实现”作为通用具身智能基础的研究。

Abstract: Behavior Foundation Models (BFMs) are emerging as a paradigm for translating human intentions into executable humanoid behaviors. As these models evolve beyond behavior generation toward general-purpose behavioral systems, a fundamental question arises: can they be reliably steered according to user intentions? In this paper, we introduce the concept of behavioral steerability, defined as the ability of BFMs to faithfully generate behaviors that satisfy user-specified intentions. To study this capability, we present RoboSteer, the first benchmark for behavioral steerability in BFMs. RoboSteer organizes behavioral steerability into a three-level hierarchy-Conditional Steering, Constraint Steering, and Compositional Steering-and establishes a unified evaluation framework supported by a large-scale multimodal motion corpus. Using RoboSteer, we conduct the first large-scale empirical study of behavioral steerability across 9 existing BFMs. We view behavioral steerability as more than a capability for controlling motion: it concerns how embodied systems translate human intentions into purposeful actions. We hope RoboSteer will advance research on intention realization as a foundation for general-purpose embodied intelligence.

</details>


### [59] [Design of a Fully Actuated 4-DOF Robotic Finger With Joint-Specific Hybrid Remote Actuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10180)
*Hyojae Kang,Hyun-mok Jung,Joonho Lee,Dongil Park,Hyunmin Do,Jongwoo Park,Jeongdo Ahn*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种全驱动的4自由度机械手指，采用关节特定的混合远程驱动架构，并通过建模与实验验证其解耦驱动与指尖力能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在紧凑机械手指中实现多关节全驱动，同时降低远端关节间的运动耦合，需要一种兼顾传动解耦、线缆长度稳定与独立驱动能力的架构。

Method: MCP关节由两组协同刚性连杆传动驱动；PIP与DIP关节由带圆形滚动接触关节（RCJ）的闭环线传动独立驱动，且PIP传动半径大于DIP。利用RCJ线缆几何在关节旋转时保持总线环长度，并设计DIP走线使固定MCP时PIP运动不影响其差动驱动。对远端线传动、MCP连杆和指尖运动学进行解析建模；实验通过ArUco标记跟踪测量滚珠丝杠位移，评估传动行为并进行孤立关节驱动与指尖力测试。

Result: MCP驱动会引起PIP和DIP传动单元的可测位移，而固定MCP时孤立驱动PIP和DIP支持远端传动间的预期机械解耦。孤立MCP、PIP、DIP驱动下的平均峰值指尖力分别为21.28 N、9.22 N和5.75 N；并使用不同几何与尺寸物体考察了手指姿态。

Conclusion: 所提出的混合远程驱动架构可实现4自由度机械手指的全驱动与远端关节解耦，解析模型和实验共同验证了其传动行为与力输出能力，适用于需要多关节独立控制与姿态适应性的机械手设计。

Abstract: This paper presents a fully actuated 4-DOF robotic finger using a joint-specific hybrid remote-actuation architecture. The metacarpophalangeal (MCP) joint is driven by two coordinated rigid-link transmission sets, whereas the proximal interphalangeal (PIP) and distal interphalangeal (DIP) joints are independently actuated by closed-loop wire transmissions incorporating circular rolling-contact joints (RCJs). A larger transmission radius is used at the PIP joint than at the DIP joint. The RCJ wire geometry maintains the total wire-loop length during joint rotation, and the DIP wire routing is designed so that PIP motion does not affect its differential actuation for a fixed MCP configuration. The distal wire transmission, MCP linkage, and fingertip kinematics are analytically modeled. In experiments, the transmission behavior was quantitatively evaluated from the ball-screw displacements measured using ArUco-marker tracking. MCP actuation produced measurable displacements of the PIP and DIP transmission units, whereas isolated PIP and DIP actuation with the MCP fixed supported the intended mechanical decoupling between the distal transmissions. The mean peak fingertip forces under isolated MCP, PIP, and DIP actuation were 21.28 N, 9.22 N, and 5.75 N, respectively. The resulting finger postures were also examined using objects of different geometries and sizes.

</details>


### [60] [Do Vision-Language-Action Models Understand Instructions? A Mechanistic Interpretability Study on Language Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10178)
*Theodor Wulff,Angelo Cangelosi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 对π0.5和GR00T N1.7的机制可解释性研究表明，二者对语言指令的依赖有限且模型特异：对抽象改写和不存在物体不敏感，但对空指令、尤其方向性语言敏感；敏感层位与归因方法可靠性不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型被设计为跨环境和任务描述泛化，但其动作生成是否真正依赖语言指令，还是主要依赖视觉线索与表面相关性，尚不明确。VLAs缺乏显式grounding模块，而依赖VLM骨干的内在语言grounding能力，因此其视觉与语言观察空间扰动的鲁棒性对实际部署至关重要。

Method: 在LIBERO基准上，针对两个SOTA VLA模型π0.5和GR00T N1.7的动作生成模块残差流，使用activation patching和attribution patching进行受控机制可解释性研究。系统扰动任务指令，采用五种策略：同义词替换、语义缩放、方向性破坏、随机物体替换和空字符串。

Result: 两模型对抽象改写和引用不存在物体相对不敏感，但对空任务描述、尤其方向性语言反应强烈。动作生成中的敏感性集中于不同位置：GR00T N1.7主要在早期周期性cross-attention层，π0.5则分布在最早层和若干后层。GR00T N1.7中方向扰动可产生较大因果效应，但内部表征几何相对不变，这种解离在π0.5中不明显。attribution patching的可靠性依模型而定：对GR00T N1.7与activation patching高度一致，对π0.5则不一致。

Conclusion: VLA的语言grounding并非均匀或完全，存在模型特异的敏感层与因果机制；方向性语言是关键因素，空指令也暴露grounding脆弱性。机制归因方法需按模型验证，不能默认替代activation patching。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models are designed to generalise across environments and task descriptions, raising the question of whether their action generation actually depends on the language instruction, or whether they largely rely on visual cues and superficial correlations. Robustness to variance in the visual and linguistic observation space is critical for real-world deployment, yet VLAs lack explicit grounding modules and instead rely on the intrinsic language grounding capabilities of their Vision-Language model backbones. For this reason, we conduct a controlled mechanistic interpretability study on the language grounding capabilities of two state-of-the-art Vision-Language-Action models, $\pi_{0.5}$ and GR00T N1.7, by applying activation and attribution patching to the residual stream of the action generation modules. We systematically corrupt the task instruction of input samples of the LIBERO benchmark following five strategies: synonym replacement, semantic scaling, directional corruption, random object substitution, and empty string. Our experiments find that both models are comparatively insensitive to abstract rephrasing and to referencing non-existent objects, but react strongly to empty task descriptions and, especially, to directional language. During action generation, this sensitivity is concentrated in different loci for each model: mainly in the early, periodic cross-attention layers for GR00T N1.7, versus distributed across the earliest and selected later layers for $\pi_{0.5}$. For GR00T N1.7, directional perturbations drive some of the largest causal effects while leaving the internal representational geometry comparatively unchanged, a dissociation we do not observe clearly for $\pi_{0.5}$. Finally, the reliability of attribution patching is model-dependent: it closely tracks activation patching for GR00T N1.7 but not for $\pi_{0.5}$.

</details>


### [61] [Lifelong small-object navigation in changing object layouts: a benchmark and method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10125)
*Jiagan Huang,Zikun Zhou,Zijian Ni,Hongpeng Wang,Guangming Lu,Jun Yu,Wenjie Pei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出LiSoNav-COL（变化布局中的终身小物体导航）任务与LiSoNav-Eval基准，并提出IVAM-Nav方法，通过多视角检查与视点锚定记忆提升小物体导航性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家庭机器人需在同一环境中反复导航到小而可移动、易遮挡的物体，且物体可能被人移动而机器人未察觉；现有工作缺少针对空场景记忆、物体重定位和记忆更新等关键假设设计的基准。

Method: 形式化LiSoNav-COL任务，要求智能体从空场景记忆开始，寻找合适视点可靠观测、积累并复用场景知识、更新过时记忆；构建LiSoNav-Eval，覆盖28个室内场景和45类小物体，包含未变与重定位目标的终身导航序列；提出IVAM-Nav，主动从互补视角观察支撑面，并将记忆锚定到观察视点以支持关系记忆复用与相似视角下的重验证。

Result: 在LiSoNav-Eval上，IVAM-Nav相较代表性方法取得更优性能；基准分析表明，更小的物体、更大的环境和更长的重定位距离会带来更大挑战。

Conclusion: LiSoNav-Eval填补了围绕变化布局中小物体终身导航定义假设的基准空白，IVAM-Nav为该挑战性任务提供了有效方案，并揭示了影响难度的关键因素；数据集与代码已公开。

Abstract: Household robots need to continually navigate to different objects in the same environment, many of which are small and portable, such as tools and toys. Their small visual footprint and frequent occlusion make reliable observation difficult, and they may be moved by people without the robot observing the changes. We formulate this challenging task as Lifelong Small-object Navigation in Changing Object Layouts (LiSoNav-COL). Agents must seek suitable viewpoints for reliable observation, accumulate and reuse scene knowledge to efficiently locate subsequent targets, and update outdated memory after object relocation. To eliminate the need for prior scene scanning, we also require agents to start navigation with empty scene memory. Although practical, this task still lacks benchmarks designed around its defining assumptions. To bridge this gap, we introduce LiSoNav-Eval, a dedicated benchmark spanning 28 indoor scenes with 45 small-object categories. Its lifelong navigation sequences include both unchanged and relocated targets to evaluate memory reuse and adaptation to object relocation. To address this challenging task, we propose a navigation method based on multi-view Inspection with Viewpoint-Anchored Memory, dubbed IVAM-Nav. IVAM-Nav actively observes supporting surfaces from complementary viewpoints for reliable small-object perception and anchors the resulting memory to their observation viewpoints, supporting relational memory reuse and revalidation under similar viewing conditions. Extensive experiments on LiSoNav-Eval demonstrate favorable performance of IVAM-Nav against representative methods. Benchmark analyses also show that smaller objects, larger environments, and longer relocation distances pose greater challenges. The dataset and code are available here.

</details>


### [62] [RealtimeWAM: How Fast Can I Run My World Action Model?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10079)
*Huanan Liu,Ye Li,Kangye Ji,Xiaoyu Chen,Hanyun Cui,Yutian Shen,Yuan Meng,Chenglei Wu,Jingyan Jiang,Bo Li,Zhi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RealtimeWAM 是一种无需训练、通用的世界动作模型低延迟推理框架，通过并行执行与自适应计算降低推理延迟，并在仿真与真实机器人任务中实现大幅加速且保持或提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型（WAMs）将视觉动态建模与动作生成结合，但高推理延迟限制机器人闭环控制的响应性。FastWAM 虽在测试时移除显式未来视频生成，但依赖专门架构设计；通用缓存策略虽利用特征冗余，却无法捕捉闭环控制中不断变化的计算需求。

Method: 提出 RealtimeWAM，一个无需训练、面向多种 WAM 架构的低延迟推理框架，协调并行执行与自适应计算。利用层间依赖将观测处理与预测重叠；针对并行分支竞争 GPU 资源的问题，采用选择性复用：在视觉稳定区域缓存观测特征、复用 Transformer 残差，并为小幅预测调整保留额外精化。

Result: 在 FastWAM 和 OpenWAM 上于 RoboTwin、LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus 评测。RTX 4090 上平均推理延迟分别为 24.09 ms 和 63.09 ms，平均加速 8.90× 和 10.67×；平均成功率 82.75% 和 87.41%，与原生推理相差 0.02 和 0.53 个百分点。在五个真实任务中，RealtimeWAM 在 FastWAM 和 OpenWAM 上较原生推理平均成功率分别提升 17.2 和 37.2 个百分点。

Conclusion: RealtimeWAM 为 WAM 提供通用、免训练的低延迟推理方案，在显著加速的同时保持仿真性能，并在真实机器人任务中提升成功率，适用于响应式机器人控制。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) combine visual dynamics modeling with action generation, but their high inference latency limits responsive robot control. Recent efforts accelerate inference by removing explicit future-video generation at test time, as in FastWAM, an approach that requires a specially tailored architectural design. More general caching strategies exploit feature redundancy, but redundancy alone does not capture the changing computational demands of closed-loop control. To address these challenges, we present RealtimeWAM, a general, training-free framework that coordinates parallel execution with adaptive computation for low-latency inference across diverse WAM architectures. We exploit layerwise dependencies to overlap observation processing with prediction. However, concurrent branches still compete for GPU resources, limiting the benefit of parallel execution. We therefore adapt computation throughout the pipeline through selective reuse, caching observation features in visually stable regions and reusing Transformer residuals while reserving additional refinement for small predicted adjustments. We evaluate RealtimeWAM on FastWAM and OpenWAM across RoboTwin, LIBERO, and LIBERO-Plus. On an RTX 4090, measured mean inference latencies are 24.09 and 63.09 ms, corresponding to average speedups of 8.90$\times$ and 10.67$\times$. Average success rates are 82.75% and 87.41%, respectively, within 0.02 and 0.53 percentage points of native inference. Across five real-world tasks, RealtimeWAM improves average success rates over native inference by 17.2 and 37.2 percentage points on FastWAM and OpenWAM, respectively.

</details>


### [63] [Distributed Motion Planning for Multi-Robot Systems under Topological Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10065)
*Gianpietro Battocletti,Dimitris Boskos,Dimos V. Dimarogonas,Bart De Schutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于模型预测控制（MPC）的分布式控制器，以绕数作为拓扑代理来执行编织（braid）拓扑约束，实现多机器人高效协同，并在仿真与实机中验证其优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑编织约束可紧凑、抽象地编码多机器人复杂协调模式，但现有分布式执行方法通常一次执行一个编织生成元，导致轨迹缓慢且次优，因此需要更高效的编织拓扑规范执行方法。

Method: 不直接跟踪编织规范，而是采用其拓扑不变量——绕数作为代理：将编织转化为可由MPC代价项跟踪的连续函数，并把全局编织规范解耦为一组成对规范，通过局部MPC问题分布式求解；同时提出基于一致性的进度估计方法，使机器人同步运动并保持全局协调。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界实验中验证了所提方法的有效性；与现有方法相比，在执行速度和控制代价方面均有改善。

Conclusion: 基于绕数与分布式MPC的协调框架能够高效执行编织拓扑规范，实现更快、更省控制量的多机器人协同运动。

Abstract: Efficient and distributed coordination of mobile robots is one of the main challenges in multi-robot systems. Topological constraints, often expressed as topological braids, are a popular tool to encode complex coordination patterns between multiple mobile robots, as they offer a compact and abstract representation of the desired qualitative relation between the space-time trajectories of the robots. However, execution of joint motion plans encoded as braid-based topological constraints via distributed controllers is challenging, with existing approaches, generally based on the execution of one braid generator at a time, producing slow and suboptimal trajectories. We propose a distributed controller based on Model Predictive Control (MPC) to efficiently execute braid-based topological specifications. Rather than directly tracking the braid specification, we propose to use winding numbers, which are topological invariants for braids, as a proxy. This has the twofold benefit of converting braids into a continuous function, which can be easily tracked by an MPC controller through an appropriate term in the cost function, and of decoupling the global braid specification into a set of pairwise specifications, which can be tracked distributedly through the solution of only local MPC problems. To maintain global coordination, we propose a consensus-based progress estimation approach, which allows the robots to synchronize their motion toward the desired specification. We validate the proposed approach in simulation and in real-world experiments, where we demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach and the improvement over existing approaches in terms of execution speed and control effort.

</details>


### [64] [Video-to-Model: Automatic Modeling of Deformable Linear Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10039)
*Akshun Sharma,Kimia Forghani,Yancy Diaz-Mercado*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种视频到模型框架，可从视频自动建模可变形缝合线的运动：先感知跟踪线并生成有序节点序列，再用时空CNN估计结构化CBF-CLF-QP模型的有效参数，并在给定针速度输入下仿真线程运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 减少可变形线性物体建模中对手动参数调整的依赖，实现从视频自动配置模型，推动基于视频的自动建模。

Method: 感知模块在视频中定位并跟踪缝合线，输出有序线程节点序列；观测到的线程运动由时空CNN处理，估计结构化CBF-CLF-QP模型的有效参数；这些参数用于在用户定义的针速度输入下仿真线程。

Result: 在未见过的线程配置和运动实验中，框架能可靠地从视频重建线程行为，自动配置结构化模型，并以低跟踪误差复现预期的线程运动。

Conclusion: 该方法减少了手动调参需求，是迈向可变形线性物体自动视频建模的一步。

Abstract: This paper presents a video-to-model framework for automatically modeling the motion of a deformable suture thread from an input video. We utilize a recently developed CBF--CLF--QP numerical model that simplifies the characterization of deformable string motion through the selection of a small number of parameters. A perception module first localizes and tracks the thread in video, producing an ordered sequence of thread nodes. The observed thread motion is then processed by a spatio-temporal CNN network that estimates the effective parameters of a structured CBF--CLF--QP model. These parameters are used to simulate the thread under a user-defined needle velocity input. Experiments using unseen thread configurations and motion demonstrate that the framework can reliably reconstruct the thread behavior from video, automatically configure the structured model, and reproduce the expected thread motion with low tracking error. The proposed approach reduces the need for manual parameter tuning and provides a step toward automatic video-based modeling of deformable linear objects.

</details>


### [65] [Tactile Reconstruction of Contact Task Frames and Forces for Hybrid Force/Motion Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10020)
*Antonio Rapuano,Simone Orelli,Barbara Bazzana,Antonio Franchi,Alessandro De Luca*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作仅用软光学触觉传感在线估计接触力与可能时变的任务帧，并通过EKF融合本体感知实现闭环力调节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合力/运动控制依赖交互力及定义力/运动控制方向的任务帧，通常需力/力矩传感或模型残差，并常假设名义环境模型；本文旨在以触觉替代外感受力感知并在线估计任务帧。

Method: 将软光学触觉传感器变形弹性体的单张图像映射为可观测接触变量：压入深度、两个表面-传感器倾斜角和3D接触力，并给出逐样本不确定性估计；映射通过自标注采集学习，机械臂施加受控接触，辅助F/T传感器离线提供真值标签；触觉测量再与机器人本体感知数据在扩展卡尔曼滤波中融合，连续更新接触任务帧和交互力估计。

Result: 在UR10机械臂上安装DigiTac传感器，针对平坦刚性板，在人类操作员施加线性与角运动时实现闭环接触力调节，且仅以触觉作为外感受反馈。

Conclusion: 表明仅凭软光学触觉传感结合EKF即可在线估计接触力与可能时变任务帧，并支撑混合力/运动控制中的闭环力调节。

Abstract: Hybrid force/motion control requires knowledge of the interaction force and of a task frame defining the force- and motion-controlled directions. These quantities are usually obtained from force/torque sensing or model-based residuals, often assuming also a nominal environment model. This work addresses the online estimation of the contact force and a possibly time-varying task frame using only soft optical tactile sensing, under the assumption of locally planar contact with a negligible contact moment. The proposed method maps a single image of the deformed elastomer of a soft optical tactile sensor to observable contact variables: indentation depth, two surface-to-sensor tilt angles, and 3D contact force, each with a per-sample uncertainty estimate. The mapping is learned through a self-labeling acquisition procedure, in which a manipulator imposes controlled contacts while an auxiliary Force/Torque sensor is used offline to provide ground-truth labels. The tactile measurement is then fused with robot proprioceptive data in an Extended Kalman Filter, producing a continuously updated estimate of the contact task frame and of the interaction force. Control experiments with a DigiTac sensor mounted on a UR10 manipulator demonstrate closed-loop contact force regulation against a flat rigid board in linear and angular motion by a human operator, with touch as the only exteroceptive feedback.

</details>


### [66] [Decoding Neural Population Dynamics through Robotic Analog](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09977)
*Wenhui Chen,Jiyue Tao,Yitao Cheng,Yutong Shi,Feitian Zhang,Xitong Liang,Ke Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究构建了包含人工肌肉、多模态传感器和强化学习神经网络控制器的机器人模拟系统，复现了动物运动皮层旋转神经群体动力学，并揭示其与精确、灵活运动结果的因果联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动物证据表明，精确随意运动源于运动皮层的旋转神经群体动力学，但这些动力学的物理效应尚不清楚。

Method: 开发一种生物运动系统的机器人模拟：使用人工肌肉、多模态传感器，以及通过强化学习训练的神经网络控制器进行任务驱动、具身建模，并与灵长类神经数据进行比较验证。

Result: 机器人模拟表现出精确运动、对损伤的鲁棒性以及类似动物的神经群体动力学；发现神经旋转会产生与到达方向正交的振荡动作，从而优化轨迹调整，并得到灵长类神经数据证实；还揭示了传感器与运动冗余下的反直觉神经能量原则，以及运动学习中的显著“Eureka”时刻。

Conclusion: 该具身模型桥接了生物与人工系统，阐明了神经动力学如何促进精确灵活运动，并为未来具有类动物运动能力的智能机器人提供新启示。

Abstract: Animal evidence shows that precise voluntary movements arise from rotational neural population dynamics in motor cortex, but their physical effects remain unknown. We developed a robotic analog of biological motor systems with artificial muscles, multimodal sensors, and a neural network controller trained via reinforcement learning. The robotic analog exhibited accurate movements, robustness to damage, and neural population dynamics akin to animals. This task-driven, embodied model illuminates the causal link between neural population dynamics and motor outcomes. We discovered that neural rotations generate oscillatory maneuvers orthogonal to the reaching direction, optimizing trajectory adjustments, which is confirmed by primate neural data. The model also revealed counterintuitive neural energy principles under sensor and motor redundancies, and striking Eureka moments during motor learning, bridging biological and artificial systems. These findings provide new perspectives on how neural dynamics contribute to accurate and flexible movement, inspiring future intelligent robots with animal-like mobility.

</details>


### [67] [Borrowed Eyes: Markerless Nano-UAV Flight with an Active Quadruped Observer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09967)
*Alejandro Lorite Mora,Dimitrios Arapis,Andrés Faíña*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种视觉定位系统：四足机器人通过机械臂相机跟踪并定位纳米无人机，利用感知感知NMPC优化自身与臂位姿以维持可见性，并将位置通过实时无线链路传给无人机；无人机仅依赖此外部估计完成起降与预设轨迹，中位3D定位误差59 mm，短时遮挡时可切换至机载惯性飞行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米无人机可进入大型机器人无法通行的狭窄空间，但其载荷能力严重限制可用传感器与算力，难以在GNSS拒止环境中自主定位。

Method: 采用四足机器人搭载臂载相机的视觉系统对纳米无人机进行外部定位：四足机器人跟踪无人机，基于分割掩码与深度在其自身坐标系中估计无人机位置，并通过实时无线链路发送位置；同时设计感知感知非线性模型预测控制（NMPC），动态调整四足机器人本体与机械臂以最大化无人机可见性，将观测可靠性作为主要控制目标。

Result: 纳米无人机仅依赖此外部估计，从起飞到降落执行预设轨迹，中位3D定位误差为59 mm；在短时视觉遮挡导致视线暂时丢失时，可平滑切换至机载惯性飞行，表现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该框架使四足机器人能够分担纳米无人机的定位负担，从而支持对单一机器人无法进入或完成的复杂空间进行巡检。

Abstract: Nano unmanned aerial vehicles (nano-UAVs) can navigate confined spaces that larger robots cannot, but their payload capacity severely limits the sensors and compute available for self-localization in global navigation satellite system (GNSS)-denied environments. We present a vision-based system that localizes a nano-UAV from a quadruped robot with an arm-mounted camera. The quadruped tracks the drone, estimates its position in its own coordinate system using segmentation masks and depth, and transmits that position over a real-time radio link. To ensure continuous tracking, we developed a perception-aware nonlinear Model Predictive Controller (NMPC) that dynamically adjusts the quadruped's body and arm to maximize the drone's visibility, treating observation reliability as a primary control objective. Relying solely on this external estimate, the nano-UAV executed predefined trajectories from takeoff to landing with a median 3D localization error of 59 mm. The system demonstrates robustness against short visual occlusions by smoothly transitioning to onboard inertial flight when line-of-sight is temporarily lost. Ultimately, this framework allows a quadruped to offload the localization burden of a nano-UAV, enabling inspection of complex spaces that neither robot could navigate alone.

</details>


### [68] [Many Ways to Succeed: Diversity-Driven RL Fine-Tuning for VLA Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09943)
*Haoru Li,Jinmei Liu,Zhiyong Wang,Xiaoming Li,Zhenhong Sun,Daoyi Dong,Chunlin Chen,Zhi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 DRIVE，一种面向 VLA 泛化的多样性驱动强化学习微调方法，通过将成功行为多样性显式纳入 RL 目标，提升分布外泛化能力并在仿真与真实平台上取得一致增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL 微调虽能通过闭环经验提升 VLA 策略，但其泛化到微调分布之外仍受限。分析发现，RL 会选择性重塑探索：全局上压缩行为，却多样化成功轨迹、以更少 rollout 取得成功，并覆盖更多潜在任务有效解空间；更广的成功模式覆盖可能为分布偏移提供替代策略。

Method: DRIVE 将成功行为多样性转化为显式 RL 目标。其在匹配任务条件下对 rollout 分组，通过时间对齐比较轨迹，并基于相对行为多样性构造成功条件内在奖励；该设计鼓励覆盖更广可行解，同时避免奖励多样的失败或表面时序差异。

Result: 在 LIBERO-Plus、ManiSkill3 和 RoboTwin 2.0 上，DRIVE 相比普通 RL 微调将平均 OOD 性能在 π0 上提升 5.3 分、在 π0.5 上提升 2.0 分。在双臂 AgileX PiPER-X 平台上，DRIVE 将平均 OOD 成功率从 64.1% 提升至 73.3%（+9.2 分），表明增益在物理部署下仍持续存在。

Conclusion: DRIVE 通过显式优化成功行为多样性，有效改善 VLA 策略的分布外泛化，并在多个仿真基准和真实双臂平台上验证了其有效性与部署潜力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) fine-tuning improves vision-language-action (VLA) policies through closed-loop experience, yet generalization beyond the fine-tuning distribution remains limited. Our analysis reveals a selective reshaping of exploration: RL contracts behavior globally, yet diversifies successful trajectories, elicits success with fewer rollouts, and covers more of the latent task-valid solution space than supervised fine-tuning. Broader successful-mode coverage may provide alternative strategies under distribution shifts. Inspired by this, we introduce DRIVE (Diversity-driven RL fIne-tuning for VLA gEneralization), which turns successful-behavior diversity into an explicit RL objective.
