DRIVE groups rollouts under matched task conditions, compares their trajectories with temporal alignment, and derives a success-conditioned intrinsic reward from relative behavioral diversity. This design encourages broader coverage of feasible solutions without rewarding diverse failures or superficial timing differences. Across LIBERO-Plus, ManiSkill3, and RoboTwin 2.0, DRIVE improves the average out-of-domain (OOD) performance over vanilla RL fine-tuning by 5.3 points on $\pi_0$ and 2.0 points on $\pi_{0.5}$. On a dual-arm AgileX PiPER-X platform, DRIVE further increases average OOD success from 64.1% to 73.3% (+9.2 points), demonstrating gains that persist under physical deployment.

</details>


### [69] [Juno: Taming Predictive Latents for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09940)
*Yuchen Zhu,Chenyi Xu,Yulin Zhang,Gang Xu,Wentao Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Juno 提出以单一动作条件 JEPA 为核心的统一 VLA 框架，解决表征-控制错配、动作学习干扰和分布偏移下教师失准，并在仿真与真实机器人上提升成功率与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA 可在表征空间预测掩码或未来观测，为 VLA 提供预测潜变量，但直接用于预训练、策略学习和部署会面临三方面失败：与具体具身控制不匹配、干扰动作学习、分布偏移下预测教师校准失效。

Method: Juno 围绕一个动作条件 JEPA 统一作为控制对齐表征骨干、预测教师和可适应动力学模型。预训练在具身匹配轨迹上训练，并用动态 CLS 损失将运动加权 patch 动力学迁移到紧凑全局状态；策略学习将当前帧 JEPA patch 融合进 VLA 感知，并用带独立变换参数的解耦推理分支蒸馏未来潜状态以生成动作；部署时在所有观测转移（含失败 rollout）上适应世界模型，冻结适应后的教师，并用 LoRA 适配器和可训练动作头在已验证执行上重对齐策略，无需专家纠正或任务奖励。

Result: 在 SimplerEnv 上，Juno 将平均成功率从最强基线 Qwen3GR00T 的 60.9% 提升至 68.5%，测试时适应进一步达到 72.7%；在真实机器人上，在背景、高度和物体偏移下仍保持 70%–75% 成功率，而基础策略降至 0%。

Conclusion: Juno 表明，将动作条件 JEPA 统一用于表征、预测与动力学适应，可有效提升 VLA 策略的控制对齐、学习效率与分布外鲁棒性，且无需专家纠正或任务奖励即可完成部署适应。

Abstract: Joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPAs) predict masked or future observations in representation space, offering a natural source of predictive latents for vision-language-action (VLA) models. Yet making these latents useful across pretraining, policy learning, and deployment requires addressing three failures: mismatch with embodiment-specific control, interference with action learning, and teacher miscalibration under distribution shifts. We introduce Juno, a unified framework built around one action-conditioned JEPA that serves as a control-aligned representation backbone, a predictive teacher, and an adaptable dynamics model. During pretraining, we train it on embodiment-matched trajectories and use a dynamic CLS loss to transfer motion-weighted patch dynamics to a compact global state. During policy learning, we fuse current-frame JEPA patches into VLA perception and use a decoupled reasoning branch with separate transformation parameters to distill future latent states for action generation. During deployment, we adapt the world model on all observed transitions, including failed rollouts, freeze the adapted teacher, and re-align the policy on verified executions using LoRA adapters and a trainable action head, without expert corrections or task rewards. On SimplerEnv, Juno raises average success from $60.9\%$ to $68.5\%$ over Qwen3GR00T, the strongest baseline, and test-time adaptation further reaches $72.7\%$; on a real robot, it retains $70\%$--$75\%$ success under background, height, and object shifts where the base policy collapses to $0\%$.

</details>


### [70] [Towards Accurate End-Effector Localization for UMI-Style Robotic Manipulation Teaching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09857)
*Junjie Zhang,Deteng Zhang,Zhisong Xu,Bo Sun,Liuyang Li,Yihong Tian,Jie Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出面向机器人示教学习的操作接口定位数据集 MILD 与无预建标定图的 AprilVINS 方法，用于评估近距离操作和相机遮挡下的末端执行器定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人示教学习需要准确且时间完整的末端执行器定位，尤其在近距离操作与相机遮挡时；现有 SLAM 基准侧重导航运动，而操作数据集更关注策略学习而非定位评估。

Method: 构建 MILD：包含真实与仿真序列。真实子集提供来自 Insta360 X5 和 Insight9 的 86 条传感器序列，覆盖 15 个重复桌面任务，并包含标定资源与每次执行的机器人末端执行器参考轨迹；MILD-Sim 在 Isaac Sim 中扩展任务覆盖，用于受控操作回放研究。在仪器化真实记录上对视觉惯性和基准辅助系统进行基准测试。提出 AprilVINS：结合鱼眼视觉惯性估计与序列局部 AprilTag 几何，分离先验准入与联合优化状态的安全导出，支持无预测量基准图的标记增强教学空间。

Result: 在 Insta360 AprilTag4 记录上，采用统一协议和序列特定配置，AprilVINS(full) 达到毫米级 SE(3) 对齐的 TCP 相对 APE RMSE，时间完成率高，且报告误差低于所测路线在各自协议下的结果；无标签因子的鱼眼 VIO 仍为厘米级。消融实验将精度与可导出性分离，MILD-Sim 回放研究提供任务特定容差参考以解释误差量级。

Conclusion: MILD 与 AprilVINS 共同为 UMI 风格示教采集提供了诊断性基准框架；代码、数据集与评估清单将在接收后发布。

Abstract: Robot demonstration learning requires accurate and temporally complete end-effector localization during close-range manipulation and camera occlusion. Existing SLAM benchmarks emphasize navigation motions, whereas manipulation datasets prioritize policy learning over localization evaluation. We introduce MILD, a Manipulation-Interface Localization Dataset with real-world and simulation sequences. The real-world subset provides 86 sensor sequences from Insta360 X5 and Insight9 across 15 repeated tabletop tasks, calibration assets, and a per-execution robot end-effector reference trajectory. The simulation subset, MILD-Sim, extends task coverage in Isaac Sim for controlled manipulation-replay studies. Benchmarking visual-inertial and fiducial-aided systems on instrumented real-world recordings reveals large differences in both TCP-relative trajectory error and temporal coverage, even under the same nominal task. To support marker-augmented teaching workspaces without a pre-surveyed fiducial map, we present AprilVINS, which combines fisheye visual-inertial estimation with sequence-local AprilTag geometry and separates prior admission from guarded export of the jointly optimized state. On Insta360 AprilTag4 recordings, AprilVINS(full) under a unified protocol with sequence-specific profiles reaches millimeter-level SE(3)-aligned TCP-relative APE RMSE with high time completion and lower reported error than the tested routes under their respective protocols, whereas fisheye VIO without tag factors remains at centimeter scale. Ablations separate accuracy from exportability, and a MILD-Sim replay study provides task-specific tolerance references for interpreting those error magnitudes. Together, MILD and AprilVINS provide a diagnostic benchmarking framework for UMI-style demonstration collection. Code, datasets, and evaluation manifests will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [71] [Enhancing Robotic Perception and Adaptability through Sensor Fusion and Origami-Inspired Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09828)
*Namai Chandra,Jaison Jose,Kavi Arya,Shivaram Kalyanakrishnan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种紧凑移动机器人，利用折纸启发轮实现运动并主动调节传感几何；通过轮位改变底盘俯仰，使2D LiDAR扫描不同高度，并与RGB-D深度流融合，显著降低无效深度比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧凑移动机器人需在载荷与成本严格受限下，应对光照变化和无纹理表面，稳定恢复场景几何。

Method: 原型低于300美元、低于2 kg；折纸启发轮在自适应构型间移动，带动底盘俯仰，使2D LiDAR扫过中间仰角，保持轮位可选定视角。IMU补偿底盘姿态，融合节点将LiDAR回波投影到RGB-D深度流并输入RTAB-Map；复用已有轮驱动执行器扩展扫描视角。

Result: 在无纹理室内走廊和阳光户外区域评估深度融合，每种传感器配置每场景3次运行。全帧无效深度比例均值：室内由21%降至11%，户外由48%降至18%。

Conclusion: 该原型用同一套轮驱动执行器，在提升深度覆盖的同时实现LiDAR视点连续可调。

Abstract: Compact mobile robots must recover scene geometry under changing lighting and surface texture while working within tight payload and cost limits. We present a compact mobile robot that uses origami-inspired wheels for locomotion and active control of its sensing geometry. As the wheels move between terrain-adaptive configurations, the changing chassis pitch sweeps a 2D LiDAR through intermediate elevations; held wheel positions provide a chosen viewing angle. An IMU accounts for chassis attitude, and a fusion node projects LiDAR returns into the RGB-D depth stream supplied to RTAB-Map. The arrangement uses the wheel actuation already present on a sub-300 USD, sub-2 kg prototype to extend the scanner's viewing geometry. We assess depth fusion in a textureless indoor corridor and an outdoor sunlit area, with three runs per sensor configuration in each setting. Mean full-frame invalid-depth fractions fell from 21% to 11% indoors and from 48% to 18% outdoors. The prototype combines improved depth coverage with a continuously adjustable LiDAR viewpoint using the same actuation that reconfigures its wheels.

</details>


### [72] [End-to-End Autonomous Generation of Human Assembly Plans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09781)
*Faustin Arion von Arx,Millicent Schlafly,Mark D. Fuge*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种端到端方法，将长期确立的面向装配设计原则编码进从网格装配体生成装配计划的流程，无需关节元数据或紧固件标注，即可输出装配手册或结构化失败报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将CAD设计转化为装配计划目前仍主要依赖人工，工程师需推理几何可行性、工具可达性、稳定性与人工装配工效学，耗时且依赖经验；同时现有流程往往需要额外元数据或标注。

Method: 方法仅输入网格装配体，自主处理制造计划的四个核心部分：装配工具清单、装配序列与子装配、装配手册以及可装配性设计反馈。序列规划通过在物理模拟器中系统拆卸物体，并应用编码DfA原则的成本函数；手册生成、工具标注和装配反馈主要依赖多模态大语言模型。

Result: 相比Tian等人始终优先移除最外层零件的基线，DfA感知序列规划在136个含5至30个零件的装配体上，以机械臂装配时间代理指标衡量，将模拟装配时间减少35%；正确工具选择率为88.6%；视觉语言模型评判器通过比较生成手册与消融变体，识别出读者所需的关键页面元素。

Conclusion: 该端到端方法无需额外标注即可生成装配计划与设计反馈，并提供开源代码，可供工程师或AI智能体用于快速加速特定产品设计的制造计划创建。

Abstract: Turning a CAD design into an assembly plan is still largely done by hand, requiring engineers to reason about geometric feasibility, tool access, stability, and the ergonomics of human assembly. In this work, we encode long-established design for assembly (DfA) principles into a contained, end-to-end approach for generating assembly plans. Our approach takes only a mesh assembly and produces either a step-by-step assembly manual or a structured failure report, requiring no joint metadata, fastener annotations, or additional information. Four major components of a manufacturing plan are addressed autonomously: an assembly tool list, the assembly sequence and subassemblies, an assembly manual, and design feedback for improving assemblability. For determining the sequence plan, we systematically disassemble the object in a physics simulator and apply a cost function that encodes DfA principles. Manual generation, tool labelling, and assembly feedback rely primarily on multimodal large language models. Compared with a baseline that always removes the outermost part first from Tian et al., DfA-aware sequence planning reduces simulated assembly time, measured with a robot-arm assembly-time proxy, by 35% on 136 assemblies of 5 to 30 parts. The correct tool is selected for 88.6% of assembly steps. A vision-language model judge compares the generated manuals against ablated variants, identifying which page elements carry the information a reader needs. The presented approach and open-source code are available for use by engineers or AI agents looking to rapidly accelerate the creation of manufacturing plans for a given product design.

</details>


### [73] [On-Demand Robotic Assembly via Differentiable Geometric Part Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09777)
*Millicent Schlafly,Fabio Schaub,Diogo Costa Pais,Luca Lelli,Janne Dvorak,Claire Colmont,Sven Marti,Mark D. Fuge*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种端到端自主流水线，将自然语言提示转化为可机器人装配的定制木结构：生成式AI生成初始3D几何，再通过基于图注意力网络代理的梯度修复优化可装配性，并在真实机器人上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前从数字设计到机器人装配通常需要专家数月手动调参，以协调零件几何与机器人约束；作者希望实现按需、快速、低量的定制化机器人制造。

Method: 流水线包含：生成式AI智能体将用户提示转为初始3D几何，并在视觉保真度与部分物理约束间平衡；随后引入基于梯度的修复阶段，通过对图注意力网络代理反向传播来调整构件几何；除修复不连通与重叠构件外，还进行硬件特定校正，即可微地优化几何以适配机器人拧螺丝操作。

Result: 在60个新的自然语言输入中，86.7%可实现机器人螺丝拧紧，较先前工作提升十倍；并选取其中10个结构，用两台UR5e机器人实际演示了可装配性。

Conclusion: 该工作向按需机器人制造迈出重要一步，有望支持定制化、小批量产品的快速生产。

Abstract: Transitioning from a digital design to a robotic assembly process currently requires months of expert manual tuning to reconcile part geometries with robotic constraints. This paper presents an end-to-end, autonomous pipeline for the design and physical construction of bespoke wooden assemblies. A generative AI agent translates user prompts into initial 3D geometries, balancing the visual fidelity of the design with select physical constraints. The assemblability of the design is further improved by a gradient-based repair stage that backpropagates through a graph attention network surrogate to adjust component geometries. In addition to correcting for disjointed and overlapping components, we demonstrate hardware-specific corrections, differentiably optimizing the geometry of components to enable robot screwdriving for 86.7% of 60 novel natural language inputs, significantly outperforming prior work by a factor of ten. For ten of the structures, we physically demonstrate assemblability with two UR5e robots. This work marks a meaningful step toward on-demand robotic manufacturing, enabling the rapid production of customized, low-volume goods.

</details>


### [74] [AeroEval: Staged Program and Execution Validation for AI-Generated Drone Missions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09764)
*Kautuk Astu,Naina Rabha,Yogesh Simmhan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AeroEval 是一种智能体辅助中间件，通过分阶段验证 AI 生成的无人机任务程序，结合确定性程序分析与上下文增强的 LLM 智能体，显著提升导航与分析任务的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 能从自然语言生成无人机程序，但语法正确的程序仍可能违背用户意图、环境约束和任务级行为；在信息物理应用中，正确性依赖生成代码、移动感知、环境几何、事件驱动分析和物理执行之间的交互。现有系统多用提示词护栏或仿真结果，故障定位能力有限。

Method: 提出 AeroEval，将确定性程序分析与上下文增强的 LLM 智能体结合，对 AI 生成无人机任务进行分阶段验证：先验证程序语法、平台 API 使用和任务意图，再基于执行轨迹、任务需求和环境上下文评估实际行为；每个阶段返回结构化故障信息以支持迭代再生成。

Result: 在 AirSim 和 Gazebo 仿真器上，使用 20 个导航任务和 5 类分析任务评估，导航成功率从 55% 提升至 95%。阶段消融中，仅 Code 与 Trajectory Validator 的平均运行级成功率分别为 44% 和 56%，完整 AeroEval 达 88%；各阶段能检测程序结构、API 使用、任务意图、避障、高度、覆盖率和事件驱动转换中的互补故障，并指导再生成修正。分析任务中，聚合运行级成功率从一次性 AeroGen 的 34% 提升至再生成预算内的 88%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在评估环境中，结合程序级验证与执行接地智能体验证，能有效提升 AI 生成无人机应用的正确性和任务成功率。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) can generate drone programs from natural-language mission descriptions, but syntactically valid programs may still violate user intent, environmental constraints, and mission-level behavior. This problem is pronounced in cyber-physical applications, where correctness depends on the interaction among generated code, mobile sensing, environmental geometry, event-driven analytics, and physical execution. Existing drone code-generation systems primarily use prompt guardrails or simulator outcomes and provide limited failure localization. We present AeroEval, an agent-assisted middleware for staged validation of AI-generated drone missions. AeroEval combines deterministic program analysis with context-grounded LLM agents. It first validates program syntax, platform API usage, and mission intent, and then evaluates the realized behavior using execution trajectories, mission requirements, and environmental context. Each stage returns structured failure information for iterative regeneration. In our evaluation using 20 navigation tasks and five analytical mission types over AirSim and Gazebo simulators, AeroEval improves navigation success from 55% to 95%. In a stagewise ablation study, our Code and Trajectory Validators by themselves achieve mean run-level success rates of 44% and 56%, respectively, while the full AeroEval pipeline achieves 88%; the stages detect complementary failures in program structure, API usage, mission intent, obstacle avoidance, altitude, coverage, and event-driven transitions and the guided regeneration corrects for them. Across the main analytics missions, AeroEval increases aggregate run-level success from 34% for one-shot AeroGen to 88% within the regeneration budget. These results demonstrate the benefit of combining program-level and execution-grounded agentic validation for AI-generated drone applications in the evaluated environment.

</details>


### [75] [ΔWAM: Distilling Action Tangent Fields into World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09734)
*Ke Wu,Hanwen Huang,Bo Gu,Kaizhao Zhang,Xiangting Meng,Yupeng Zheng,Zijun Xu,Jieru Zhao,Wenchao Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Action Tangent Fields，用动作引起的未来动态局部一阶变化来重构世界动作模型监督，从而提升机器人策略鲁棒性与推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型（WAM）通过稠密未来预测增强稀疏动作监督，但可预测的未来大多由外观和场景持续性主导，而非动作相关动态；因此世界监督应提高动作相关变化的比例。

Method: 将世界监督重构为动作如何引起未来动态变化的局部泰勒展开；在 Residual-VAE 空间中表示未来动态，使未来潜变量可由当前潜变量及其残差恢复；使用强动作条件世界模型（ACWM）探测动作变化与残差世界变化之间的局部对应关系；将该局部一阶结构蒸馏到 WAM 中，引导去噪监督偏向与动作强耦合的动态，而非仅由外观可预测的动态；并将多步 VideoDiT 去噪蒸馏为单步。

Result: 在 LIBERO-Plus、RoboTwin 和 RoboTwin2.0-Plus 上，方法对光照、背景、相机、布局等环境扰动均一致提升鲁棒性；在无大规模具身预训练情况下，若干分布偏移下鲁棒性强于预训练策略；单步蒸馏实现高效推理。

Conclusion: 有效的 WAM 监督应保持信息丰富，同时将预测能力集中在动作改变未来动态的方向上。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAM) improve robot policies by augmenting sparse action supervision with dense future prediction. However, much of the predictable future is dominated by appearance and scene persistence rather than action-dependent dynamics. We observe that several recent WAM designs, including optical flow, motion-centric representations, and latent actions, can be understood from a common perspective in which world supervision becomes more efficient as it contains a higher proportion of action-relevant variation. Based on this insight, we introduce Action Tangent Fields, which reformulate world supervision through a local Taylor expansion of how actions induce changes in future dynamics. We represent future dynamics in Residual-VAE space, where the future latent remains recoverable from the current latent and its residual, and use a strong action-conditioned world model (ACWM) to probe the local correspondence between action variations and residual-world variations. This local first-order structure is distilled into the WAM to guide its denoising supervision toward dynamics that are more tightly coupled to action, rather than merely predictable from appearance. Across LIBERO-Plus, RoboTwin, and RoboTwin2.0-Plus, our method consistently improves robustness to lighting, background, camera, layout, and other environmental perturbations. Despite using no large-scale embodied pretraining, it achieves stronger robustness under several distribution shifts than pretrained policies. We further distill multi-step VideoDiT denoising into a single step for efficient inference. Our results suggest that effective WAM supervision should remain information-rich while concentrating its predictive capacity on the directions along which actions change the future.

</details>


### [76] [YUBI-STAG: Contact and Semantic-Rich Alignment for VLAs via Automated Video-Language Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09718)
*Masatoshi Tateno,Takehiko Ohkawa,Yueh-Hua Wu,Hanlong Li,Tatsuya Matsushima,Yoichi Sato,Kei Ota*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出YUBI-STAG自动为机器人操作演示添加交互丰富的时空语义标注，并蒸馏为高效模型YUBI-VLM；利用这些标注后训练VLA策略，可提升双臂操作的细粒度语言对齐、指令跟随和接触感知控制能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型虽经大规模预训练具备广泛操作能力，但仅靠语言触发这些能力需要指令与物理交互之间的细粒度对齐。现有机器人演示通常只提供粗粒度任务描述，缺少动作执行细节，例如哪只夹爪动作、接触哪个物体、如何抓取和移动。

Method: YUBI-STAG结合接触物体分割与视觉语言模型，自动标注物体身份、属性与状态、单夹爪动作、双臂协调以及空间接地的交互信息。为缓解对局部序列和多阶段VLM推理的依赖，进一步蒸馏出YUBI-VLM。YUBI-VLM可直接从原始、未分割视频中通过少量推理调用恢复动作结构和标注，并可仅依赖腕部视角。两个框架在YUBI-STAG-Bench上按时间、语义和空间接地任务评估。

Result: YUBI-VLM以更少推理调用和更短运行时间保留了YUBI-STAG的大部分标注准确率，并能泛化到未见过的操作。使用这些标注后训练VLA策略后，策略与细粒度语言及接触感知结构对齐。双臂实验显示性能和指令跟随均有提升，包括对物体身份、执行夹爪、目标位置和空间关系的控制，而这些在原标签中并不存在。

Conclusion: 通过自动生成交互丰富的语义标注并蒸馏高效VLM，可弥合粗粒度演示与细粒度语言对齐之间的差距，进而提升VLA策略的接触感知、指令可控性和双臂操作表现。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models acquire broad manipulation capabilities via large-scale pretraining, yet eliciting them through language requires fine-grained alignment between instructions and physical interactions. Existing robot demonstrations typically provide only coarse task descriptions, omitting how actions are executed, including which gripper acts, which object is contacted, and how it is grasped and moved. We introduce YUBI-STAG, a framework for Spatio-Temporal Annotation and Grounding that automatically enriches manipulation demonstrations with interaction-rich semantics to align pretrained VLAs with fine-grained manipulation language. Combining contact-object segmentation with vision-language models, YUBI-STAG annotates object identities, attributes and states, per-gripper actions, bimanual coordination, and spatially grounded interactions. To address YUBI-STAG's reliance on localized sequences and multi-stage VLM inference, we distill it into YUBI-VLM. YUBI-VLM directly recovers action structure and annotations from raw, unsegmented video in few inference calls and operates from wrist views alone. We evaluate both frameworks on YUBI-STAG-Bench across temporal, semantic, and spatial grounding tasks. YUBI-VLM retains much of YUBI-STAG's annotation accuracy with fewer inference calls and shorter runtime while generalizing to unseen manipulations. Finally, post-training VLA policies on these annotations aligns them with fine-grained language and contact-aware structure. Bimanual experiments demonstrate improved performance and instruction following, including control over object identity, acting gripper, target location, and spatial relations absent from original labels.

</details>


### [77] [RoboPace: Contact-Aware Time-Optimal Retiming for Action-Chunk Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09696)
*Mimo Shirasaka,Takehiko Ohkawa,Takuya Okubo,Nicola Scianca,Tatsuya Matsushima,Kei Ota*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboPace 是一种在线重定时层，可在不重新训练策略的情况下，保留 VLA 策略的几何路径并根据目标机器人约束和接触预测调整执行时序，从而在接触丰富任务中兼顾速度与安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作数据采集正从遥操作转向无机器人演示（如 UMI 或人手直接演示），但 VLA 策略会继承演示者的时序。人类时序不能直接迁移到机器人：柔性手可承受快速接触，而机器人可能因执行器和跟踪限制而过冲；机器人在自由空间又可比人类更快。因此需要统一方法协调执行速度与接触安全。

Method: 提出 RoboPace，一个在线重定时层。它保持策略的几何路径不变，仅调整其时序，并尊重目标机器人的运动学与动力学约束。该方法根据预测接触自适应执行速度，联合考虑接触相关速度限制和机器人运动约束；无需策略重训练，可实时运行。

Result: 在双臂机器人的三个接触丰富任务上，更快的均匀执行和仅考虑物理限制的重定时基本失败。RoboPace 相比慢速均匀执行取得更高总体成功率，并在约一半时间内完成五条命令中的四条，保留了慢速执行的可靠性且消除了其时间成本。

Conclusion: RoboPace 能有效将人类演示时序适配到目标机器人，在接触丰富操作中实现速度与安全性的平衡，且无需重新训练策略，具备实时性。

Abstract: Robot manipulation data collection has been shifting from teleoperation toward robot-free demonstrations, through interfaces such as the Universal Manipulation Interface (UMI) or directly from human hands. Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies trained on such data inherit the demonstrator's timing. Yet human timing does not directly transfer to robots: compliant hands tolerate fast contact, whereas robots may overshoot due to actuator and tracking limitations; conversely, robots can move faster in free space. This motivates a unified approach that reconciles execution speed with contact safety. We present RoboPace, an online retiming layer that preserves the policy's geometric path while adapting its timing, respecting the target robot's kinematic and dynamic constraints. It adapts execution speed based on predicted contact, jointly accounting for contact-dependent speed limits and the robot's motion constraints. The method requires no policy retraining and operates in real time. Across three contact-rich tasks on a dual-arm robot, faster uniform execution and physical-limit-only retiming largely fail. RoboPace instead achieves higher overall success than slow uniform execution while completing four of five commands in approximately half the time, retaining the reliability of slow execution without its time cost.

</details>


### [78] [Do Better Visual Representations Always Lead to Better End-to-End Autonomous Driving?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09695)
*Zihao Zhang,Haochen Tian,Tianyu Li,Changhui Jing,Jingliang He,Naisheng Ye,Ziyuan Pu,Zhenjie Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出与规划器无关的视觉表征对齐框架 ViRA，系统研究视觉基础模型（VFM）何时能提升端到端自动驾驶性能，并据此开发 ViRA-Diffusion，在 NAVSIM v2 navtest 上取得 92.3 EPDMS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VFM 已被广泛集成到端到端自动驾驶中，但其表征在何种条件下能真正提升驾驶性能尚不明确。

Method: 提出 ViRA：一种保持规划器架构与推理成本不变的规划器无关视觉表征对齐框架；通过多规划器、多 VFM 目标及辅助感知监督进行对比研究，并进一步训练无辅助感知监督的扩散式规划器 ViRA-Diffusion。

Result: 发现三点：(1) VFM 引导的视觉表征可一致提升多种端到端规划器性能，并泛化到零样本闭环评测；(2) VFM 目标选择影响规划性能，对齐到不同 VFM 可进一步惠及已有预训练 VFM 编码器的规划器；(3) 辅助感知监督降低对 VFM 目标选择的敏感性，将五个目标间的 EPDMS 差距从 2.7 分缩小到 0.5 分，并可补偿较弱 VFM 目标。ViRA-Diffusion 在 NAVSIM v2 navtest 上达到 92.3 EPDMS，至少领先近期方法 1.9 分。

Conclusion: 将 VFM 集成到端到端自动驾驶时，应联合考虑 VFM 目标选择与规划器监督方式。

Abstract: Visual foundation models (VFMs) are increasingly integrated into end-to-end autonomous driving for their powerful representations, yet it remains unclear when these representations improve driving performance. To investigate this question, we introduce ViRA, a planner-agnostic visual representation alignment framework that keeps the planner architecture and inference cost unchanged. Our study reveals three findings: (1) VFM-guided visual representations consistently improve driving performance across diverse end-to-end planners, with gains extending to zero-shot closed-loop evaluation. (2) The choice of VFM target matters for planning performance, and alignment to a different VFM can further benefit planners with pre-trained VFM encoders. (3) Auxiliary perception supervision reduces sensitivity to VFM target selection, narrowing the EPDMS spread across five targets from 2.7 to 0.5 points and potentially compensating for less effective VFM targets. Guided by these findings, we develop ViRA-Diffusion, a diffusion-based planner trained without auxiliary perception supervision, which achieves 92.3 EPDMS on NAVSIM v2 navtest, outperforming recent methods in our comparison by at least 1.9 points. The results motivate jointly considering target selection and planner supervision when integrating VFMs into end-to-end autonomous driving. The results and demo are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [79] [NAViLoss: An Underwater Navigation-Aware Dual-Residual Objective for Physics-Consistent Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09690)
*Arup Kumar Sahoo,Itzik Klein*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出NAViLoss——一种面向学习式AUV速度估计的导航感知、鲁棒且不确定性感知的损失函数，并将其与DeepONet结合为NAVi-DeepONet，在约10,000米半合成真实海试数据上使速度估计精度相比传统与学习基线提升44%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AUV依赖INS与DVL组合导航，DVL速度估计精度至关重要。现有学习式方法虽在退化测量条件下有所改进，但训练目标多采用常规回归损失，对大幅残差与损坏观测敏感，且未显式考虑DVL传感过程的物理一致性与测量不确定性。

Method: 提出NAViLoss：在导航状态域惩罚速度估计残差，并在DVL测量域惩罚波束一致性残差；采用有界形式限制大残差影响，并通过自适应机制调节波束几何中的不确定性。进一步将NAViLoss与DeepONet架构集成，形成NAVi-DeepONet模型，用于水下航行器速度的端到端估计。

Result: 使用多次真实海试采集的约10,000米半合成AUV实验数据进行评估，结果表明速度估计精度相比常规方法与学习式基线提升44%。

Conclusion: 结果验证了导航感知与不确定性自适应损失设计在鲁棒学习式水下速度估计中的有效性。

Abstract: Autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) commonly rely on inertial navigation systems (INS) aided by Doppler velocity logs (DVLs) for reliable underwater navigation. Accurate DVL velocity estimation is therefore essential for successful operation. Recent learning-based methods have demonstrated improved DVL velocity estimation, particularly under degraded measurement conditions. However, their training objectives typically rely on conventional regression losses that are highly sensitive to large residuals and corrupted observations. Additionally, they do not explicitly account for the physical consistency and measurement uncertainty associated with the underlying sensing process. To address these limitations, this paper introduces navigation-aware loss (NAViLoss), a robust and uncertainty-aware objective function for learning-based AUV velocity estimation. NAViLoss jointly penalizes the velocity-estimation residual in the navigation-state domain and the beam-consistency residual in the DVL measurement domain. Its bounded formulation limits the influence of large residuals, while an adaptive mechanism regulates the uncertainty in beam geometry. Furthermore, NAViLoss is integrated with a DeepONet architecture to form a novel NAVi-DeepONet model for seamless estimation of an underwater vehicle's velocity. Lastly, our model is evaluated using approximately 10,000m of semi-synthetic AUV experimental data collected during multiple real-world sea trials. Experimental results demonstrate a 44% improvement in velocity-estimation accuracy compared with conventional and learning-based baselines. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of navigation-aware and uncertainty-adaptive loss design for robust learning-based underwater velocity estimation.

</details>


### [80] [Design optimization of tendon-driven robots considering tendon wrapping and shortcut](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09645)
*Yuta Sahara,Kento Kawaharazuka,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种肌腱驱动机械臂的多目标优化设计方法，通过肌腱走线与捷径效应权衡关节扭矩与臂厚，并利用NSGA-II获得帕累托最优设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肌腱驱动臂设计中存在关节扭矩与臂厚度之间的固有矛盾；肌腱缠绕和捷径效应可扩展有效力臂，但需要系统优化以兼顾紧凑性与高扭矩。

Method: 将问题建模为同时最大化关节扭矩、最小化臂厚度的多目标优化问题，采用NSGA-II求解，并优化肌腱走线、滑轮配置及附着点。

Result: 得到帕累托最优设计，发现通过策略性捷径可有效扩展力矩臂；优化构型呈现超越传统设计直觉的非平凡模式，并为紧凑高扭矩臂提供实用设计准则。

Conclusion: 该研究为高效机器人系统的工程设计提供了新见解，表明多目标优化可有效指导肌腱驱动臂的紧凑化与高扭矩设计。

Abstract: This study proposes a multi-objective optimization method for tendon-driven arm design, addressing the inherent trade-off between joint torque and arm thickness through tendon wrapping and shortcut effects. We formulate the problem to simultaneously maximize torque and minimize thickness, solved using NSGA-II. The algorithm optimizes tendon routing, pulley configurations, and attachment points. Results reveal Pareto-optimal designs with effective moment arm expansion via strategic shortcuts, providing practical guidelines for compact high-torque arms. The optimized configurations demonstrate non-trivial patterns beyond conventional design intuition, offering new insights for engineering efficient robotic systems.

</details>


### [81] [Fast and Robust Teach-and-Repeat Navigation Using MixVPR Visual Place Recognition*](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09631)
*Václav Truhlařík,Tomáš Pivoňka,Libor Přeučil*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于MixVPR的高效示教-重复导航系统，在降低硬件需求的同时实现与现有先进方法相当的室内外定位与导航性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的示教-重复导航与视觉位置识别方案计算需求高，限制了其在机器人平台上的实际应用；而长期自主导航需要能在非结构化和动态环境中稳定运行。

Method: 构建一种新颖且高效的示教-重复导航系统，采用现代视觉位置识别方法MixVPR进行定位。

Result: 真实环境测试表明，系统可在室内和室外运行，其鲁棒性和导航精度与其他先进系统相当，同时硬件需求更低。

Conclusion: 该系统适合多种机器人平台和实际应用场景，提升了示教-重复导航的实用性与部署可行性。

Abstract: Teach-and-repeat navigation systems employing advanced visual place recognition techniques for localization exhibit key attributes for long-term mobile robot navigation, such as the ability to operate in unstructured and dynamic environments. However, existing solutions based on deep-learning techniques are computationally demanding, limiting their applicability. This work introduces a novel and efficient teach-and-repeat system built on the modern visual place recognition method MixVPR. Real-world testing demonstrated its ability to operate both indoors and outdoors, achieving robustness and navigation precision comparable to other state-of-the-art systems. In addition, its lower hardware requirements make it suitable for a wide range of robotic platforms and practical applications.

</details>


### [82] [Adaptive Code Generation for Controlling Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09588)
*Justus Flerlage,Thorsten Wittkopp,Alexander Acker,Odej Kao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种双AI机器人控制架构：LLM将高层自然语言意图转化为受正式机器人库约束且语法可验证的可执行代码，VLM通过蒸馏提供语义接地，并结合环境触发重规划与运行时监控，从而在动态未知环境中稳健实现复杂意图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统刚性命令库难以适应未知动态环境，自然语言是表达超出有限指令集的复杂目标的唯一媒介；LLM虽提供自然语言目标描述路径，但其集成面临形式化鸿沟、不可预测环境引发的分类学鸿沟，以及时序状态与进度感知难以维持等挑战。

Method: 采用双AI设计：LLM负责将高层意图翻译为可执行程序代码，并限制在正式机器人库内且受可验证语法约束；VLM通过蒸馏过程提供语义接地。框架还引入基于几何与语义阈值的环境驱动重规划触发、持续运行时监控以及自适应规划闭环，以增强鲁棒性。

Result: 在多个前沿模型上进行基准测试，结果表明该框架架构通过将生成式AI置于反应式、受约束的闭环中，能够在动态和未知环境中稳健完成复杂意图。

Conclusion: 将生成式AI接地到反应式、受约束的闭环控制架构，是弥合意图到行动、环境分类与时序状态维护等鸿沟的有效途径，有助于实现基于意图的机器人自主性。

Abstract: Deploying robots as Complex Adaptive Systems (CAS) in unknown and dynamic environments necessitates a transition from rigid command libraries toward intention-based autonomy, as natural language represents the only medium capable of articulating complex goals beyond the capacity of finite instruction sets. While Large Language Models (LLMs) offer a path toward natural language goal description, their integration introduces significant challenges: the formalization gap between imprecise intentions and executable actions, the taxonomy gap induced by unpredictable environments, and the challenge of maintaining temporal state and progress awareness. This work introduces an architectural framework that enables robotic control by leveraging generative AI. The system follows a dual-AI design: an LLM translates high-level intentions into executable program code restricted to a formal robotic library and constrained by verifiable syntax, while a Vision-Language Model (VLM) provides semantic grounding via a distillation process. To ensure robustness, the framework incorporates environment-driven replanning triggers based on geometric and semantic thresholds, complemented by continuous runtime monitoring and an adaptive planning loop. Benchmarked across frontier models, our framework architecture demonstrates that grounding generative AI in a reactive, constrained loop enables robust fulfillment of complex intentions in dynamic and unknown environments.

</details>


### [83] [Point It, Strike It: Direction-Conditioned Dynamic Manipulation of Deformable Linear Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09573)
*Yi Yang,Xiang Fei,Lehong Wang,Zilin Dai,Ruogu Li,Jiting Cai,Liyao Chang,Xinyi Yang,Henry Kou,Ruijie Fu,Lu Li,Howie Choset*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作研究一次摆动绳索击打任务，目标同时指定绳尖的3D位置与到达方向，并跨工作空间和不同绳索。作者提出DeformX2.0仿真器、TRACE数据生成、条件流匹配策略和RECAP残差校准策略；仿真达92.1%，真机上将位置目标5cm内成功率从72%提至87%，位置+方向目标10cm/10°内成功率从50%提至79%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有形变线性物体（DLO）目标条件操作多只把目标设为绳尖位置，但许多任务取决于绳尖如何到达，即到达方向。单摆绳索击打同时满足位置与方向目标很困难：绳索动力学难以建模、缺乏示教、不同摆动到达同一目标的可靠性不同，且仿真到现实差距不止在绳索本身。

Method: 作者首先扩展SOTA DLO仿真器DeformX：加入GPU加速、稳定的Cosserat杆求解器和横流气动模型，得到DeformX2.0，速度提升超过20000倍。然后提出TRACE（Trace-rooted Adaptive Cross-Entropy），通过将每个新目标热启动到已存储的、绳尖路径最接近该目标的摆动来生成击打数据；其代价惩罚绳索弯曲和突变绳尖运动，以偏向可重复摆动。基于该数据训练条件流匹配策略。最后提出RECAP（Residual Calibration Policy），用少量校准摆动拟合仿真器的绳索和装置参数，并用仿真中训练的校正策略自适应调整动作。

Result: 条件流匹配策略在仿真中达到92.1%准确率。在真实机器人上、三种绳索下，RECAP将位置目标5cm内成功率从72%提升到87%；对同时指定位置和到达方向的目标，将10cm与10°内成功率从50%提升到79%。

Conclusion: 通过高速高保真仿真、路径引导的数据生成、条件流匹配策略以及残差校准，方向感知的单摆绳索击打能够在真实机器人上获得显著性能提升，并有效缩小仿真到现实的差距。

Abstract: Goal-conditioned dynamic manipulation of deformable linear objects has mainly specified goals as positions for a rope tip to reach. Many tasks, however, depend on how the tip arrives. We therefore study single-swing rope striking with goals that specify the tip's 3D position and arrival direction, across the workspace and on different ropes. This is challenging because rope dynamics are hard to model, no demonstrations exist, distinct swings reach the same goal with different reliability, and the sim-to-real gap extends beyond the rope. To address these challenges, we extend the state-of-the-art DLO simulator DeformX with GPU acceleration, a stable Cosserat rod solver, and a cross-flow aerodynamic model, yielding DeformX2.0, which is more than $20{,}000\times$ faster. We then propose TRACE (Trace-rooted Adaptive Cross-Entropy), which generates striking data by warm-starting each new target from the stored swing whose tip path passes closest to it. Its cost penalizes rope bending and abrupt tip motion to favor repeatable swings. A conditional flow-matching policy trained on this data reaches 92.1% accuracy in simulation. Finally, we propose RECAP (Residual Calibration Policy), which fits the simulator's rope and rig parameters to a few calibration swings and adapts actions with a correction policy trained in simulation. On a real robot, across three ropes, RECAP raises success within 5cm from 72% to 87% for position goals, and within 10cm and 10° from 50% to 79% for goals that also specify the arrival direction.

</details>


### [84] [Targeted Modality Dropout for Real-Robot Manipulation Robust to Intermittent Vision Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09566)
*Genki Shikada,Kazuki Osamura,Masaru Ide,Tetsuya Ogata,Kanata Suzuki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Targeted Modality Dropout（TMD），利用注意力估计各模态依赖度并选择性丢弃主导模态，配合依赖分布熵正则，缓解多模态模仿学习策略对视觉等主导模态的过度依赖，在视觉缺失下保持机器人任务执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态模仿学习策略易在训练中过度依赖某主导模态（如视觉），推理时该模态缺失会导致策略执行失败；随机模态丢弃等常规方法不足，甚至在没有模态缺失时也会影响多任务表现。

Method: 用注意力估计每个模态的依赖程度，选择当前最主导模态进行定向丢弃；并对模态依赖分布施加熵正则，以平衡各模态利用；在双臂机械臂上进行真机评估。

Result: 真机实验中，视觉缺失时基线策略成功率大幅下降，而TMD仍能持续执行任务；对比随机选择丢弃模态且无熵正则的常规dropout，后者即便无视觉缺失也在许多任务上失败。

Conclusion: TMD通过定向丢弃主导模态与熵正则，可有效降低多模态模仿学习策略的模态过度依赖，提升缺失模态下的鲁棒性，优于随机dropout。

Abstract: Imitation learning policies that integrate multiple sensory modalities are prone to overreliance on a dominant modality, such as vision, during training, which can disrupt policy execution when that modality is lost at inference time. In this paper, we introduce Targeted Modality Dropout (TMD), in which the dependence on each modality is estimated using attention and the most dominant modality is selectively dropped. This is combined with entropy regularization over the dependence distribution. Through real-robot evaluation using a bimanual manipulator, we show that under vision loss the success rate of the baseline policy drops substantially, whereas TMD sustains task execution. In contrast, a conventional dropout that selects the dropped modality at random, without the entropy regularization, fails on many tasks even without vision loss.

</details>


### [85] [MagCilia: A Compact Magnetociliary Tactile Sensor with 3D Force Sensing for Robotic Contact Perception and Grasping Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09536)
*Yu Feng,Hao Wu,Haotian Guo,Haoming Liu,William Su,Jingxiang Guo,Jiankun Li,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Wen Jung Li,Jianshu Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出紧凑型磁纤毛触觉传感器MagCilia，结合柔性磁纤毛与霍尔传感器实现3D力感知，并用因果历史融合回归与频域特征完成抓取反馈和表面识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人抓取与表面探索需要同时测量法向/切向力并获取接触表面信息，因此需要一种紧凑且能同时实现多轴力重建与表面识别的触觉单元。

Method: 设计MagCilia：柔性磁纤毛结构结合霍尔传感器；采用准静态有限元分析研究多向载荷下的结构变形与磁响应；提出因果历史融合回归CHFR，将当前磁场测量与近期变化融合，从耦合磁通道重建力；通过机器人实验验证切向力引导夹爪调整与外部扰动下的多轴负载监测；对重建力提取频域特征以分类表面。

Result: 五折交叉验证（按标定记录分组）得到Fx、Fy、Fz的RMSE分别为0.40、0.57、0.69 N，决定系数分别为0.93、0.90、0.92；频域特征区分六类表面，在三折交叉验证（按采集会话分组）中准确率达99.39%；在线机器人演示识别全部六种测试表面。

Conclusion: 结果表明，单个紧凑触觉单元即可实现3D力重建、抓取反馈和表面识别，在机器人抓取与表面探索中具有应用潜力。

Abstract: Robotic grasping and surface exploration benefit from simultaneous measurement of normal and tangential forces and from surface information obtained through contact. Here, we present a compact magnetociliary tactile sensor (MagCilia) that combines a flexible magnetic-cilia structure with a Hall sensor for 3D force sensing. Quasi-static finite element analysis is used to investigate structural deformation and magnetic responses under multidirectional loading. To reconstruct forces from the coupled magnetic channels, we propose causal history fusion regression (CHFR), which combines current magnetic-field measurements with their recent changes. Five-fold cross-validation grouped by calibration record yields root-mean-square errors of 0.40, 0.57, and 0.69 N for Fx, Fy, and Fz, respectively, with corresponding coefficients of determination of 0.93, 0.90, and 0.92. Robotic experiments demonstrate tangential-force-guided gripper adjustment and multi-axis load monitoring under external perturbations. Frequency-domain features of the reconstructed forces distinguish six surface categories with 99.39% accuracy in three-fold cross-validation grouped by acquisition session. An online robotic demonstration additionally identifies all six tested surfaces. These results demonstrate 3D force reconstruction, grasping feedback, and surface recognition using a single compact tactile unit.

</details>


### [86] [Contact-Aware Imitation Learning Through Contact Factorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09533)
*Jiho Hong,Daeun Song,Sanghyun Kim,Mingyo Seo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FACE 是一种接触因素分解的模仿学习框架，通过接触相对坐标归一化交互力，并在执行时在线估计接触法线与摩擦尺度，从而在不更新策略参数的情况下适应接触条件并保持任务意图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的机器人操作难以泛化：表面几何、姿态和摩擦的微小变化会显著改变交互力，导致直接使用原始力测量的策略难以迁移到示范条件之外。

Method: 将任务意图与环境相关的接触因素分离；用归一化的接触相对坐标表示交互力；学习接触法线估计器和在线摩擦估计器；在执行中将力观测编码，并将策略输出解码为物理运动与力指令，从而适应当前接触条件且不更新策略参数。

Result: 在真实机器人接触丰富操作任务上，针对未见过的表面属性和几何变化进行评估，并与适配到该设定下的先前方法变体进行受控比较，验证了跨接触条件的稳健泛化能力。

Conclusion: FACE 能在保持预期任务行为的同时，根据当前接触条件调整物理执行方式，实现无需策略参数更新的跨接触条件泛化。

Abstract: Generalizable contact-rich manipulation requires robots to preserve intended task behavior while adapting its physical realization to changing contact conditions. However, interaction forces can vary substantially with small changes in surface geometry, orientation, and friction, making policies trained directly on raw force measurements difficult to transfer beyond demonstrated conditions. We introduce FACE, a contact-factorized imitation learning framework that separates intended task behavior from environment-dependent contact factors. Our representation expresses interaction forces in normalized, contact-relative coordinates, while a learned contact-normal estimator and an online friction estimator infer the local contact normal and effective friction scale. Together, these estimators enable force observations to be encoded and policy outputs to be decoded into physical motion and force commands during execution. In this way, FACE adapts execution to current contact conditions while preserving the intended task behavior, without updating the policy parameters. We evaluate FACE on real-robot contact-rich manipulation under unseen variations in surface properties and geometry, demonstrating robust generalization across contact conditions through controlled comparisons with variants that adapt prior approaches to our setting. Videos and additional materials can be found on the project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [87] [Co${}^{2}$Skill: Whole-Body Control via Skill Composition for Long-Horizon Human-Environment Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09291)
*Jeonghwan Kim,Hyeonwoo Kim,Hanbyul Joo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Co²Skill统一策略，将全身场景交互与灵巧物体操作整合到同一控制框架中，实现复杂环境下的序列任务与灵巧操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于物理的控制器虽能分别生成场景交互或灵巧操作行为，但通常将两者独立处理，难以满足复杂非结构化环境中类人灵巧性所需的全身场景交互与物体操作无缝集成。

Method: 基于预训练运动先验构建统一策略，使用任务与阶段依赖的观测掩码选择当前交互目标相关信息；提出目标条件loco-manipulation课程，结合部分参考引导的精确性与多初始状态探索，并支持超越演示轨迹的目标导向执行；进一步提出跨任务课程，联合训练单项技能与选定任务序列，跨任务边界保持物理状态，并在后续场景交互中保持抓取。

Result: 评估了坐下、站立、攀爬、上下楼梯、目标导向操作，以及序列执行和随机不同条件；并在室内环境中展示技能组合，说明其可在同一控制公式内集成。

Conclusion: Co²Skill支持序列任务执行，以及场景交互与物体操作的同时进行，并可统一组合多种全身灵巧操作技能。

Abstract: Achieving human-level dexterity in complex, unstructured environments requires the seamless integration of whole-body scene interaction and dexterous object manipulation skills. While existing physics-based controllers generate physically plausible behaviors in each domain, they largely address these two capabilities independently. In this paper, we present Co${}^{2}$Skill that integrates scene interaction and dexterous manipulation through a unified policy formulation. Built on a pretrained motion prior, the policy uses task and phase dependent observation masks to select information relevant to the current interaction goals. We introduce a goal-conditioned loco-manipulation curriculum that combines partial reference guidance for precision with exploration from varied initial states while allowing goal-directed execution beyond the demonstrated trajectories. We further introduce a cross-task curriculum that jointly trains individual skills and selected task sequences, preserving physical states across task boundaries and maintaining grasps during subsequent scene interactions. Together, these support sequential task execution and simultaneous scene interaction with object manipulation. We evaluate sitting, standing, climbing, stair traversal, and goal-directed manipulation, together with sequential execution and with random different conditions. Additionally, we demonstrate skill compositions in indoor environments, illustrating their integration within the same control formulation.

</details>


### [88] [LACE-CRAFT: Robot Co-Design with Actor Inheritance and Blackboard Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09283)
*Yuhan Wen,Jiawei Wang,Qixuan Zhang,Yusen Qin,Lan Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LACE-CRAFT 通过延续学习、策略自适应与多角色协同设计，在机器人形态-奖励联合搜索中复用既有控制经验，并在多个运动基准上显著优于 D2C。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人协同设计通常将形态搜索与策略学习耦合，但每个新设计都从零训练策略，会丢弃已获得的控制经验，导致样本效率低、学习成本高。

Method: 提出 LACE-CRAFT：LACE 在当前机器人上延续学习，同时将新形态-奖励对适配为挑战者；延续学习恢复 incumbent 的完整学习状态，挑战者用其 actor 参数与观测统计初始化，并由固定任务指标在两条分支和冻结 incumbent 间选择。CRAFT 通过共享实验记录和行为回放协调反馈、形态、奖励与集成四类角色，生成并交叉评审成对提案。生成式扩展将生成网格转为可编辑铰接模型，配置关节、执行器接口并一致更新仿真资产。

Result: 在五个运动基准上，三种评估种子的平均分数比 D2C 高 6.4%–91.9%。两种方法均在五轮中训练 30 个新形态-奖励对，LACE 额外使用四个延续训练单元。五任务消融考察策略继承与回放反馈。制作的原型展示了室内行走，并说明了从几何到硬件的流程。

Conclusion: 结果表明，复用策略与学习状态、结构化协同生成评审以及生成式仿真资产转换，能提升机器人协同设计的效率，并打通从形态生成到实物原型的流程。

Abstract: Robot co-design couples morphology search with policy learning, yet training every new design from scratch discards acquired control experience. We present LACE-CRAFT, which compares continued learning on the current robot with policy adaptation to new morphology-reward pairs. LACE resumes the incumbent's full learning state and initializes compatible challengers with its actor parameters and observation statistics. A fixed task metric selects among both branches and the frozen incumbent. CRAFT coordinates Feedback, Morphology, Reward, and Integration roles through shared experimental records and behavioral replays to generate and cross-review paired proposals. A generative extension converts generated meshes into editable articulated models with configured joints, actuator interfaces, and consistently updated simulation assets. Across five locomotion benchmarks, mean scores over three evaluation seeds are 6.4-91.9% higher than D2C. Both methods train 30 new morphology-reward pairs over five rounds; LACE additionally uses four continuation training units. Five-task ablations examine policy inheritance and replay-derived feedback. A fabricated prototype demonstrates indoor walking and illustrates the geometry-to-hardware workflow.

</details>


### [89] [Evaluating the Transfer of Co-Evolved Communication from 2D to 3D Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09280)
*Fernando Montes-Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究两个机器人智能体共进化的通信机制从离散二维模拟器迁移到具有真实物理的三维模拟器后，是否仍保留其功能角色，并考察回合时间预算、社会线索和两个共进化角色间不对称性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是：在简化2D环境中共同进化出的通信机制，能否在更真实的3D物理环境中继续发挥功能；同时需要识别影响迁移后性能差距的因素。

Method: 将2D中协同进化得到的通信机制迁移到3D物理模拟器；评估不同回合时间预算（2,000至6,000物理步）、社会线索的作用，以及两个共进化角色之间的不对称性；进行8次独立进化运行，并通过控制智能体间的处理顺序排除物理引擎伪影。

Result: 成功率随评估时间预算近似线性增加，在2,000至6,000物理步之间未出现平台期，说明较短回合可能低估控制器性能。在两种模拟器中，社会线索主要作为拥堵援助（jam-assistance）机制而非导航引导，且在2D中效果更明显。8次独立进化运行显示不对称方向一致，但幅度有变化。控制处理顺序后排除了物理引擎伪影。

Conclusion: 短回合评估可能低估训练后控制器性能；共进化通信机制迁移后保留部分功能，但仍存在性能差距，该差距可能受时间预算、社会线索功能变化和角色不对称等因素影响，需进一步分析迁移后剩余性能差距的来源。

Abstract: This work examines the transfer of a co-evolved communication mechanism between two robotic agents from a discrete two-dimensional (2D) simulator to a three-dimensional simulator with real physics (3D). The study focuses on whether a communication mechanism co-evolved in a 2D environment retains its functional role after transfer to a 3D physics-based simulator. To support this analysis, the effects of the episode time budget, the social cue, and the asymmetry between the two co-evolved roles were examined. The results indicate that the success rate increased approximately linearly with the evaluated time budgets, with no evidence of a plateau between 2,000 and 6,000 physics steps, suggesting that evaluations based on shorter episodes may underestimate the performance of the trained controllers. In both simulators, the social cue functioned primarily as a jam- assistance mechanism rather than as a navigation guide, although with a more pronounced effect in 2D. Analysis of eight independent evolutionary runs revealed a consistent direction of asymmetry, although its magnitude varied across runs. Controlling the processing order between agents allowed us to rule out an artifact of the physics engine. Finally, the results are discussed in terms of the factors that may contribute to the remaining performance gap observed after transfer.

</details>


### [90] [RoboRender: Robot-Oriented Video Generation for Visual Sim-to-Real Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09254)
*Huang Huang,Wensi Ai,Ziyu Chen,Youhui Wang,Zijian Du,Yang Liu,Jiaolong Yang,Li Fei-Fei,Jiajun Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboRender 将仿真轨迹通过以深度、语言指令和机器人掩码为条件的视频生成模型转为照片级真实 RGB 视频，与仿真状态/动作配对训练策略，实现零样本真机部署，真实任务平均成功率达 71%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真可大规模低成本生成机器人数据，但仿真与真实世界存在视觉差异，导致策略难以迁移；现有方法依赖中间表征，既可能丢失丰富语义信息，又需在部署时引入额外感知模块。

Method: 提出 RoboRender：训练面向机器人的视频生成模型，以仿真深度视频、语言指令和机器人 RGB 掩码视频为条件，在保留仿真器几何、机器人运动与动作标签的同时合成真实纹理、背景和干扰物；生成的 RGB 视频与仿真器提供状态、动作配对，用于训练可零样本部署的真实世界策略。

Result: 在机器人视频测试集上，生成质量优于深度条件视频生成基线；在抓取放置、铰接物体操作和移动操作的真实实验中，使用 RoboRender 生成数据训练的策略平均成功率为 71%，分别约为原始仿真渲染和常规视觉域随机化的 7.1 倍和 3.6 倍；每条仿真轨迹生成更多视频可提升策略性能，开抽屉任务成功率提升 65 个百分点。

Conclusion: 生成式视频渲染能够有效缓解视觉层面的 sim-to-real 差距，支持零样本策略迁移。

Abstract: Simulation enables large-scale, low-cost robot data generation, but policies trained in simulation often fail to transfer to the real world due to the sim-to-real visual discrepancies. Existing approaches often rely on intermediate representations, which can discard rich semantic information or require additional perception modules at deployment. We address this visual sim-to-real gap with RoboRender, a framework that converts simulated trajectories into photorealistic RGB videos for policy learning. RoboRender trains a robot-oriented video generation model conditioned on simulated depth videos, language instructions, and robot RGB mask videos, preserving simulator geometry, robot motion, and action labels while synthesizing realistic textures, backgrounds, and distractors. The generated RGB videos are paired with simulator-provided states and actions to train policies for zero-shot real-world deployment. On robot video test sets, our video model outperforms depth-conditioned video generation baselines in generation quality. In real-world experiments across pick-and-place, articulated-object manipulation, and mobile manipulation tasks, policies trained on RoboRender-generated data achieve a 71% average success rate, outperforming raw simulation renderings and conventional visual domain randomization by approximately 7.1x and 3.6x, respectively. We further show that policy performance improves with more generated videos per simulation trajectory, increasing opening-task success by 65 percentage points. These results demonstrate that generative video rendering mitigates the visual sim-to-real gap for zero-shot policy transfer. Project website: this https URL .

</details>


### [91] [Co-Evolving Robot Orchestrators and Policies through Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09228)
*Xilun Zhang,Maggie Wang,Erik Bauer,Hong-Xing Yu,Huang Huang,Jiajun Wu,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Robo-COP，让机器人部署中的 VLM 编排器与 VLA 策略共同演化，通过自采数据微调策略并仅在验证提升后采纳，从而突破冻结策略瓶颈，实现持续自我改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 策略在训练域内有效，但难以泛化到真实部署场景；用 VLM 编排器调用冻结策略只能规避失败，无法克服策略本身的局限，策略成为系统瓶颈。单独微调策略又会与旧行为下调优的编排器脱节。

Method: Robo-COP 在部署过程中让编排器与策略协同演化：系统从自身执行中整理技能示范，当数据能解决反复出现的失败时微调策略，并且仅当新策略在对应技能上确有提升后才采纳。

Result: 在十个模拟 RoboLab 任务上，平均留出成功率从冻结策略的 64.8% 提升至 73.8%，而固定计划微调且无验证仅达 65.8%；在三个真实任务上，留出成功率从 38.3% 提升至 50.0%。

Conclusion: Robo-COP 将部署转化为自我改进飞轮：机器人边做边学，每次执行改进都产生更优数据供下一轮学习，从而持续提升策略与整体系统性能。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies trained on large datasets are capable within their training domains, yet they still fail to generalize to the variety of situations a robot meets in real-world deployment. Agentic robot systems complement the policy with a vision-language model (VLM) orchestrator that learns when to call the policy, how to instruct it, and when to use scripted skills instead. However, because the harness is built around a frozen policy that has limited language steerability, the orchestrator can avoid the policy's failures but never overcome them. The policy becomes the bottleneck of the whole system. Fine-tuning the policy can remove this bottleneck, but updating it alone decouples it from an orchestrator tuned to its old behavior. We propose Robo-COP, in which the orchestrator and policy co-evolve during deployment. Robo-COP curates skill demonstrations from its own executions, fine-tunes the policy when this data can address recurring failures, and adopts each new policy only after it improves the skills it was trained for. Across ten simulated RoboLab tasks, Robo-COP raises mean held-out success from 64.8% to 73.8% over the same harness with a frozen policy, while fine-tuning on a fixed schedule without verification reaches only 65.8%. On three real-world tasks, Robo-COP raises held-out success from 38.3% to 50.0%. Robo-COP turns deployment into a self-improving flywheel in which robots learn by doing, with each improvement in execution producing better data for the next round of learning. Videos and code are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [92] [Fast Planning for Multi-object Multi-target Throwing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09224)
*Zhengming Zhu,Yang Liu,Xiao Gao,Aude Billard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出多物体多目标抛掷（MOMT）的统一规划框架：离线构建可行抛掷集模型，在线在5ms内匹配筛选出可行抛掷序列，实现一次挥动抛掷多个物体；仿真中两物体协调抛掷减少执行时间达46%，实物两物体减少29%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抛掷可扩大工作空间并加速流程，是提升物流与仓储自动化效率的潜在技术，但单物体抛掷吞吐量有限。为显著提高吞吐，需在一次挥动中抛掷多个物体，利用拟人手的多自由度，而难点在于快速生成既快又可行的抛掷动作，需在短轨迹时长与短规划时间之间取得复杂折衷。

Method: 采用两阶段方法。离线阶段，结合物体的倒飞动力学与机器人的运动学、动力学，构建可行抛掷集模型；在线阶段，通过快速解匹配与筛选物体的有效脱离状态和机器人的可行状态，组合成抛掷序列，规划耗时小于5ms。在配备多指手的7自由度机械臂上验证。

Result: 仿真中，协调的双物体抛掷相比独立单物体规划可将执行时间最多减少46%，且扩展到三物体时该提升仍保持。实物实验中，两物体任务执行时间减少29%，与理论50%的差距归因于抛掷间过渡开销。当目标位置在执行中途随机改变时，系统在100ms延迟内重新规划并到达新目标，且无需停止机器人。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了首个面向MOMT抛掷的统一规划框架，仿真证明其可扩展到多物体，实物验证了两物体任务的可行性，推动了高通量机器人操作的前沿发展。

Abstract: Robot throwing has emerged as a promising technique for improving efficiency in logistics and warehouse automation, by enlarging the workspace and speeding up the process. To significantly increase the throwing system's throughput, we develop strategies for throwing multiple objects in one swipe. Such multi-object multi-target throwing (MOMT) leverages the large degrees of freedom of anthropomorphic hands. The key is to quickly generate fast and feasible throwing motions, which involves a complex trade-off between short trajectory duration and short planning time. We solve this problem in two stages. Offline, we build a model of the feasible set by combining object's inverted flying dynamics and the robot's kinematics and dynamics. Online, we generate feasible throws through fast solution matching and filtering of object's valid detach state and robot's feasible state that can compose sequences of throws in less than 5 ms. We validate the framework on a 7-DoF manipulator equipped with a multi-fingered hand. In simulation, coordinated two-object throwing reduces execution time by up to 46% compared to independent single-object planning, and this improvement is maintained when scaling to three objects. Real-world experiments with two objects confirm a 29% reduction; the remaining gap to the theoretical 50% is attributed to inter-throw transition overhead. When target positions are randomly changed mid-execution, the system re-plans and successfully reaches the new targets within 100 ms latency without stopping the robot. These results establish the first unified planning framework for MOMT throwing -- demonstrating scalability to multiple objects in simulation and real-world feasibility on two-object tasks -- advancing the frontier of high-throughput robotic manipulation. A video summarizing the method and the hardware experiments is available at this https URL

</details>


### [93] [Belief-Space Planning with Planner-Conditioned Estimator Error under Intermittent Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09207)
*Peter M. Donley,Joseph L. Moore*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种信念空间规划方法，沿候选轨迹预测、传播并惩罚规划器条件化的估计器误差，将仿射误差动力学与保留随机校正不确定性的矩递推结合，并在倾转旋翼VTOL舰船甲板着陆仿真中验证其优于仅协方差规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信念空间规划器若与单独设计或现成估计器分离，规划器可能掌握估计器未观测到的状态相关信息。因此，即使估计器在自身信息下最小化均方误差，在规划器信息条件下其误差均值仍可能非零。当未来校正间歇且随机时，接受与拒绝校正事件的误差均值差异会引入额外不确定性，而传统零均值假设会忽略这些项，导致规划风险建模不准确。

Method: 提出规划器条件化的估计器误差模型：用仿射误差动力学描述误差演化，并用矩递推保留随机校正事件引起的不确定性；在指定运行区间内预测估计器误差动力学，并将预测误差矩纳入任务加权二次风险目标，用于滚动时域规划。仿真场景为倾转旋翼VTOL飞行器在舰船甲板上着陆。

Result: 对命令盲EKF，条件化规划器信息使误差预测损失相对规划器无知模型降低13.4%，且预测能力强烈依赖运行区间与预测时域。在16组配对闭环试验中，规划器将终端任务指标中位数从1.60降至0.99；对2s以后的估计器误差预测在1.3倍以内，而仅协方差规划低估达5.7倍。

Conclusion: 显式预测并惩罚规划器条件化估计器误差，可改善信念空间规划的风险建模与闭环性能，尤其在随机间歇校正和长时域场景下，优于忽略规划器信息或仅使用协方差的规划方法。

Abstract: Belief-space planners with separately designed or off-the-shelf estimators may have access to state-relevant information the estimator does not observe. Consequently, even an estimator that minimizes mean-squared error under its own information can have a nonzero error mean when conditioned on planner information. When future corrections are intermittent and stochastic, differences between accepted and rejected error means introduce additional uncertainty terms to correction events that zero-mean assumptions ignore. In this paper, we propose a belief-space planning approach that predicts, propagates, and penalizes planner-conditioned estimator error along evaluated trajectories. A planner-conditioned estimator-error model combines affine error dynamics with a moment recursion that preserves stochastically-induced correction uncertainty. We describe a method by which to predict estimator error dynamics within specified operating regimes and incorporate predicted error moments into a task-weighted quadratic risk objective. We evaluate the approach in simulation for a tilt-rotor VTOL landing on a ship deck using receding-horizon planning. We find that conditioning on planner information reduced error-prediction loss for a command-blind EKF by 13.4% relative to a planner-ignorant model, with strongly regime and horizon-dependent forecasting abilities. In a small set of 16 paired closed-loop trials, the planner lowered the median terminal task gauge from 1.60 to 0.99, and predicted estimator error beyond 2s within a factor of 1.3, versus a 5.7-fold underprediction by covariance-only planning.

</details>


### [94] [Embedded Evaluation of Task Admission Coalescing in Decentralized Multi-Robot Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09187)
*James Lott,Vahraz Honary*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文评估任务准入合并机制：在多机器人任务分配器中，通过批处理/延迟接纳新任务来降低处理器工作量，同时权衡目标服务延迟，并发现其效果取决于到达强度、分配器行为和计算平台成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态任务中的多机器人任务分配器通常即时接纳新发布任务，可能导致每来一个任务就触发一次分配计算，造成不必要的处理器负载；因此需要研究任务准入合并在控制分配器计算量时，如何影响目标服务延迟。

Method: 以CBAA、ACBBA、PI和HIPC为代表MRTA套件，比较Eager即时准入与阈值2、4、8任务以及带10秒等待上限的4任务策略，并在三种任务到达率下测试。实验包括3000任务AGX Orin campaign并测量计算延迟、3000个配对的零计算任务，以及96任务Pololu 3pi+ RP2040分配器硬件在环campaign。

Result: AGX实验中，合并在48个评估条件中的31个减少分配器调用和处理器工作量，但节省幅度因分配器而异。稀疏到达下主要代价出现：四任务批处理使在线目标平均延迟增加20.08–23.57秒，主要来自准入等待。有界准入在12个分配器-负载条件中的10个降低平均工作量，例如高到达率HIPC降低19.35%工作量，但平均延迟增加1.40秒。RP2040实验表明，处理器约束越紧，这种权衡可能越有利；在四个匹配的中等到达率HIPC场景中，Count b=4使RP2040平均工作量降低59.85%、服务延迟降低39.47%，而对应AGX案例延迟增加27.69%。

Conclusion: 任务准入合并的有用性取决于任务到达强度、具体分配器行为以及执行平台上计算成本的相对代价。

Abstract: Multi-robot task allocators in dynamic missions commonly admit newly released tasks immediately, potentially invoking allocation for each new arrival. We evaluate task-admission coalescing as a mechanism for controlling allocator processor work while accounting for target-service latency, using CBAA, ACBBA, PI, and HIPC as a representative MRTA suite. The study comprises a 3,000-mission AGX Orin campaign with measured computation delay, 3,000 paired zero-compute missions, and a 96-mission Pololu 3pi+ RP2040 allocator hardware-in-the-loop campaign. Immediate (Eager) admission is compared with thresholds of two, four, and eight tasks and a four-task policy with a 10-s waiting bound across three arrival rates. Across the AGX experiments, coalescing reduces both allocator calls and processor work in 31 of 48 evaluated conditions, although the magnitude of the saving varies by allocator. The principal cost appears under sparse arrivals, where four-task batching increases online-target mean latency by 20.08-23.57 s, predominantly through admission waiting. Bounded admission reduces mean work in ten of twelve allocator-load conditions, including a 19.35% reduction with a 1.40-s mean latency increase for high-arrival HIPC. RP2040 experiments show that this tradeoff can become more favorable as processor constraints tighten. Across four matched medium-arrival HIPC scenarios, Count b=4 reduces mean RP2040 work by 59.85% and service latency by 39.47%, while the corresponding AGX cases increase latency by 27.69%. These results show that the usefulness of task-admission coalescing depends on arrival intensity, allocator-specific behavior, and the relative cost of computation on the execution platform.

</details>


### [95] [CAP: Codebook-Aligned Prediction for Tokenized Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09178)
*Haoran Chen,Jingtian Ji,Samuel Wheeler,Kaylene Caswell Stocking,Matthew Walter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Codebook-Aligned Prediction (CAP)，通过将动作 tokenizer 的码本向量直接复用为策略的分类原型，保留 tokenizer 学到的潜在结构，从而提升下游策略的任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 tokenizer 的策略通常忽略 tokenizer 学到的潜在码本结构：tokenization 后，策略把离散 token 当作互不相关的类别索引，并从零学习新的分类器，导致 tokenizer 中已有的结构化信息被丢弃。

Method: CAP 在不改变 tokenizer 和策略主干的前提下，直接复用 tokenizer 的码本向量作为策略的类别原型，使策略分类头与 tokenizer 的潜在码本空间对齐。

Result: 在四类量化器、三个仿真基准和两个真实机器人任务中，CAP 相比标准 token 分类头持续提升任务成功率，同时保持 tokenizer 及其重建质量不变。分析表明，该提升并非来自更高的 token 准确率或仅分类头变化，而是因为复用码本让策略获得 token 间潜在结构信息，使 token 预测误差在动作空间中更温和，并改善策略主干学到的表示。

Conclusion: 动作 tokenizer 不仅提供离散目标，还学到了对动作有用的潜在结构；训练下游策略时应保留并利用这些结构。

Abstract: Action tokenization converts continuous robot actions into discrete symbols that can be modeled autoregressively. However, existing tokenizer-based policies typically ignore the tokenizer's learned latent code structure: after tokenization, the policy treats tokens as unrelated class indices and learns a new classifier from scratch. We show that this discarded structure is valuable. We introduce Codebook-Aligned Prediction (CAP), a method that directly reuses the tokenizer's code vectors as policy class prototypes while leaving the tokenizer and policy backbone otherwise unchanged. Across four quantizer families, three simulation benchmarks, and two real-robot tasks, CAP consistently improves task success over standard token classification heads while holding the tokenizer (and therefore its reconstruction quality) fixed. Our analysis further shows that these gains are not explained by higher token accuracy or changes in the policy head alone. Instead, reusing the tokenizer codebook provides the policy with valuable information about the tokenizer's learned latent structure across tokens, making token prediction errors more benign in action space and improving the representations learned by the policy backbone. These results suggest that action tokenizers learn useful action-aware latent structure beyond discrete targets that should be preserved when training downstream policies.

</details>


### [96] [Beyond Reconstruction: What Matters in Action Tokenization for Robot Policies?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09170)
*Haoran Chen,Jingtian Ji,Samuel Wheeler,Kaylene Caswell Stocking,Matthew Walter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ProAct 是一种动作分词器训练方法，通过策略性地增强重建目标来提升下游策略的可预测性与鲁棒性，在多个机器人基准上平均提升 11.3 个百分点，并适用于 VLA 策略和真实世界操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归动作令牌策略需要动作分词器将离散令牌序列映射为连续控制动作。仅靠重建目标不足以确保下游机器人策略成功，因为策略还需为新观测预测正确令牌，且未见过的令牌预测仍能解码为合理动作，这些性质未被重建目标直接激励。

Method: 提出 Predictable and Robust Action Tokenization (ProAct)，一种策略无关的分词器训练方法，仅使用动作数据集，通过策略性增强重建目标来改善下游可预测性和鲁棒性。

Result: 在 Robomimic、LIBERO、RoboTwin 基准及多种分词器架构上，ProAct 将 rollout 成功率平均提升 11.3 个百分点；在视觉-语言-动作策略和真实世界机器人操作中分别平均提升 21.8 和 36.7 个百分点。

Conclusion: 有效的动作分词应被设计为平衡保真度、可预测性和鲁棒性的策略接口，而不仅仅是重建问题。

Abstract: Autoregressive action-token policies such as vision-language-action models require action tokenizers to translate discrete token sequences into precise control actions in continuous space. Many action tokenizers learn the mapping between tokens and actions via a reconstruction objective. However, as we show through extensive analysis, sufficiently accurate action reconstruction is only one part of what makes a downstream robot policy successful. It is also critical that the policy is able to predict the right tokens for new observations, and that unseen policy token predictions still decode into reasonable actions. These properties are downstream of tokenizer training and are not directly incentivized by a reconstruction objective alone. In this work, we introduce Predictable and Robust Action Tokenization (ProAct), a tokenizer training method that strategically augments reconstruction with the goal of improving downstream predictability and robustness. ProAct is policy-agnostic and uses only action datasets for training. Across the Robomimic, LIBERO, and RoboTwin benchmarks and a diverse set of tokenizer architectures, ProAct improves rollout success by an average of 11.3 percentage points. These improvements also translate to vision-language-action policies and real-world robotic manipulation, yielding average gains of 21.8 and 36.7 percentage points, respectively. These results suggest that effective action tokenization should be designed as a policy interface that balances fidelity, predictability, and robustness, rather than as a reconstruction problem alone.

</details>


### [97] [geodex: A Library for Motion Planning on Riemannian Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09165)
*Phone Thiha Kyaw,Ben Wei,Sepehr Samavi,Miguel Angel Rogel Garcia,Jonathan Kelly*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出开源C++20库geodex，通过统一采样规划接口支持在带用户定义Riemann度量的多种流形与机器人构型空间中进行避障运动规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流形优化库缺少避障规划，通用运动规划库又不支持将配置相关Riemann度量作为驱动距离、插值和测地线的几何；而利用构型空间内蕴几何（曲率与配置相关代价）可生成更短、更低能耗、更自然的轨迹。

Method: geodex将流形、Riemann度量、retraction和采样器暴露为独立可互换组件，并统一到单一基于采样的运动规划接口；支持R^n、T^n、S^n、SO(2)、SE(2)、SO(3)、SE(3)等矩阵Lie群、其乘积空间及关节机器人构型空间，并可搭配用户定义Riemann度量。

Result: 同一规划器无需修改即可运行于上述各类空间；库已公开提供文档、测试和可复现基准套件。

Conclusion: geodex填补了在障碍物环境中按配置相关Riemann几何进行运动规划的工具空白，为内蕴几何驱动的避障轨迹规划提供开源、可扩展且可复现的解决方案。

Abstract: Planning motions that respect the intrinsic geometry of a robot's configuration space, including its curvature and a configuration-dependent notion of cost, yields shorter, lower-energy, and more natural trajectories than planning under the ambient flat metric. Existing libraries for optimization on manifolds provide rich geometric primitives but do not plan around obstacles. While general-purpose motion planning libraries support many state spaces and custom distance functions, they do not yet treat a configuration-dependent Riemannian metric as the geometry that drives distance, interpolation, and geodesics. We present geodex, an open-source C++20 library with Python bindings. The library exposes the manifold, its Riemannian metric, the retraction, and the sampler as independent, interchangeable components through a single sampling-based motion planning interface. The same planner runs unchanged on canonical spaces $\mathbb{R}^n$, $\mathbb{T}^n$, $\mathbb{S}^n$, matrix Lie groups such as $SO(2)$, $SE(2)$, $SO(3)$, and $SE(3)$, products of these spaces, and articulated-robot configuration spaces, each equipped with a user-defined Riemannian metric. We make geodex publicly available with documentation, tests, and a reproducible benchmark suite.

</details>


### [98] [A Unified Kinematic Representation Enables Reusable Biological Joint Moment Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09150)
*Jinwoo Hwang,Ilseung Park,Changseob Song,Vu Phan,Eni Halilaj,Inseung Kang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出以关节运动学作为中间表示，用开源生物力学数据训练生物关节力矩估计器，并在髋外骨骼、膝外骨骼和IMU上验证跨平台迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用于估计生理状态尤其是生物关节力矩的数据驱动模型广泛用于外骨骼控制，但通常与设备特定传感器配置耦合，限制了控制器迁移和开源生物力学数据集的使用。

Method: 以关节角度和角速度构成的关节运动学作为中间表示，解耦硬件特定传感与下游生物关节力矩估计；估计器仅用开源生物力学数据训练，并在髋外骨骼、膝外骨骼和IMU传感套件于平地、上坡、下坡行走中获取的运动学上评估，覆盖双侧髋、膝、踝关节。

Result: 使用髋外骨骼运动学时RMSE为0.17 Nm/kg、R2为0.79；使用膝外骨骼运动学时RMSE为0.19 Nm/kg、R2为0.62；使用IMU运动学时RMSE为0.15 Nm/kg、R2为0.85。

Conclusion: 关节运动学使仅用开源数据训练的估计器可跨不同可穿戴平台运行；该框架有望减少目标设备数据采集，并支持外骨骼控制与可穿戴生物力学中可迁移的生物关节力矩估计。

Abstract: Objective: Data-driven models that estimate physiological states, particularly biological joint moments, are widely used in exoskeleton control. However, these estimators are often coupled to device-specific sensor configurations, limiting controller transfer and the use of open-source biomechanics datasets. Methods: We proposed joint kinematics as an intermediate representation that decouples hardware-specific sensing from downstream biological joint moment estimation. A joint-moment estimator using joint angles and angular velocities was trained exclusively on open-source biomechanics data and evaluated using kinematics from a hip exoskeleton, knee exoskeleton, and inertial measurement unit (IMU) sensor suite during level-ground, ramp-ascent, and ramp-descent walking. Results: The estimator achieved an root mean square error (RMSE) of 0.17 Nm/kg and coefficient of determination (R2) of 0.79 using hip exoskeleton kinematics, 0.19 Nm/kg and 0.62 using knee exoskeleton kinematics, and 0.15 Nm/kg and 0.85 using kinematics derived from IMUs across bilateral hip, knee, and ankle joints. Conclusion: Joint kinematics enabled an estimator trained only on open-source data to operate across distinct wearable platforms. Significance: This framework may reduce target-device data collection and support transferable biological joint moment estimation for exoskeleton control and wearable biomechanics.

</details>


### [99] [World Models Dream of Success: Diagnosing and Repairing Failure Insensitivity in Robot World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09134)
*Jiuyi Xu,Xiao Hu,Meida Chen,Peng Gao,Yang Ye,Yangming Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 机器人世界模型需区分成功与失败动作以支持策略评估、规划和合成数据生成，但已发布检查点对动作变化不敏感且将失败预测为成功。CureWM 通过执行验证的反事实回放数据，在不改变架构与训练目标的情况下对已发布模型进行事后修复，显著降低乐观偏差并改善成功–失败判别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人世界模型用于策略评估、规划与合成数据生成，要求预测能够区分成功动作与失败动作。然而，来自两个架构家族的四个已发布检查点表现出对动作变化的弱敏感性，并在已验证失败上给出类成功预测。尽管近期工作已将失败纳入模型训练，但哪些数据能够在不改变已发布检查点架构或训练目标的前提下修复模型，仍未得到充分探索。

Method: 提出 CureWM：从成功演示出发，在严重度网格上构造替代动作，通过仿真或硬件执行验证其结果，得到失败样本与存活成功样本；随后将这些反事实失败、存活成功与常规演示数据一起用于微调已发布模型。该构造从共享起始上下文提供受控的动作对比。

Result: 在 484 个留出的 LIBERO 失败反事实上，乐观率从使用官方数据微调后的 80% 降至四个独立微调 CureWM 模型的 30–43%（均值 38%）。在物理机械臂的两次独立评估中，潜在距离诊断将失败预测判为类成功的比例从仅用成功演示微调后的 90% 降至 CureWM 的 33%。在每任务失败数、成功回放数据与训练预算匹配时，反事实失败带来 0.124 的成功–失败价值差，而新采集的在线策略失败为 0.014。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持使用执行验证的反事实回放进行事后修复，并表明在评估降低乐观偏差时，必须同时考察成功–失败判别能力。

Abstract: Robot world models support policy evaluation, planning, and synthetic data generation, but these applications require predictions that distinguish successful actions from failures. Across four released checkpoints from two architecture families, we observe weak sensitivity to action changes and success-like predictions on verified failures. Although recent work incorporates failures into model training, which data can repair released checkpoints without changing their architecture or training objective still remains underexplored. To this end, we introduce CureWM, which constructs alternative actions from successful demonstrations across a severity grid, verifies their outcomes through execution in simulation or on hardware, and fine-tunes released models on the resulting failures and surviving successes alongside nominal demonstrations. This construction provides controlled action contrasts from shared starting contexts. On 484 held-out LIBERO failure counterfactuals, optimism falls from 80\% after fine-tuning on the official data to 30--43\% across four independently fine-tuned CureWM models (38\% mean). In two separate evaluations on a physical robot arm, failure predictions scored as success-like by a latent-distance diagnostic decrease from 90\% after fine-tuning on successful demonstrations alone to 33\% with CureWM. With failure counts per task, successful replay data, and training budget matched, counterfactual failures yield a success--failure value gap of 0.124, compared with 0.014 for freshly collected on-policy failures. These findings support execution-verified counterfactual replay for post-hoc repair and show why reduced optimism must be evaluated alongside success--failure discrimination. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [100] [FlashNeRD: Performance-First Contact-Rich Neural Robot Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09130)
*Mohammadmehdi Ataei,Mohammad Amin Nabian,Jie Xu,Miles Macklin,Yashraj Narang,Eric Heiden,Octi Zhang,Yunrong Guo,Ruben D'Sa,Oliver Hennigh,Gavriel State,Sanjay Choudhry*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FlashNeRD 是一种并行流式学习的机器人动力学模型，消除 NeRD 的速度、接触建模和双向耦合限制，在多种机器人上实现更快更准的动力学、策略学习与规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习动力学模型有望比解析物理仿真更快、可微且易适配真实数据。NeRD 保留解析碰撞检测并用学习模型替代机器人数值动力学，但仍存在三点不足：相对其所学仿真器加速有限、仅在预定义点接受接触、与被操作物体缺乏双向耦合。

Method: FlashNeRD 采用并行流式架构提升单步预测速度与精度；使用编码器接受任意位置发生的接触；并与解析求解器仿真的物体实现双向耦合，从而移除 NeRD 的三项限制。

Result: 在五种机器人上，实验显示动力学更快更准、策略学习更快、推理时规划更快。在三种机器人上，其动力学模型比优化后的 NeRD 最多快 55 倍，且长时滚动更准确。该加速扩展到策略学习：PPO 在 34 秒内训练出 ANYmal 运动策略，比解析仿真器快 3.8 倍，比优化 NeRD 快 2.7 倍。使用 DIAL-MPC 时，机器人爬上全部六个测试平台，而固定接触 NeRD 仅完成一个，规划时间约为解析仿真器的一半。以 FlashNeRD 训练的立方体重定向策略的完成数量与仿真器训练策略的目标完成数相差在 2% 以内。

Conclusion: FlashNeRD 通过并行流式学习、任意接触编码和双向耦合，克服了 NeRD 的关键限制，为机器人提供快速、准确且可扩展的学习动力学，并有效加速策略学习与基于采样的规划。

Abstract: Compared with analytical physics, learned dynamics models promise robot simulation that is faster, inherently differentiable, and easily adaptable to real data. Neural Robot Dynamics (NeRD) pursues this by keeping collision detection analytical and replacing a simulator's numerical dynamics for the robot with a learned model. Three limitations remain. NeRD offers little speedup over the simulator it learned from, accepts contact only at predefined points, and has no two-way coupling with objects it manipulates. FlashNeRD removes all three with a parallel streaming architecture that makes each prediction faster and more accurate, an encoder that accepts contacts wherever they occur, and two-way coupling with objects simulated by analytical solvers. Experiments across five robots show faster and more accurate dynamics, faster policy learning, and faster inference-time planning. Across three robots, FlashNeRD's dynamics model is up to $55\times$ faster than an optimized NeRD and more accurate over long rollouts. This speedup extends to policy learning, where PPO trains an ANYmal locomotion policy in 34 seconds, $3.8\times$ faster than the analytical simulator and $2.7\times$ faster than an optimized NeRD. With DIAL-MPC, a sampling-based MPC method, the robot climbs all six test platforms where fixed-contact NeRD manages one, at approximately half the analytical simulator's planning time. Cube-reorientation policies trained with FlashNeRD complete within 2% of the simulator-trained policy's target count.

</details>


### [101] [Workhorse: Learning Robust Whole-Body Humanoid Loco-Manipulation from Human Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09117)
*Songbo Hu,Qiayuan Liao,Yufeng Chi,Kevin Zakka,Yakun Sophia Shao,Pieter Abbeel,Koushil Sreenath*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Workhorse 通过无机器人的人类演示学习接触丰富的全身操作：视觉规划器预测躯干、双腕和双脚五端目标，RL 全身跟踪器在 Unitree G1 上执行，仿真箱体分拣成功率达 77%，40 N·s 推动下为 64%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人仍难以仅凭第一人称 RGB 和本体感觉规划接触丰富的全身操作，且依赖机器人遥操作或重定向的数据成本高。

Method: 从无机器人的人类演示中学习：视觉规划器预测躯干、双腕、双脚五端位姿；强化学习全身跟踪器在机器人上跟随这些目标。两个策略分别在同一记录人类姿态上训练，无需重定向；并互相增强训练数据，以模仿对方在部署时的误差。

Result: 在真实 Unitree G1 上完成用双手和踢腿分拣箱子、接住抛来的箱子、推倒并爬上行李箱；箱体分拣中可被人推或拿走箱子后恢复。仿真演示房间中箱体分拣成功率 77%，在 40 N·s 推动下为 64%；用相同演示重训后，仿真第二个人形机器人无推动下分拣成功率为 83%。

Conclusion: Workhorse 表明可从机器人无关的人类演示中学习接触丰富的全身人形操作，并通过双策略误差增强获得对扰动的恢复能力与跨本体迁移潜力。

Abstract: Humanoid robots still struggle to plan contact-rich whole-body manipulation from egocentric RGB and proprioception. Workhorse learns such manipulation from robot-free human demonstrations. A visual planner predicts five-link targets: the poses of the torso, both wrists, and both feet. A reinforcement-learning whole-body tracker follows them on the robot. Both policies train separately on the same recorded human poses, without retargeting. We augment the training data of each policy to imitate the errors that the other makes at deployment. On a real Unitree G1, Workhorse sorts boxes with its hands and a kick, catches a thrown box, and topples and climbs a suitcase. During box sorting, we show recoveries after a person pushes the robot or takes the box away. In a simulated copy of the demonstration room, the system completes box sorting in 77% of episodes, and in 64% under 40 N.s pushes. With both policies retrained from the same demonstrations, a simulated second humanoid completes box sorting in 83% of episodes without pushes.

</details>


### [102] [MimicX: Policy-in-the-Loop Supervision Refinement for Video-Driven Humanoid Motion Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09055)
*Shuaijun Liu,Chenglong Zhang,Xuhao Liu,Feiyang You,Yifan Liao,Shuyang Hao,Chaozhe Zhang,Chengyu Wu,Zhen Sun,Ningxin Su*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 MimicX：一种策略在环框架，利用执行反馈优化视频驱动的人形运动跟踪，将物理执行中的失败转化为可操作的监督信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视频能为人形学习提供丰富运动目标，但视觉上合理的参考动作在基于物理的执行中仍可能持续失败；这些失败揭示了训练监督应调整之处。

Method: 从重建并重定向的运动出发，MimicX 定位困难转段与受影响身体区域，联合调整跟踪目标与重置课程以支持策略继续；通过重复 rollout 验证，在跟踪守护下选择以执行为优先的改进。MimicX-HLoop 通过异构执行加速反馈。

Result: 在四个核心视频任务上，相较 Fixed Reference 基线，MimicX 持续提升跟踪精度与 Robust Execution Horizon；任务平均身体跟踪误差降低 25.7%，执行时域增加 255.6%。额外视频、运动参考与碰撞场景研究进一步评估方法。

Conclusion: MimicX 将策略失败转化为可操作的监督，用于决定应优化什么以及保留哪种优化。

Abstract: Human videos provide rich motion targets for humanoid learning, yet visually plausible references can still produce persistent failures under physics-based execution. These failures reveal where training supervision should change. We present MimicX, a policy-in-the-loop framework that uses execution feedback to refine video-driven humanoid motion tracking. Starting from reconstructed and retargeted motion, MimicX localizes difficult transitions and affected body regions, then jointly adapts tracking objectives and the reset curriculum for policy continuation. Repeated rollout verification selects execution-priority improvements subject to tracking guards. Across four core video tasks, MimicX consistently improves tracking accuracy and Robust Execution Horizon relative to the Fixed Reference baseline. Task-averaged results show a 25.7% reduction in body-tracking error and a 255.6% increase in execution horizon. Additional video, motion-reference, and collision-scene studies evaluate the method beyond the core tasks, while MimicX-HLoop accelerates feedback through heterogeneous execution. Overall, MimicX turns policy failure into actionable supervision for deciding what to refine and which refinement to retain.

</details>


### [103] [PhysEvo: Astra Can Act, Let It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08995)
*Wenqing Tian,Zeyu Zhang,Zhaocheng Liu,Fengwei Liu,Qiang Liu,Liang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 PhysEvo：一种围绕单个冻结模型的物理递归自我改进框架，通过任务智能体与元智能体的闭环，将机器人执行轨迹转化为持久、可测试的工具与技能修订，在不更新模型权重、不单独训练动作策略的情况下提升操作能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Astra 虽能行动，但可靠操作取决于其观测与控制世界所依赖的系统。需要让冻结模型也能利用行动后果持续改进，而非依赖一次性策略或权重更新。

Method: 任务智能体执行机器人任务；元智能体利用生成轨迹诊断失败、修订工具与技能并测试修正。元智能体还可改进自身诊断工具，使保留的修订同时支持后续行动与后续自我改进。该过程发展关节级控制、证据寻求式观测和可复用操作技能，无需模型权重更新或单独训练动作策略。

Result: 在 42 个 RoboDojo 任务上，对保留的任务特定部署版本进行留出布局评估，五维平均得分 68.14/100，成功率 62.00%；对比 RoboDawn 的一次性 Astra 智能体为 47.17%，是本文比较中最强的已发表参考。在 8 个挑战直接 Astra 的操作任务上，PhysEvo 成功率为 55.00%，直接 Astra 参考为 1.25%。将仿真演化的 harness 部署到 AgileX PiPER 并继续技能修订，在 5 个真实任务、25 次试验中取得平均 90.60/100 和 84.00% 成功率。

Conclusion: PhysEvo 将行动后果转化为对冻结模型如何行动与如何改进的持久且可测试的改变，实现了物理递归自我改进。

Abstract: Astra can act, yet reliable manipulation depends on the system through which it observes and controls the world. We introduce PhysEvo, a framework for physical recursive self-improvement (RSI) around a single frozen model.
