A task agent executes robot tasks; a meta-agent uses the resulting trajectories to diagnose failures, revise tools and skills, and test corrections. The meta-agent can also improve its own diagnostic tools, so retained revisions support both later action and later self-improvement. This process develops joint-level control, evidence-seeking observation, and reusable manipulation skills without model-weight updates or a separately trained action policy. Across 42 RoboDojo tasks, held-out-layout evaluation of retained task-specific deployment versions yields a five-dimension average score of 68.14/100 and 62.00% success, compared with 47.17% for RoboDawn's one-shot Astra agent, the strongest published reference in our comparison. On eight manipulation tasks challenging direct Astra, PhysEvo achieves 55.00% success, compared with 1.25% for the direct-Astra reference. Deploying the simulation-evolved harness on AgileX PiPER and continuing skill revision yields 90.60/100 average score and 84.00% success across 25 trials on five real-world tasks. PhysEvo turns the consequences of action into persistent, testable changes to how a frozen model acts and improves.

</details>


### [104] [HULK: Learning Whole-Body Forceful Loco-Manipulation for Humanoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08970)
*An Dang,Arturo Flores Alvarez,Yu-Ming Chen,Conor Mc Gartoll,Helen Sun,Aaron Ames,Nima Fazeli,Manikantan Nambi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HULK 是一种面向人形机器人全身强力 loco-manipulation 的 MPC 引导强化学习与教师蒸馏框架；通过腕力教师和负重运动教师，并结合 capture-point 控制障碍函数，提升重载下的平衡、速度跟踪与抗扰能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 搬运大型重物需要全身强力交互，但负载会改变人形机器人质心并给上半身施加持续载荷，严重挑战平衡与指令跟踪。

Method: 使用 MPC 引导强化学习，并利用负载动力学预测；训练两个教师：一个在腕部受力下跟踪手臂运动，另一个将大物体抵靠身体进行移动。训练中用 capture-point 控制障碍函数增强腕力教师以改善负载下平衡，最终将两个教师蒸馏为单一策略。

Result: 在仿真和 Unitree G1 上评估。仿真中，带障碍函数的教师在每臂 10 kg 时取得最低前向和侧向速度跟踪误差，并将聚合 DCM 偏移幅度较仅 MPC 引导强化学习降低 35.7%。腕力教师可承受高达 130 N 的躯干推力扰动。

Conclusion: HULK 通过 MPC 引导强化学习、CBF 增强与教师蒸馏，实现了人形机器人全身强力负载移动操作，并提升了重载下的平衡、速度跟踪和抗扰能力。

Abstract: Humanoid loco-manipulation of large, heavy objects demands forceful interaction across the entire body. However, such payloads shift a humanoid's center of mass and impose sustained loads across the upper body, challenging balance and command tracking. We present HULK, a whole-body control framework for forceful loco-manipulation. Using model predictive control (MPC) to guide reinforcement learning with predictions of the loaded dynamics, we train two teachers: one tracks arm motions under wrist forces, and the other locomotes while holding large objects against the body. A capture-point control barrier function augments the wrist-force teacher during training to improve balance under load. We distill both teachers into a single policy. Evaluation spans simulation and the Unitree G1. In simulation, the teacher with the barrier function achieves the lowest forward and lateral velocity tracking errors at 10 kg per arm among evaluated controllers and reduces aggregate divergent component of motion (DCM) excursion magnitude by 35.7% relative to MPC-guided reinforcement learning alone. Our wrist-force teacher withstands torso push disturbances of up to 130 N.

</details>


### [105] [Toward Evidence-Driven Human-Agent-Robot Teaming for Earth-Independent Anomaly Triage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08933)
*Ignacio G Lopez-Francos,Alexis Gallagher,Samira Shalal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种证据驱动的人-智能体-机器人协同架构，用于地外独立异常分诊，并通过硬件在环原型验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深空乘组无法依赖实时地面支持处理紧急异常；初始警报往往不足以确定原因，而关键证据可能存在于乘组观察中，或位于乘组无法安全、低成本、可及地检查的位置。

Method: 将智能体AI视为对受限、可检查服务的有状态协调器，而非完全自主的载具控制器。分诊状态管理器维护假设、证据来源、不确定性、操作上下文与工具状态；面向乘组的具身智能体引导观察并解释评估变化；移动机器人获取有针对性的局部证据。类型化接口分离对话与编排同监控、机器人指令、上下文检索及安全关键控制。以两个场景说明：基于国际空间站氨气误报的载人深空任务（疑似污染限制乘组进入）与载人月球基地电源接口异常（机器人检查区分局部连接器故障与远程遥测中其他歧义原因）。

Result: 在硬件在环集成原型中实现并演练，使用Reachy Mini与Innate MARS移动机器人。

Conclusion: 主要贡献为一种权限受限的闭环证据回路架构。

Abstract: Deep-space crews cannot rely on real-time ground support for urgent off-nominal events. Initial alerts may underdetermine cause, while discriminating evidence may reside in crew observations or at locations that are unsafe, costly, or unavailable for crew inspection. We present an evidence-driven architecture for human-agent-robot teaming in Earth-independent anomaly triage. Agentic AI is treated as a stateful coordinator over bounded, inspectable services rather than as a fully autonomous vehicle controller. A triage state manager maintains hypotheses, evidence provenance, uncertainty, operational context, and tool status; a crew-facing embodied agent elicits observations and explains assessment changes; and a mobile robot acquires targeted, localized evidence. Typed interfaces separate dialogue and orchestration from monitoring, robot command, context retrieval, and safety-critical control. Two scenarios illustrate the architecture: a crewed deep-space mission based on an actual ISS ammonia false alarm, where suspected contamination restricts crew access, and a power-interface anomaly at a crewed lunar base, where robotic inspection distinguishes a local connector fault from other causes ambiguous in remote telemetry. Our main contribution is an authority-bounded closed evidence-loop architecture, exercised in a hardware-in-the-loop integration prototype using Reachy Mini and an Innate MARS mobile robot.

</details>


### [106] [From Wearable Interfaces to Dexterous Policies: Contact Shifts and Tactile Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08870)
*Ruitong Tian,Fang Xu,Noah B. Wilson,Xianyao Li,Eric Jing Du*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究比较两种DexUMI家族外骨骼穿戴接口：机器人指令、映射流程和触觉模块类型相同，仅手部几何不同。修订接口降低体力需求、改善设备评价、在基线被机械阻挡的精准抓取中实现触觉访问，并改变记录到的接触；用修订接口示范训练的策略在拧盖和开鸡蛋盒任务上成功率更高，且空间+力输入优于二值聚合输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴接口允许操作者通过自身手部动作直接与任务物体交互来收集灵巧示范，减少对目标机器人的依赖，但也使采集硬件成为生成示范的物理过程的一部分。接口几何可能影响任务执行方式和可记录的触觉观测，因此需要评估采集硬件对数据生成及下游策略学习的影响，而不仅是操作者体验。

Method: 比较两种DexUMI家族外骨骼：共享相同机器人命令定义、映射流程和触觉模块类型，仅手侧几何不同。收集示范并分析操作者体力需求、设备评分、触觉可及性及记录接触变化；训练匹配策略，比较二值聚合输入与空间+力输入，并在拧盖、开鸡蛋盒、USB插入和焊具拾放任务上评估修订接口与基线接口示范训练的策略成功率。

Result: 修订接口降低报告体力需求，改善部分设备评分，在基线因机械阻挡无法触觉感知的精准抓取中实现触觉访问，并引起任务相关接触变化：拧盖主要改变接触位置，开鸡蛋盒主要改变接触发生。拧盖和开鸡蛋盒任务上，修订接口示范训练的策略成功率高于基线，合并接口效应显著；在这四个任务中，空间+力输入均优于二值聚合。

Conclusion: 可穿戴采集硬件是机器人学习数据生成过程的一部分。评估此类接口不应只看操作者体验，还应考察其使哪些触觉交互可被记录，以及其示范能支持何种策略性能。

Abstract: Unlike conventional teleoperation, wearable interfaces allow operators to collect dexterous demonstrations through their own hand motions while directly interacting with task objects. This direct interaction reduces dependence on the target robot during collection, but it also makes the collection hardware part of the physical process that generates each demonstration. Interface geometry can influence both how a task is performed and what tactile observations are recorded for learning. We study two versions of a DexUMI-family exoskeleton that share the same robot command definition, mapping procedure, and tactile module type but differ in hand-side geometry. The revised interface reduces reported physical demand, improves selected device ratings, enables tactile access in a precision grasp that is mechanically blocked by the baseline, and produces task-dependent changes in recorded contact. Lid twisting primarily exhibits a change in contact location, whereas egg carton opening primarily exhibits a change in contact occurrence. We then train matched policies using either a binary aggregate input or a spatial-plus-force input. On lid twisting and egg carton opening, policies trained on revised-interface demonstrations achieve higher success than those trained on baseline demonstrations, with a significant pooled interface effect. Across these and two additional tasks, USB insertion and soldering tool pick and place, the spatial-plus-force input likewise outperforms binary aggregation. These results show that wearable collection hardware is part of the data-generation process for robot learning. Such interfaces should therefore be evaluated not only through operator experience, but also through the tactile interactions they make recordable and the policy performance their demonstrations support.

</details>


### [107] [Trajectory Planning without Trajectory Data: A Manifold-Guided Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08863)
*Silong Yong,Anji Liu,Cunxi Dai,Carl Busart,Guanya Shi,Yilun Du,Katia Sycara,Yaqi Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Ariadne：仅用状态观测学习状态空间流形，并利用流形几何进行轨迹规划，无需专家轨迹监督，可泛化到未见起终点约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹规划方法依赖大规模专家轨迹训练生成模型，监督成本高、难以扩展到长序列，并且对未见约束（如新起终点对）泛化较差。

Method: 学习状态空间的内在流形，在推理时利用该流形的几何结构构造满足任务目标约束的可行轨迹；训练仅需状态观测，无需轨迹数据。

Result: 在Maze2D和机器人运动规划基准上，Ariadne能从纯状态监督生成可行路径，并泛化到未见的起终点组合；在高维双臂规划任务中，与轨迹监督方法和经典规划器保持竞争力，同时训练不使用轨迹数据。

Conclusion: 以状态空间流形几何替代生成式轨迹建模，可显著降低监督需求并提升对未见约束的泛化能力，是轨迹规划的一种有效替代方案。

Abstract: A common way for trajectory planning is to leverage generative models trained on large collections of expert trajectories. At inference time, the model generates executable trajectories by conditioning on task goal constraints. However, trajectory-based methods rely on costly supervision, scale poorly with sequence length, and often generalize poorly to unseen constraints such as novel start-goal pairs. We propose an alternative to learn the underlying state-space manifold and use the geometry of the manifold for trajectory planning. This approach requires only state observations and enables generalization to unseen constraints by con- structing trajectories on the learned manifold of the state space. Experiments on Maze2D and robotic motion-planning benchmarks show that Ariadne constructs feasible paths from state-only supervision and generalizes to unseen start-goal combinations. On high-dimensional dual-arm planning, it remains competitive with trajectory-supervised and classical planners, while requiring no trajectory data for training.

</details>


### [108] [CIRRA: Dual-Level Continual Instruction Reconciliation with Ongoing Execution for Embodied Robot Agents in Interactive Household Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08862)
*Ci Zhang,Enfu Nan,Arman Akbari,Lin Zhao,Li Wang,Chen Wang,Weiwei Chen,Yanzhi Wang,Geng Yuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出持续指令协调问题及 CIRRA 框架，将 LLM 语义推理与规则约束的结构化整合结合，使家庭机器人在执行任务中接收新指令时无需重生成或大改剩余计划。作者还发布 CHIRP 基准，并在文本基准与 Unitree G1 人形机器人上验证 CIRRA 优于现有重规划基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人在执行持续任务时遇到新用户指令，通常会重新生成或大幅修改剩余任务序列，导致计划模糊、逻辑不一致和冗余执行。因此，需要一种能在保持当前执行结构的同时，持续协调并融合新指令的方法。

Method: 论文形式化持续指令协调问题，并提出 CIRRA：一个结合 LLM 语义推理与规则约束结构整合的双层框架。CIRRA 首先将新指令 grounding 到唯一可执行技能，并解析欠指定的动作与执行位置；随后保留正在进行的子任务序列作为执行 backbone，通过把新子任务插入位置匹配的片段来生成整合候选。语义推理器只评估被修改片段，识别依赖与冲突，并选择逻辑最一致的局部整合。该结构保持过程可复用共享子任务，减少冗余执行。作者还构建 CHIRP 文本基准，包含 8 个家庭环境、6 类日常活动、120 个 episode。

Result: 在 CHIRP 上，CIRRA 达到 74.2% 决策一致率，超过最强重规划基线 30 个百分点；每个正确的融合决策都能生成位置正确且无冲突的调度。在 Unitree G1 人形机器人上，CIRRA 在所有试验中都能在正确时刻打断正在进行的技能，并在所有指标上显著优于所有基线。

Conclusion: CIRRA 通过语义推理与结构约束整合，有效实现持续指令协调，保持与当前执行的一致性，缓解计划模糊、逻辑不一致和冗余执行问题，并在文本基准与真实人形机器人上得到验证。

Abstract: Household robots must accommodate new user instructions while executing ongoing tasks. Existing agents often regenerate or extensively revise the remaining task sequence, introducing plan ambiguity, logical inconsistency, and redundant execution. We formulate continual instruction reconciliation and propose CIRRA (Continual Instruction Reconciliation for Robot Agents), a dual-level framework combining LLM-based semantic reasoning with rule-constrained structural integration. CIRRA first grounds incoming instructions to unique executable skills and resolves underspecified actions and execution locations. It then preserves the ongoing subtask sequence as an execution backbone and generates integration candidates by inserting incoming subtasks into location-matched segments. The semantic reasoner evaluates only modified segments to identify dependencies and conflicts and select the most logically coherent local integration. This structure-preserving process maintains alignment with ongoing execution, mitigates ambiguity and inconsistency, and reuses shared subtasks to reduce redundant execution. We also introduce CHIRP (Continual Household Instruction Reconciliation and Planning), a text-based benchmark of 120 episodes across eight household environments and six categories of everyday activities. On CHIRP, CIRRA achieves 74.2% decision agreement, exceeding the strongest replanning baseline by 30 percentage points; every correct fusion decision yields a correctly placed, conflict-free schedule. On a Unitree G1 humanoid, CIRRA interrupts ongoing skills at the correct moment in every trial and significantly outperforms all baselines on every metric.

</details>


### [109] [Autonomous Droplet Navigation via Model-Based Reinforcement Learning: Zero-Shot Transfer and Emergent Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08852)
*Rajneesh Anand,Mayuresh V. Kothare*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 首个基于模型强化学习的机器人平台，可在开放无约束表面上闭环自主导航液滴；通过双轴倾斜板与硅油膜驱动、顶置相机实时反馈，无需仿真或解析模型，50–150个真实回合即可训练完成，且全流程少于90分钟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自驱动实验室（SDL）正推动化学与材料发现自动化，但软、可变形物质的物理操作仍超出当前机器人平台能力。开放表面液滴自主运输面临接触角滞后、毛细钉扎和表面非均匀性，导致部分可观测动力学，传统基于模型的控制器难以应对。

Method: 搭建双轴倾斜板并涂覆薄硅油膜以驱动液滴，使用顶置相机提供实时反馈。采用基于模型的强化学习训练控制策略，仅依赖50–150个物理回合，具体取决于几何复杂度，不使用仿真或解析模型。

Result: 策略可零样本鲁棒迁移到未见几何；在液滴粘滞时自主发现振荡去钉扎策略；完整训练流程可在90分钟内完成。除性能外，平台还展示了上述三项对SDL社区有价值的能力。

Conclusion: 该工作实现了开放表面上液滴的闭环自主导航，将强化学习操作从刚性微机器人扩展到可变形软物质系统，为下一代自驱动实验室提供了自动化软物质操作基础设施。

Abstract: Self-driving laboratories (SDLs) are transforming chemical and materials discovery through closed-loop automation, yet automated infrastructure for physical manipulation of soft, deformable matter remains beyond current robotic platforms. A critical instance is autonomous droplet transport on an open surface, where contact-angle hysteresis, capillary pinning, and surface heterogeneity produce partially observable dynamics that pose significant challenges for classical model-based controllers. We introduce the first robotic platform for closed-loop autonomous liquid droplet navigation on an open, unconfined surface using model-based reinforcement learning. A two-axis tilting board coated with a thin silicone oil film drives the droplet, while an overhead camera provides real-time feedback. A learned policy was trained on just 50 to 150 physical episodes depending on geometric complexity, without simulation or analytical models. Beyond performance alone, the platform demonstrates three capabilities of interest to the SDL community: it robustly transfers zero-shot to unseen geometries; it autonomously discovers an oscillatory depinning strategy to free the droplet when it sticks; and it completes its full training pipeline in under 90 minutes. These results extend reinforcement-learning manipulation from rigid microrobots to deformable soft-matter systems for next-generation SDLs.

</details>


### [110] [Taming an End-to-End Autonomous Driving Policy for Urban Navigation of Quadruped Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08812)
*Joochan Kim,Chanuk Yang,Tackgeun You,Ziran Wang,Hwasup Lim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Go2-DrivoR，将端到端自动驾驶轨迹规划框架 DrivoR 适配为面向四足机器人城市导航的目标条件局部规划方法，并实现仿真训练到真实开环轨迹预测的零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将自动驾驶规划框架迁移至四足机器人城市导航时，需要支持基于局部子目标的短时域规划，并适配人行道场景，同时避免重新设计核心解码器。

Method: 通过 goal token 以局部坐标系子目标为条件生成轨迹，并调整车辆中心评分公式；重定义可行驶区域合规项以面向人行道导航，并将原始自车进度项重构为目标条件自车进度。

Result: 仅在 TartanGround 仿真数据上训练，Go2-DrivoR 在未见仿真环境中提升了 waypoint 条件规划性能，并能零样本迁移到真实世界开环轨迹预测。

Conclusion: 目标条件适配可有效将 DrivoR 扩展至四足机器人短时域局部规划，并展现出仿真到现实的迁移潜力。

Abstract: We present Go2-DrivoR, a goal-conditioned adaptation of the end-to-end autonomous driving trajectory planning framework DrivoR for urban navigation with quadrupedal robots. By conditioning trajectory generation on a local-frame subgoal through a goal token and adapting the vehicle-centric scoring formulation, the method extends DrivoR to short-horizon goal-conditioned local planning without redesigning its core decoders. Specifically, we redefine drivable-area compliance for sidewalk-oriented navigation and reformulate the original ego progress term as goal-conditioned ego progress. Trained exclusively on TartanGround simulation data, Go2-DrivoR improves waypoint-conditioned planning performance on unseen simulation environments and transfers zero-shot to open-loop real-world trajectory prediction.

</details>


### [111] [Context-Aware Adaptive Pesticide Spraying for Agricultural Robots under Changing Weather and Terrain Using Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08807)
*Cong-Thanh Vu,Yen-Chen Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的上下文感知自适应喷洒框架，融合作物类型、农药类型和天气数据，并结合MPPI轨迹跟踪控制实现精准导航与喷洒；作物行检测精度至少提升30%，且能灵活调整喷洒量与行进速度，减少农药飘移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精准农药喷洒对提升施药效率和保证化学药剂均匀分布至关重要，但喷洒性能受温度、风速等环境条件、农药类型以及机器人感知作物与目标位置能力等多因素影响。现有方法多侧重作物检测并依赖预设喷洒参数，未能像人工操作员那样根据环境、区域作物特征和农药类型动态调整喷洒策略。

Method: 构建基于VLM的上下文感知自适应喷洒框架，使机器人利用空间推理并整合作物类型、农药类型和天气数据等多源信息，做出自适应且优化的喷洒决策；随后采用基于模型预测路径积分（MPPI）的轨迹跟踪控制器，确保精确导航并在作物位置准确喷洒。

Result: 对比结果表明，所提方法在作物行检测中精度至少提升30%；在两个环境中的实验评估进一步表明，机器人能够灵活调整喷洒量和行进速度，同时减少农药飘移。

Conclusion: 该研究验证了VLM驱动的上下文感知决策与MPPI轨迹控制相结合在精准喷洒中的有效性，可提升作物行检测精度和喷洒自适应性，并降低农药飘移。

Abstract: Precision pesticide spraying is essential for optimizing application efficiency and ensuring uniform chemical distribution. Spraying performance is influenced by multiple factors, including environmental conditions such as temperature and wind speed, pesticide type, and the robot's capability to accurately perceive crops and target spray locations. Existing approaches predominantly emphasize crop detection and rely on predefined spraying parameters, whereas human operators dynamically adjust their spraying strategies by considering environmental conditions, region-specific crop characteristics, and the type of pesticide being applied. In this study, we propose a context-aware adaptive spraying framework based on Vision-Language Models (VLMs), which enables robots to leverage spatial reasoning and integrate information from multiple sources, including crop type, pesticide type, and weather data, to make adaptive and optimized spraying decisions. Subsequently, a trajectory-tracking controller based on Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control is employed to ensure precise navigation and accurate spraying at crop locations. The comparative results demonstrate that the proposed method improves accuracy by at least 30% in detecting crop rows. In addition, the experimental evaluations conducted in two environments further demonstrate the robot's ability to flexibly adjust spraying volume and travel speed, while reducing pesticide drift.

</details>


### [112] [SAFE: Unified Slip and Fracture Detection with Low-Cost Acoustic Sensing in Robotic Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08802)
*Zerun Wang,Vivek Kamat,Shekhar Bhansali*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SAFE 是一种低成本、无需视觉和先验材料知识的实时滑动与断裂检测与自适应抓取控制方法，利用两个 PVDF 声学传感器和电机本体感知实现脆弱物体抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人抓取脆弱物体时，需要实时理解抓取状态（如滑动或断裂）并据此调整动作，尤其在材料属性未知时更具挑战；现有方法常依赖视觉或物体特定标定，难以泛化。

Method: 在柔性 Fin Ray 夹爪上安装两个被动 PVDF 声学传感器，结合电机本体感知提取 79 维特征；训练统一的 HistGradientBoosting 分类器，将抓取状态判定为正常、滑动或断裂；在该检测层上构建自适应抓取控制器，运行于 Jetson Orin Nano（104 Hz）。

Result: 留一抓取交叉验证下 Alert-F1 达 0.884，滑动与断裂混淆接近零，消融实验表明声学传感不可或缺；46 次闭环机器人试验中总体成功率为 91.3%，而固定力策略在条件不匹配时成功率降至 0%；对训练中未见的新物体成功率为 82.4%。

Conclusion: SAFE 证明了无需物体特定标定、不依赖视觉和先验材料知识，也能实现鲁棒且具备故障感知能力的机器人抓取控制，适用于脆弱物体操作。

Abstract: Manipulating fragile objects remains challenging as robots must understand the state of what they grasp, such as slip or fracture, to respond appropriately, especially when material properties are unknown. In this paper, we present SAFE: a low-cost, general-purpose sensing approach that detects both slip and fracture in real time using two passive polyvinylidene fluoride (PVDF) acoustic sensors and motor proprioception, without relying on vision or prior material knowledge. The sensors are mounted on a compliant Fin Ray gripper, and a unified HistGradientBoosting classifier reports the state (normal, slip, or fracture) from a 79-dimensional feature vector. Under leave-one-grasp-out cross-validation, SAFE achieves an Alert-F1 of 0.884 with near-zero slip-fracture confusion, and ablations confirm that acoustic sensing is indispensable. An adaptive grasp controller built on this detection layer runs at 104 Hz on a Jetson Orin Nano, achieving 91.3% success across 46 closed-loop robot trials spanning diverse object categories, while each fixed-force strategy drops to 0% on object conditions that mismatch its preset. It further reaches 82.4% success on novel objects unseen during training, demonstrating robust, failure-aware grasp control without object-specific calibration.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [113] [Q-Learning with Scalar Adjoint Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10437)
*Yonghoon Dong,Minsung Yoon,Jaehyuk Kim,Jungwoo Park,Changyeon Kim,Jinwoo Shin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 SQAM，通过发现预训练流策略的 batch 平均速度雅可比近似对角化，推导出闭式标量伴随，用最终动作的值梯度按流时间缩放，从而消除逐步向量-雅可比积，并结合策略生成动作上的值惩罚，实现高效 off-policy RL 微调流策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流策略能表达丰富动作分布，但用 off-policy RL 微调以超越演示仍具挑战：策略通过多步流生成动作，反向传播价值信息到各流步需要每步的向量-雅可比积，计算代价随流步数和策略规模增长。

Method: 观察到预训练流策略的 batch 平均速度雅可比集中于对角线，据此推导闭式标量伴随：将最终动作的值梯度按流时间缩放，避免逐步向量-雅可比积；同时发现标量伴随下控制 critic 在策略生成动作处的值尤为重要，故提出 Q-learning with Scalar Adjoint Matching (SQAM)，将标量伴随与这些动作上的值惩罚结合。

Result: 在 OGBench 上，SQAM 的增益集中在四个最难域，成功率分别比各域最强基线高 18 至 35 个百分点。为验证扩展到大型预训练策略，还在真实双臂机器人上微调视觉-语言-动作策略，SQAM 在三个任务上均优于监督微调。

Conclusion: SQAM 通过标量伴随匹配显著降低流策略 off-policy RL 微调的计算瓶颈，并在困难基准和真实机器人任务上取得优于强基线和监督微调的表现，表明其可用于高效微调流策略及大型 VLA 策略。

Abstract: Flow policies capture rich and diverse action distributions, and fine-tuning them with off-policy RL to improve beyond the demonstrations has drawn growing interest. However, fine-tuning a flow policy against a learned value function is not trivial, because the policy generates its action over many flow steps. Adjoint matching offers a principled way to update the flow model itself by propagating value information from the final action back to each flow step, but it requires a vector--Jacobian product through the policy at every step, a cost that grows with the number of flow steps and the policy size. We observe that the batch-averaged velocity Jacobian of pretrained flow policies concentrates on its diagonal. Motivated by this finding, we derive a closed-form scalar adjoint that scales the value gradient at the final action by the flow time, eliminating the per-step vector--Jacobian products. We further find that controlling the critic's value at policy-generated actions is particularly important under the scalar adjoint. Based on these findings, we propose Q-learning with Scalar Adjoint Matching (SQAM), which combines the scalar adjoint with a value penalty at those actions. SQAM's gains concentrate on the four hardest OGBench domains, where its success rate exceeds that of the strongest baseline in each domain by 18 to 35 percentage points. To test whether SQAM extends to large pretrained policies, we also fine-tune a vision-language-action policy on a real bimanual robot. SQAM improves over supervised fine-tuning on all three tasks.

</details>


### [114] [Temporally Interpretable Differentiable Decision Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10367)
*Eisuke Hirota,Aarav Sane,Rohan Paleja*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该工作提出“时间可解释性”，通过动作分块与可微决策树（DDT）提升序列决策的可解释性，并表明热启动的动作分块DDT能在多数环境中以更少参数达到神经网络策略的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可解释性有助于实现安全自主，但现有可微决策树（DDT）存在单时间步树行为与人类多时间步规划不匹配的问题，因此不适合序列决策任务。

Method: 引入时间作为可解释性的新维度，即时间可解释性，并利用动作分块实现时间抽象；提出两种结合动作分块的政策梯度算法；同时提出信息论树重构算法，在训练过程中修改树以保持参数高效。

Result: 在四个仿真环境中，从蒸馏后的动作分块策略热启动动作分块DDT最有效；其在三个领域中匹配神经网络策略，且参数最多减少80%。

Conclusion: 动作分块与热启动结合可生成时间可解释的DDT，在保持性能的同时显著降低参数量，相关代码已公开。

Abstract: Interpretability offers a solution to safe autonomy by providing transparency into an agent's underlying decision-making model. Within sequential-decision making tasks, differentiable decision trees (DDTs) are one approach to such interpretability, maintaining automatic-differentiable policies while providing humans with a discrete tree-based visualization. Nonetheless, current implementations of DDTs are not well-suited for sequential-decision making domains, as there exists an inherent mismatch between a tree's single-timestep behavior and a human's multi-timestep planning. Our work thus introduces time as a new dimension of interpretability, coined as temporal interpretability, and demonstrates how temporal abstractions via action chunking improve it. We achieve this by first introducing two novel policy gradient algorithms that incorporate action chunking. Additionally, to maintain parameter-efficient trees, we develop an information-theoretic tree restructuring algorithm that modifies the tree during training. Across four simulation environments, we find that warm-starting action chunked DDTs from a distilled action chunked policy is the most effective way to obtain temporally interpretable trees: they match neural network policies in three of the four domains while using up to 80$\%$ fewer parameters. Our code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [115] [Multi-Agent Coordination via Support-Preserving Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10087)
*Sangmin Lee,Youngju Na,Chanmi Lee,Sung-eui Yoon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出离线MARL中基于流的生成式教师因独立配对噪声与回放目标，导致邻近噪声被路由到冲突协调模式，教师生成模式间伪影并经蒸馏传播给分散执行者；提出MoSDOT，通过模式支撑半离散最优传输在教师训练前将每个噪声样本分配至单一模式，并研究共享随机性变体以暴露严格乘积执行的残余差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在CTDE范式下，离线MARL常用生成策略建模多模态联合行为，并将集中式教师蒸馏为分散式一步执行者。然而教师训练阶段存在失败模式：标准流教师独立地将噪声与回放目标配对，使邻近噪声样本可能指向相互冲突的协调模式，教师因此产生位于有效模式之间的样本；由于蒸馏损失将各局部执行者回归到教师输出在局部输入下的条件均值，该误差不会被吸收，而会传播给学生，损害端点质量与路由一致性。

Method: 提出Mode-Support Semi-Discrete Optimal Transport（MoSDOT）。该方法将多模态回放总结为具有预设容量的有限模式支撑，并利用条件半离散最优传输，在教师训练前把每个噪声样本分配到唯一模式，从而消除教师侧的模式间伪影。此外研究共享随机性变体：执行时使用共享噪声分量，以暴露严格乘积执行所固有的残余差距。

Result: 在受控诊断实验和离线MARL基准上，MoSDOT改善了端点质量与路由一致性，尤其在呈现多模态联合行为的数据集上表现更明显。

Conclusion: 结果表明，教师训练阶段的噪声-模式路由是离线MARL生成式策略的关键问题；MoSDOT能有效缓解该失败模式，而共享随机性变体有助于分析严格乘积执行中的残余差距。

Abstract: Offline MARL increasingly relies on generative policies to model multimodal joint behavior, typically by distilling a centralized teacher into decentralized one-step actors under the CTDE. We identify a failure mode at the teacher training stage: standard flow-based teachers pair noise with replay targets independently, so nearby noise samples can be routed toward conflicting coordination modes. The teacher then produces samples between valid modes, and because the distillation loss regresses each local actor onto the conditional mean of the teacher's output given local input, this error is not absorbed but propagated to the student. To remove this teacher-side artifact, we propose Mode-Support Semi-Discrete Optimal Transport (MoSDOT), which summarizes multimodal replay into a finite mode support with prescribed capacities and uses conditional semi-discrete optimal transport to assign each noise sample to a single mode before teacher training. We additionally study a shared-randomness variant that uses a shared noise component at execution to expose the residual gap intrinsic to strict-product execution. On controlled diagnostics and offline MARL benchmarks, MoSDOT improves endpoint quality and routing consistency, particularly on datasets exhibiting multimodal joint behavior.

</details>


### [116] [Beyond Policy Support: Interaction Constrained Offline Reinforcement Learning for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09763)
*Mahmoud Selim,Cristina Cipriani,Karl Henrik Johansson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出交互分布偏移（IDS）概念与离线强化学习框架ICDP，通过对比密度比估计约束自车与周围智能体未来的联合支持，抑制高价值但交互不可支持轨迹，提升交互关键场景下的驾驶策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习无需在线探索即可从固定数据中改进策略，适合自动驾驶等安全关键领域。但其核心挑战是分布偏移：策略优化可能偏向离线数据支持不足的动作，导致价值估计不可靠。现有方法主要在策略自身动作空间控制偏移；在自动驾驶等交互环境中，这不够，因为候选自车轨迹可能在边际行为分布下支持良好，但与日志交互中周围智能体行为联合支持很差。作者将这种交互支持退化称为交互分布偏移（IDS）。

Method: 提出交互约束驾驶策略（ICDP），显式控制交互级分布偏移。方法从自车与周围智能体未来的联合数据分布出发，证明联合支持退化可精确分解为自车支持分量与残差交互支持分量；再通过对比密度比估计恢复残差交互支持分量，从而隔离交互兼容性。该过程无需显式联合密度建模、周围智能体预测，也无需在策略优化中进行反应式模拟器或学习世界模型中的rollout。

Result: 在nuPlan、InterPlan以及真实卡车实验上的闭环评估表明，ICDP能抑制高价值但交互不可支持的轨迹选择，并在交互关键驾驶场景中提升性能。

Conclusion: ICDP通过显式约束交互级分布偏移，为交互环境中的离线强化学习提供了有效方案，尤其适用于自动驾驶等安全关键且强交互场景。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning enables reward-driven policy improvement from fixed datasets without requiring online exploration, making it particularly attractive in safety-critical domains. A central challenge, however, is distribution shift: policy optimization may favor actions that are weakly supported by the offline data, rendering value estimates unreliable. Existing approaches primarily control this shift in the policy's own action space. In interactive environments such as autonomous driving, this can be insufficient: a candidate ego trajectory may remain well supported under the marginal behavior distribution while being poorly supported jointly with the surrounding-agent behavior observed in the logged interaction. We refer to this degradation in interaction support as \emph{interaction distribution shift} (IDS), and introduce \emph{Interaction-Constrained Drive Policy} (ICDP), an offline reinforcement learning framework that explicitly controls interaction-level distribution shift. Starting from the joint data distribution over ego and surrounding-agent futures, we show that joint-support degradation decomposes exactly into an ego-support component and a residual interaction-support component. We recover the latter through contrastive density-ratio estimation, isolating interaction compatibility without explicit joint-density modeling, surrounding-agent prediction, or rollouts in reactive simulators or learned world models during policy optimization. Closed-loop evaluations on nuPlan, Interplan and real-world truck experiments show that ICDP suppresses high-value yet interaction-unsupported trajectory selections and improves performance in interaction-critical driving scenarios. Project webpage: this https URL

</details>


### [117] [DSReg: Provably Recovering Individual World Latents without Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09457)
*Yujia Zheng,David Klindt,Randall Balestriero,Bernhard Schölkopf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出“结构多样性”条件与DSReg，在无需重建、解码器或标签的情况下，使JEPA等线性可辨识表示进一步恢复单个世界潜变量，且至多相差符号置换，并保持密集预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非线性ICA、字典学习和因果表示学习依赖重建、辅助监督或非高斯性等分布不对称来锚定潜变量；而JEPA等无锚方法只能将潜在状态识别到线性变换，导致单个潜变量仍相互混合。

Method: 基于LeJEPA提供的线性可辨识性，定义结构多样性：不同潜变量在观测上留下不同的依赖足迹。提出依赖稀疏正则化DSReg，可后处理任意线性可辨识表示，复用已训练checkpoint，无需重建或解码器，并证明在结构多样性下可将个体世界潜变量恢复到符号置换。

Result: 在合成机制、世界模型探针、学习视觉编码器和外部渲染器上，DSReg保持密集预测能力，并随规模提升个体潜变量恢复与下游使用；结构多样性作为依赖足迹条件严格弱于先前可辨识潜变量模型的所有结构条件；建立首个可恢复每个世界潜变量的完全可辨识JEPA。

Conclusion: 无需重建、解码器或标签即可实现个体世界潜变量的可证明恢复；DSReg为JEPA补全完全可辨识性，且条件更弱、可作为后处理兼容已有线性可辨识表示。

Abstract: Methods that recover individual latent variables of the world, from nonlinear ICA to dictionary learning and causal representation learning, anchor the latents to observations through reconstruction, auxiliary supervision, or distributional asymmetries such as non-Gaussianity. Methods without these anchors, including joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPAs), identify the latent state only up to a linear transformation, so individual latents remain mixed. We close this gap: individual world latents can be provably recovered with no reconstruction, no decoder, and no labels. The key condition is Structural Diversity: different latents leave distinct dependency footprints on observations, just as no two snowflakes are alike. Building on the linear identifiability that LeJEPA provides, we prove that under Structural Diversity, DSReg (Dependency-Sparsity Regularization) recovers individual world latents up to signed permutation, without reconstruction or a decoder. It applies post hoc to any linearly identified representation, reusing trained checkpoints at no loss over joint training, and establishes the first fully identifiable JEPA that recovers every world latent. Moreover, as a condition on dependency footprints, Structural Diversity is strictly weaker than all structural conditions of prior identifiable latent variable models. Across synthetic regimes, world model probes, learned visual encoders, and external renderers, DSReg preserves dense prediction while improving individual-latent recovery and downstream use with scales.

</details>


### [118] [Kuration SDK: Addressing the Virtual2Real Gap via Data Curation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09305)
*Nirmit Desai,Eric Song,Mayank Sengupta,Tejal Bedmutha,Siri Reddy,Sahiti Dharmavaram,Kunal Sawarkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出动作条件世界模型基准正转向动作语义与物理基础指标，但现有基准对领域/任务无关训练信号有限；在Counter-Strike数据上训练扩散世界模型后，发现FVD、LPIPS、JEDi等指标与可玩性不一致，称为Virtual2Real gap。作者提出通过整理原始游戏数据并测量多种诊断属性来提供更稳健信号，并开源Kuration SDK。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作条件世界模型基准仍不成熟，视觉相似性指标难以反映可玩性与物理合理性；对于领域和任务无关的训练，已有基准提供的监督信号有限，导致模型评估与真实表现脱节。

Method: 在Counter-Strike gameplay数据上训练并评估扩散世界模型，对比FVD、LPIPS、JEDi等指标与定性可玩性；提出数据整理策略和通用物理AI数据整理工具Kuration SDK，用于在训练前诊断数据与任务属性。

Result: 实验确认定性可玩性与FVD、LPIPS、JEDi并不对应，即存在Virtual2Real gap；Kuration SDK成功揭示了一个具体案例的根因：两个在相同地图、动作和状态分布上训练的世界模型，尽管LPIPS和FVD很接近，实际游玩表现却差异很大。

Conclusion: 在缺乏可靠基准时，整理原始游戏数据并测量多种诊断属性是弥合Virtual2Real gap的更稳健途径；Kuration SDK有助于发现特定数据集中的根因，并加速样本高效训练数据集的开发。

Abstract: Benchmarks for measuring the quality of action-conditioned world models are still evolving and shifting away from visual similarity-based metrics to action-semantic and physically-grounded metrics. However, for domain and task-agnostic action-conditioned world model training, existing benchmarks provide a limited signal. By training and evaluating diffusion world models on CounterStrike gameplay data, we confirm that qualitative playability does not correspond with metrics such as FVD, LPIPS, and JEDi. We term this the Virtual2Real gap. We posit that, in lieu of reliable benchmarks, curating raw gameplay data and measuring a variety of diagnostic properties provides a more robust signal to bridge the gap, before the training even begins. We present several curation strategies and a general-purpose kit for physical AI data curation called Kuration SDK, which is being open-sourced with this paper. The SDK was instrumental in uncovering the root cause of the virtual2real gap in a specific case: why two world models trained on identical gameplay map, action and state distribution, behaved very differently when played in spite of having very similar LPIPS and FVD scores. Thus, Kuration SDK has the potential to uncover the root causes of Virtual2Real gap in specific datasets and accelerate development of sample-efficient training datasets.

</details>


### [119] [LeCuration: A Tiny World Model as a Data Curation Multi-Tool](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09285)
*Mayank Sengupta,Nirmit Desai,Eric Song,Kunal Sawarkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 LeCuration 小型世界模型作为物理 AI 数据策展工具：以 LeWorldModel 为潜空间编码器与预测器，并加入扩散 Transformer（DiT）解码器生成游戏视觉，在 CS:GO 数据上进行定性概念验证，利用嵌入做异常检测与内容聚类，并通过自回归游戏状态预测检查动作-状态一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理 AI 常运行于有限或封闭物理世界中，对象行为受有限物理规律支配，如仓库机器人和视频游戏智能体。为更好地组织、过滤和策展物理 AI 应用数据，作者提出围绕单个数据集独特设置与物理规律的新方法。

Method: 训练 LeCuration，一个小型世界模型，作为独立下游更大模型的数据策展工具。模型以 LeWorldModel（LeWM）作为潜空间编码器和预测器，并添加扩散 Transformer（DiT）解码器，为自回归游戏 rollout 补充视觉生成。利用模型嵌入作为异常检测信号和基于内容的聚类启发式；通过自回归预测游戏状态，定性检查动作-状态一致性。

Result: 在 CS:GO 游戏数据上进行了定性概念验证：发现嵌入可用作异常检测信号和基于内容的聚类启发式，且自回归预测游戏状态可定性检查动作-状态一致性。论文尚未报告定量策展指标或下游训练结果。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了以小型世界模型进行物理 AI 数据策展的可行性，但仍是定性概念验证；关键下一步是引入定量策展指标并评估下游训练效果。

Abstract: Many applications of physical AI run within finite or closed physical worlds with a limited set of physical laws governing object behavior. Examples include robots working in a warehouse and agents moving around in a video game. In order to better organize, filter, and curate data for physical AI applications, we propose a new approach centered on the unique settings and physical laws of individual datasets. We train LeCuration, a small world model intended to serve as a data curation tool for a separate, larger downstream model. To build this model, we choose LeWorldModel (LeWM)as our latent encoder and predictor, adding a diffusion transformer (DiT) decoder to add visuals to autoregressive gameplay rollout. We find that the embeddings of this model can be used as an anomaly detection signal and as a content-based clustering heuristic, and that auto-regressively predicting the game state with this model allows us to qualitatively check for action-state consistency. This paper presents a qualitative, proof-of-concept case study on CS:GO gameplay data; we do not yet report quantitative curation metrics or downstream training results, which we identify as the key next step.

</details>


### [120] [An Informational Curse of Horizon in Goal-Conditioned Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09247)
*John L. Zhou,Yuxuan Dong,Jonathan C. Kao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现目标条件策略学习中除传统“时域诅咒”外，还存在一种信息性时域诅咒：目标重标记时域越长，策略泛化与性能越差；短时域策略的输入雅可比蒸馏可显著缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习目标到达策略的困难常被归因于时域诅咒，表现为时序差分备份中的偏差累积和优势估计噪声。作者进一步探究目标重标记时域是否会对目标条件策略学习引入额外的信息瓶颈，并影响策略泛化。

Method: 通过使用预言规划器的控制实验，将训练时采样的目标时域与测试时需要到达的目标时域解耦；对比目标条件行为克隆与强化学习目标；从动作与事后重标记目标的条件互信息角度解释现象；用策略输入雅可比衡量策略对目标信息与状态信息的敏感性；并将短时域策略的输入雅可比蒸馏到长时域策略中。

Result: 即使仅在邻近子目标序列上评估，目标条件行为克隆策略也会出现严重的、依赖训练时域的性能退化，而强化学习目标可缓解该退化；训练于长时域目标的行为克隆与强化学习策略均表现出敏感性从目标信息向状态信息偏移；动作与事后重标记目标的条件互信息随目标时域增加而下降；将短时域策略的输入雅可比蒸馏到长时域策略可带来显著性能提升，尤其在组合式操作任务中。

Conclusion: 目标重标记时域是离线数据学习通用目标条件策略时的重要考虑因素；短时域策略所包含的目标敏感信息可通过雅可比蒸馏迁移到长时域策略，从而改善泛化与性能。

Abstract: The difficulty of learning goal-reaching policies is often attributed to a "curse of horizon" that manifests as bias accumulation in temporal-difference backups and noisy advantage estimates. In this work, we identify an additional informational curse of horizon in goal-conditioned policy learning, where increasing the goal relabeling horizon can significantly reduce policy generalization and performance. Through a series of controlled experiments with oracle planners, we decouple the goal horizons sampled during training from those that the policy is asked to reach at test time. Even when evaluated only on a sequence of nearby subgoals, goal-conditioned behavioral cloning (BC) policies suffer from severe, training horizon-dependent performance degradation that is mitigated by reinforcement learning (RL) objectives. We explain this phenomenon as a horizon-dependent decrease in the conditional mutual information between actions and hindsight-relabeled goals, and find empirically that both BC and RL policies trained on longer-horizon goals exhibit a shift in sensitivity from goal to state information, as measured by the policy's input Jacobians. Motivated by this observation, we find that distilling the input Jacobians of short-horizon policies into long-horizon policies yields significant performance gains, especially in combinatorial manipulation tasks. Taken together, our results highlight goal relabeling horizon as an important consideration when learning generalist policies from offline data.

</details>


### [121] [LASER: Latent Space Adjoint Matching for Support-Constrained Entropy-Regularized Offline RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08989)
*Songyuan Zhang,Oswin So,Eric Yang Yu,Matthew Cleaveland,Peter Crowley-Dolen,Chuchu Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LASER 提出一种在流匹配行为克隆的潜在空间中进行熵正则化离线强化学习的方法，通过潜在空间伴随匹配避免时间反向传播，在 40 个 OGBench 任务上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习受限于分布外动作风险；现有方法虽在流匹配行为克隆的受限潜在空间中做 RL，但朴素优化潜在策略易导致策略坍缩为脆弱模式或利用 critic 的尖锐伪影。本文发现熵正则化对潜在空间 RL 至关重要。

Method: 提出 LASER：一种离线 RL 算法，采用潜在空间伴随匹配实现带熵正则化的潜在空间 RL，兼容表达性强的流策略，同时避免通过时间反向传播。

Result: 在 40 个具有不同数据质量的 OGBench 挑战性任务上进行全面实验，LASER 达到最先进性能；且所有任务使用固定方法特定超参数，仍优于包括任务与数据集特定调参在内的基线。

Conclusion: 熵正则化是潜在空间离线 RL 的关键；LASER 在无需逐任务调参的情况下展现出稳健适用性与优越性能。

Abstract: While offline reinforcement learning (RL) enables policy optimization from static datasets without costly online interaction, it remains bottlenecked by the risk of executing out-of-distribution (OOD) actions. Recent approaches mitigate this by learning a behavior-cloning policy through flow matching and then performing RL within its constrained latent space. However, naively optimizing the latent policy can easily cause the policy to collapse into a brittle mode or exploit sharp artifacts of the learned critic. In this work, we find that entropy regularization is essential in latent-space RL for addressing these challenges. We introduce LASER, a novel offline RL algorithm that applies latent-space adjoint matching to achieve entropy-regularized latent-space RL with expressive flow policies while avoiding backpropagation through time. Through comprehensive experiments on 40 challenging OGBench tasks with varying dataset qualities, we show that LASER achieves state-of-the-art performance. Notably, LASER uses fixed method-specific hyperparameters across all tasks and outperforms the evaluated baselines, including those with task- and dataset-specific tuning, which highlights the robust applicability of LASER. Project website: this https URL .

</details>
